------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE acertagradeeti
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE acertagradeeti
Local lcCor, lcTam, lcLoc, lcFil, lnTot
lnTot = 0
lnTot2= 0

If !Used('CrTSigPro') Or !Used('CrSigMvEst') Or !Used('CrSigOpEtq1')
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

If Used('CsEtiqueta')
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta.grd_historico.RecordSource = ''
EndIf

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.pag_etiqueta
	*********************************************************************
	* Dados Para Filtro da Grade de Etiquetas
	lcCor = .GetCor.Value
	lcTam = .getTam.Value
	lcLoc = .getLoc.Value
	lcFil = IIf(!IsEmpty(lcCor),[a.codcors = ']+lcCor+[' And ],'')+;
			IIf(!IsEmpty(lcTam),[a.codtams = ']+lcTam+[' And ],'')+;
			IIf(!IsEmpty(lcLoc),[a.localizas = ']+lcLoc+[' And ],'')+ [.T.]

	lcEstEmp = crSigMvEtr.emps
	lcEstGru = crSigMvEtr.grupos
	lcEstCta = crSigMvEtr.estos
	lcCodPro = CrTSigPro.cpros

	Select a.cpros,a.empos,a.grupos,a.contas,a.cbars,a.qtds,a.pesos,a.localizas,a.codtams,a.codcors,a.datas,a.cps1s,a.locals,a.codamss, a.CBars_old ;
  	  From CrSigOpEtq1 a ;
	 Where a.empos = lcEstEmp And ;
		   a.grupos = lcEstGru And ;
		   a.contas = lcEstCta And &lcFil. ;
	  Into Cursor CsEtiqueta ReadWrite

	Select CsEtiqueta
	Index on cBars Tag cBars
	*** Mena 12/07/2013 - estes indices foram criados para ordenar a grade de etiquetas no click das colunas correspondentes ***
	Index on qtds  Tag qtds
	Index on pesos Tag pesos
	Index on localizas Tag localizas
	Index on codtams Tag codtams
	Index on codcors Tag codcors
	Index on DTOS(datas) Tag datas

	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.pag_etiqueta.grd_historico
		.RecordSource = 'CsEtiqueta'
		.Column1.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqueta.cbars'
		.Column2.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqueta.qtds'
		.Column3.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqueta.pesos'
		.Column4.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqueta.localizas'
		.Column5.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqueta.codtams'
		.Column6.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqueta.codcors'
		.Column7.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqueta.datas'
		.Column8.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqueta.CPros'
		.Column9.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqueta.CBars_old'
		.Column8.Visible = (ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.chkEquivalentes.Value = 1)
		.Column2.Header1.Caption = 'Qtd ('+CrTSigPro.CUnis+')'
		.Column3.Header1.Caption = 'Peso'
		If Not IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.cestos) And CrTSigPro.cEstos = '3'
			.Column3.Header1.Caption = 'Qtd ('+CrTSigPro.CUniPs+')'
		EndIf
	EndWith

	.getRefs.ControlSource = [CsEtiqueta.CodAmss]

	Select CsEtiqueta
	Go Top
	Scan
		lnTot = lnTot + CsEtiqueta.qtds
		lnTot2= lnTot2 + csEtiqueta.Pesos
	EndScan
	.getTotEti.Value = lnTot
	.getTotEti2.Value = lnTot2
	.getTotEti.Refresh
	.getTotEti2.Refresh
	
	Select CsEtiqueta
	Go Top

	.Grd_Historico.Refresh
EndWith
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE acertaordemgrade
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE acertaordemgrade
Lparameters grdOrdenar as Grid, lcCmpOrd as String, plDescend

Local i as Integer, lcCampo as String, lcCursor as String, lnRecno as Integer 

If( Empty(lcCmpOrd) )
	Return
Endif

lcCursor = Left(lcCmpOrd,At([.],lcCmpOrd)-1)
lcOrdem  = Substr(lcCmpOrd,At([.],lcCmpOrd)+1)
lnRecno  = Recno(lcCursor)

For i = 1 to grdOrdenar.ColumnCount
	lcCampo = grdOrdenar.Columns(i).ControlSource	
	If( lcCampo == lcCmpOrd )
		grdOrdenar.Columns(i).Header1.BackColor = Rgb(220,255,220)
		If plDescend 
			lcOrdem = lcOrdem + [d]
		Endif
		Set Order To lcOrdem in (lcCursor)
	Else
		grdOrdenar.Columns(i).Header1.BackColor = Rgb(212,208,200)
	Endif
Next i 
grdOrdenar.Refresh()
If Between(lnRecno,1,Reccount(lcCursor))
	Go lnRecno in &lcCursor
Endif	

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE atualizacot
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE atualizacot
Parameters pdData, poPag
LOCAL loPag

loPag = ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.&poPag

*** mena - 06/10/2015 - atualiza grade de cotações ***
lcSql = [Select cmoes, 0000.000 as valos From Sigcdcot group by cmoes ]
lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpCot')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf
Select TmpCot
Go Top
SCAN
	replace valos with fBuscarCotacao(TmpCot.cmoes, pdData, ThisForm.poDataMgr)
ENDSCAN

Select TmpCot
Go Top

loPag.GrdCot.Titulo.Caption = 'Cotações na Data'
loPag.GrdCot.Grade_cot.childorder=''
loPag.GrdCot.Grade_cot.recordsource='TmpCot'
loPag.GrdCot.Grade_cot.column2.width=100
loPag.GrdCot.Grade_cot.column2.inputmask='999.999'
loPag.GrdCot.Grade_cot.column1.header1.Caption='Moe'
loPag.GrdCot.Grade_cot.column2.header1.Caption='Cotacao'
loPag.GrdCot.Grade_cot.column1.controlsource='TmpCot.Cmoes'
loPag.GrdCot.Grade_cot.column2.controlsource='TmpCot.Valos'
loPag.grdcot.refresh
***************************************************************

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE atualizacpoest
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE atualizacpoest
Local lcEdn, loPag

loPag = ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque

loPag.getOrigem.Value    = []
loPag.getDOrigem.Value   = []
loPag.getDestino.Value   = []
loPag.getDDestino.Value  = []
loPag.getVendedor.Value  = []
loPag.getDVendedor.Value = []

lcEdn = CrSigMvHst.EmpOs + CrSigMvHst.Dopes + Str(CrSigMvHst.Numes, 6)

lcQuery = [Select ContaOs, ContaDs, Vends ] + ;
		    [From SigMvCab ] + ;
		   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcEdn + [']

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalEest]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalEest)])
	Return 0
EndIf
Select LocalEest
Go Top In LocalEest
If Not Eof([LocalEest])
	If Not Empty(LocalEest.ContaOs)
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdCli], [LocalCli], [IClis], LocalEest.ContaOs, [RClis])
		If Not Used([LocalCli])
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - LocalCli 1)])
			Return 0
		EndIf
		loPag.getOrigem.Value  = LocalEest.ContaOs
		loPag.getDOrigem.Value = LocalCli.RClis
	EndIf
	
	If Not Empty(LocalEest.ContaDs)
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdCli], [LocalCli], [IClis], LocalEest.ContaDs, [RClis])
		If Not Used([LocalCli])
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - LocalCli 2)])
			Return 0
		EndIf
		loPag.getDestino.Value  = LocalEest.ContaDs
		loPag.getDDestino.Value = LocalCli.RClis
	EndIf
	
	If Not Empty(LocalEest.Vends)
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdCli], [LocalCli], [IClis], LocalEest.Vends, [RClis])
		If Not Used([LocalCli])
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - LocalCli 3)])
			Return 0
		EndIf
		loPag.getVendedor.Value  = LocalEest.Vends
		loPag.getDVendedor.Value = LocalCli.RClis
	EndIf
EndIf

loPag.getOrigem.Refresh
loPag.getDOrigem.Refresh
loPag.getDestino.Refresh
loPag.getDDestino.Refresh
loPag.getVendedor.Refresh
loPag.getDVendedor.Refresh

thisform.atualizacot(CrSigMvHst.datas, 'Estoque') 	&& atualiza grade de cotacao na guia estoque


* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE carregagrpope
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE carregagrpope
Private pTru, pNot
Local loPag, lcDgO, lcPro

loPag = ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta

Zap In crGrpOpe

pTru = .t. 
pNot = .f.

lcDgO = Padr(loPag.cntGrpOpe.getGrpOpe.Value, 20)
lcPro = crTSigPro.CPros

ThisForm.poDataMgr1.CursorQuery('SigCdGpe', 'LocalGope', 'DgOpes', lcDgO, 'OpPends')
lnPen = Iif(Used('LocalGope') And Not Eof('LocalGope'), LocalGope.OpPends, 1)
lcWhe = Iif(lnPen = 2, [b.ChkSubN = ?pNot And ], Iif(lnPen = 3, [b.ChkSubN = ?pTru And ], []))

lcQuery = [Select b.Emps, b.Dopes, b.Numes, Sum(c.Qtds - c.QtBaixas) as Qtds ] + ;
			[From SigCdGpo a, SigMvCab b, SigMvItn c ] + ;
		   [Where a.DgOpes = '] + lcDgO + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [a.Dopers = b.Dopes And ] + lcWhe + ;
		   		 [b.EmpDopNums = c.EmpDopNums And ] + ;
		   		 [c.CPros = '] + lcPro + [' ] + ;
		   [Group By b.Emps, b.Dopes, b.Numes ] + ;
		   [Order By b.Emps, b.Dopes, b.Numes]

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crDados') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Processamento - crDados)')
	Return 0
EndIf

lnQtd = 0
Select crDados
Scan
	Insert Into crGrpOpe (Emps, Dopes, Numes, Qtds) Values (crDados.Emps, crDados.Dopes, crDados.Numes, crDados.Qtds)
	lnQtd = lnQtd + crDados.Qtds
EndScan
loPag.cntGrpOpe.getTotGrpOpe.Value = lnQtd

loPag.cntGrpOpe.getGrpOpe.Refresh
loPag.cntGrpOpe.getTotGrpOpe.Refresh
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE chklimite
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE chklimite
LParameters _OldVal, _NewVal, _pValVar, _pVar

llVerLimite = .T.
lnValos   = _OldVal
If _NewVal <> 0
	lnValVars = (_NewVal - _OldVal)
	lnVars    = (((_NewVal - _OldVal) / _NewVal) * 100)
Else
	lnValVars = _pValVar
	lnVars    = _pVar
EndIf

llLimite = .F.

=ThisForm.poDataMgr1.CursorQuery('SigCdUsu','CrSigCdUsu','Usuarios',Usuar)
=Seek(CrSigCdUsu.cCargs,'CrSigCdCrg','CCargs')
If lnVars < 0
	If (CrSigCdCrg.LimDescs <> 0 Or CrSigCdCrg.DescPors <> 0)
		If (Abs(lnValVars) <= CrSigCdCrg.LimDescs Or CrSigCdCrg.LimDescs = 0) And (Abs(lnVars) <= CrSigCdCrg.DescPors Or CrSigCdCrg.DescPors = 0)
			llLimite = .T.
		EndIf
	EndIf
Else
	If lnVars > 0
		If (CrSigCdCrg.LimAcres <> 0 Or CrSigCdCrg.AcrePors <> 0)
			If (Abs(lnValVars) <= CrSigCdCrg.LimAcres Or CrSigCdCrg.LimAcres = 0 ) And (Abs(lnVars) <= crSigCdCrg.AcrePors Or CrSigCdCrg.AcrePors = 0)
				llLimite = .T.
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
Return(llLimite)
ENDPROC

lcRec = crSigPrCpo.Mats

* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mgrdestoque
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mgrdestoque
Local lcCodPro,lcFilQtd,lnEscolha,lnErro1,lcMcrsql1,lcMcrsql2,llContinua1,lnNreg1,;
		lnCbar,lcEmpBar1,lcIdChave1

lnSegIni = Datetime()
lcCodPro  = CrTSigPro.cpros
lnEscolha = ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.Optiongroup1.Option2.Value
lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.pnIdConn

ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta.grd_historico.RecordSource = ''
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.Grd_Estoque.RecordSource = ''

Create Cursor crSigOpEtq1 (CPros c(14), EmpOs c(3), Grupos c(10), Contas c(10), CBars n(14), Qtds n(10,3), Pesos n(10,3), ;
						 Localizas c(10), CodTams c(4), CodCors c(4), Datas d(8), CPs1s c(1), Locals c(10), CodAmss c(10), CBars_Old n(14))

If Not ThisForm.MontaEquivals(crTSigPro.CPros)
	Return .f.
EndIf

*lcFilQtd = Iif(lnEscolha = 1,[a.sqtds <> 0],[0 = 0])
lcFilQtd = Iif(lnEscolha = 1,[a.sqtds > 0],[0 = 0])

Select crProEtqEqs
Scan
	lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select e.cpros,e.empos,e.grupos,e.contas,e.cbars,e.qtds,e.pesos,] +;
					[e.localizas,e.codtams,e.codcors,e.datas,e.CodAmss,'1' as cps1s, IsNull(c.CBars_old,0) as CBars_old ] +;
					[ From SigOpEtq e ] +;
					[ Left join SigOpEta c on e.cbars = c.cbars_new ] + ;
					[ Where e.cpros = '] + crProEtqEqs.CPros + ['] +;
					[ And e.Empos In (Select b.cemps From SigCdEmp b Where Not b.dircolins='] + Space(32) + [')] +;
					[ And e.cpros + e.empos + e.grupos + e.contas In (Select a.cpros + a.emps + a.grupos + a.estos] +;
					[ From SigMvEst a where a.cpros='] + crProEtqEqs.CPros + [' and &lcFilQtd.) ] +;
					[ Union All ] + ;
					[Select e.cpros,e.empos,e.grupos,e.contas,e.cbars,e.qtds,e.pesos,] +;
					[e.localizas,e.codtams,e.codcors,e.datas,e.CodAmss,'0' as cps1s, IsNull(c.CBars_old,0) as CBars_old ] +;
					[ From SigOpEtq e ] +;
					[ Left join SigOpEta c on e.cbars = c.cbars_new ] + ;
					[ Where e.cpros = '] + crProEtqEqs.CPros + ['] +;
					[ And e.Empos In (Select b.cemps From SigCdEmp b Where b.dircolins='] + Space(32) + [')] +;
					[ And e.cpros + e.empos + e.grupos + e.contas In (Select a.cpros + a.emps + a.grupos + a.estos] +;
					[ From SigMvEst a where a.cpros='] + crProEtqEqs.CPros + [' and &lcFilQtd.) ] +;				
					[ Order by 1,2,3,4,5], 'CrSigOpEtq2')
	If lnQueryOk < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	If Used('crTmpChkEti1')
		USE In ('crTmpChkEti1')
	EndIf 

	Select CrSigOpEtq2
	Index On cbars Tag cbars
	Index On Str(cbars,14)+empos Tag skgroup1
	Set Order To ('') In CrSigOpEtq2

	m.lcMcrsql1 = [Select a.empos, a.cbars, IsNull(c.CBars_old,0) as CBars_old ] + ;
		[From SigOpEtq a ] + ;
		[ Left join SigOpEta c on a.cbars = c.cbars_new ] + ;
		[Where a.cpros='] + crProEtqEqs.CPros + [' Order By 1]

	If m.lnErro1>0 
		m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr2.SQLExecute(m.lcMcrSql1, 'crTmpChkEti1')
	EndIf 
	If Used('crTmpChkEti1')
		Select crTmpChkEti1
		Set Order To ('') In crTmpChkEti1
		Locate 

		Scan While m.lnErro1>0 And Not Eof('crTmpChkEti1')
			m.lnCbar = crTmpChkEti1.cbars
			m.lcEmpBar1 = crTmpChkEti1.empos
			m.lcIdChave1 = (Str(m.lnCbar,14)+m.lcEmpBar1)

			Update CrSigOpEtq2 Set cps1s='0' Where Str(cbars,14)+empos=m.lcIdChave1
		EndScan 
	EndIf
	
	Select crSigOpEtq2
	Scan
		Scatter Memvar Memo
		m.Datas  = Nvl(m.Datas, Ctot([]))
		Insert Into crSigOpEtq1 From Memvar	
	EndScan
EndScan

Zap In crSigMvEstPd
Zap In crSigMvEst

Select crProEtqEqs
Scan
	lcQuery = [Select a.CPros, a.Emps, a.Grupos, a.Estos, a.Sqtds, a.CodCors, a.CodTams, ] + ;
					 [9999999999.999 as QtdEmpHs, a.SPesos, ] + ;
					 [9999999999.999 as Pedidos, Space(20) as Fk_Chave, b.Rclis as DContas ] + ;
		   		[From SigMvEst a inner join SigCdCli b on a.Estos = b.Iclis ] + ;
		   	   [Where a.CPros = '] + crProEtqEqs.CPros + [' And &lcFilQtd ] + ;
	   		   [Order By a.Emps, a.Grupos, a.Estos, a.CodTams, a.CodCors]

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalEst]) < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (mGrdEstoque - LocalEst)')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	Select LocalEst
	Scan
		Scatter Memvar Memo
		If Seek(LocalEst.Emps + LocalEst.Grupos + LocalEst.Estos + LocalEst.CodCors + LocalEst.CodTams, [crSigMvEst], [EmpGruCon])
			Replace SQtds  With SQtds + LocalEst.SQtds, ;
					SPesos With SPesos + LocalEst.SPesos In crSigMvEst
		Else
			Insert Into crSigMvEst From Memvar
		EndIf
	
		Replace Fk_Chave With fUniqueIds(), ;
				QtdEmphs With 0, ;
				Pedidos  With 0 In crSigMvEst
	EndScan
EndScan

* Alimentando a Coluna de Entradas Pendentes
If InList(crTSigPro.TipoEstos, 2, 3, 4)
	lcQuery = [Select a.CPros, a.CodCors, a.CodTams, a.Emps, a.Dopes, a.Numes, a.Qtds, a.QtBaixas ] + ;
				[From SigMvIts a, SigCdOpe o, SigCdTom t ]
Else
	lcQuery = [Select a.CPros, '    ' as CodCors, '    ' as CodTams, a.Emps, a.Dopes, a.Numes, a.Qtds, a.QtBaixas ] + ;
				[From SigMvItn a, SigCdOpe o, SigCdTom t ]
EndIf
lcQuery = lcQuery + [Where a.CPros = '] + lcCodPro + [' And ] + ;
			   		 	  [a.Dopes = o.Dopes And ] + ;
			   		 	  [o.TipoOps = t.Codigos And ] + ;
			   		 	  [t.EntPends = 1]

lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalEstI])
If (lnQueryOk < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, [Falha na Conexão])
	ThisForm.Release
EndIf

Select LocalEstI
Scan
	lcEdn = LocalEstI.Emps + LocalEstI.Dopes + Str(LocalEstI.Numes, 6)
		
	lcQuery = [Select a.GrupoOs, a.ContaOs, b.Rclis From SigMvCab a inner join SigCdCli b on a.ContaOs = b.Iclis Where a.EmpDopNums = ']+ lcEdn +[']
	lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalEest])
	If (lnQueryOk < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, [Falha na Conexão])
		ThisForm.Release
	EndIf
	Select LocalEest
	Go Top In LocalEest

	Select crSigMvEst
	Locate For Grupos = LocalEest.GrupoOs And Estos = LocalEest.ContaOs And ;
			   Cpros = LocalEstI.Cpros And CodCors = LocalEstI.CodCors And ;
			   CodTams = LocalEstI.CodTams

	If Eof([crSigMvEst])
		Insert Into crSigMvEst (Emps, Grupos, Estos, CPros, CodCors, CodTams, Fk_Chave, DContas) ;
					  Values (LocalEstI.Emps, LocalEest.GrupoOs, LocalEest.ContaOs, ;
					  		  LocalEstI.CPros, LocalEstI.CodCors, LocalEstI.CodTams, ;
					  		  fUniqueIds(), LocalEest.Rclis)
	EndIf

	Replace Qtds 	 With Qtds 	   + LocalEstI.Qtds, ;
			QtBaixas With QtBaixas + LocalEstI.QtBaixas, ;
			EntPends With EntPends + (LocalEstI.Qtds - LocalEstI.QtBaixas) In crSigMvEst

	If ((LocalEstI.Qtds - LocalEstI.QtBaixas) > 0)
		Select crSigMvEstPd
		Go Top In crSigMvEstPd
		Locate For EmpDopNums = lcEdn
		If Eof([crSigMvEstPd])
			Insert Into crSigMvEstPd (Fk_Chave, EmpDopNums) Values (crSigMvEst.Fk_Chave, lcEdn)
		EndIf
		Replace Qtds With Qtds + (LocalEstI.Qtds - LocalEstI.QtBaixas) In crSigMvEstPd
	EndIf
EndScan

oobjG = ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.estoque
With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.estoque.grd_estoque
	.RecordSource = 'CrSigMvEst'

*!*			lnCnt = 0

	.Column9.ControlSource  = 'CrSigMvEst.Pedidos'

		.Column8.ControlSource = 'CrSigMvEst.sPesos'

		.column8.InputMask       = "99999999.999"
		.column8.text1.InputMask = "99999999.999"
*!*			.Column8.Text1.Width     = 100
*!*			.Width = 770 - lnCnt
*!*			oobjG.Shape_Estoque.Width = 776 - lnCnt
	Else
		.Column8.ControlSource = ""
		.Column8.Header1.Caption = ""
		.column8.InputMask       = ""
		.column8.text1.InputMask = ""
		.Column8.Visible = .f.
*!*			.Column8.Width     = 2
*!*			.Width = 676 - LnCnt
*!*			oobjG.Shape_Estoque.Width = 682 - lnCnt
	Endif

If !IsEmpty(CrSigCdPam.transfres) Or !IsEmpty(CrSigCdPac.MovEmpEco)

	Select CrSigMvEst
	Select Distinct Emps From CrSigMvEst Into Cursor csTmpContas ReadWrite 
	Select csTmpContas
	Go Top
	Scan 
		
		For i=1 to 2
			
			lcMovEmp = []
			If i=1
				lcMovEmp = CrSigCdPam.transfres	
			Else
				lcMovEmp = CrSigCdPac.MovEmpEco
			EndIf 
			
			Select CrSigCdOpe
			_QtRet = 0
			Locate For (CrSigCdOpe.dopes = lcMovEmp)
			If Found() And CrSigCdOpe.estoqs # 1
				lcDopes  = CrSigCdOpe.dopes
				_QtRet = 0
				lcEdnIs = csTmpContas.Emps + lcDopes + Str(0,6)
				lcEdnFs = csTmpContas.Emps  + lcDopes + Str(999999,6)
				
				If Not Used('CrEmpenhado')
					*** Tiago - 04/10/2011 - Criado cursor para armazenar as movimentações que possuem empenho - Vianna
					lcQuery = [Select cpros, codCors, codTams, emps, dopes, numes, qtds, Space(10) as ContaDs, Space(10) as GrupoDs, DtAlts as Datas From SigMvIts Where 0=1]
					If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrEmpenhado') < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão - CrEmpenhado')
						ThisForm.Release()
						Return .f.
					EndIf
				EndIf 
				
				***
				If InList(CrTSigPro.TipoEstos,2,3,4)
					lcQuery = [Select cpros, codCors, codTams, emps, dopes, numes, qtds, qtbaixas ]+;
							[From SigMvIts Where EmpDopNums Between ?lcEdnIs And ?lcEdnFs And Cpros=']+lcCodPro+[']
				Else
					lcQuery = [Select cpros, '    ' as codCors, '    ' as codTams, emps, dopes, numes, qtds, qtbaixas ]+;
							[From SigMvItn Where EmpDopNums Between ?lcEdnIs And ?lcEdnFs And Cpros=']+lcCodPro+[']
				Endif
				lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrSigMvItn')
				If lnQueryOk < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					ThisForm.Release
				EndIf 
				Select CrSigMvItn
				Go Top
				If Not Eof()
					Scan
						If Qtds - QtBaixas # 0
							lcEdn = crSigMvItn.Emps + crSigMvItn.Dopes + Str(crSigMvItn.Numes, 6)
							
							lcQuery = [Select Grupoos, ContaOs, GrupoDs, ContaDs, Datas From SigMvCab Where EmpDopNums = ']+ lcEdn +[']
							If ThisForm.PodataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcquery,'CrTprMvCab') > 0
								Select CrSigMvEst
								Do Case 
								Case CrTSigPro.TipoEstos = 2
									Locate For Grupos = crTprMvCab.GrupoOs And Estos = crTprMvCab.ContaOs And ;
										   Cpros = crSigMvItn.Cpros And CodCors = crSigMvItn.CodCors And Emps = crSigMvItn.Emps
								Case CrTSigPro.TipoEstos = 3
									Locate For Grupos = crTprMvCab.GrupoOs And Estos = crTprMvCab.ContaOs And ;
										   Cpros = crSigMvItn.Cpros And CodTams = crSigMvItn.CodTams And Emps = crSigMvItn.Emps
								Case CrTSigPro.TipoEstos = 4
									Locate For Grupos = crTprMvCab.GrupoOs And Estos = crTprMvCab.ContaOs And ;
										   Cpros = crSigMvItn.Cpros And CodCors = crSigMvItn.CodCors And ;
										   CodTams = crSigMvItn.CodTams And Emps = crSigMvItn.Emps
								OtherWise 
									Locate For Grupos = crTprMvCab.GrupoOs And Estos = crTprMvCab.ContaOs And ;
										   Cpros = crSigMvItn.Cpros And Emps = crSigMvItn.Emps
								EndCase 								
								If Not Eof()
									Replace QtdEmphs With QtdEmphs + (crSigMvItn.Qtds - crSigMvItn.QtBaixas) In crSigMvEst
									*** Tiago - 04/10/11 - Vianna - Atualiza cursor com as operações que possuem empenho
									If (crSigMvItn.Qtds - crSigMvItn.QtBaixas) > 0
										Insert into crEmpenhado(Cpros, CodCors, CodTams, Qtds, Emps, Dopes, Numes, ContaDs, GrupoDs, Datas) ;
											values (crSigMvItn.Cpros, crSigMvItn.CodCors, crSigMvItn.CodTams, (crSigMvItn.Qtds - crSigMvItn.QtBaixas), ;
											 crSigMvItn.Emps, crSigMvItn.Dopes, crSigMvItn.Numes, crTprMvCab.ContaDs, crTprMvCab.GrupoDs, crTprMvCab.Datas)
									EndIf
								Endif
							Endif
						EndIf
					EndScan
				EndIf
			EndIf
		Next 
		Select csTmpContas
	EndScan 
EndIf

lcSql = [Select * from SigCdTom Where GerGdmis = 6 ]
lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpTope')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	ThisForm.Release
EndIf 
Select CrTmpTOpe
If Reccount() > 0
	If InList(CrTSigPro.TipoEstos,2,3,4)
		lcSql = [Select a.Emps,a.Dopes,a.Numes,a.grupods,a.contads,d.cpros, sum(d.qtds-d.qtbaixas) as qtds, d.CodCors, d.CodTams, e.Rclis as Dcontas ]+;
				[from SigMvIts d, SigMvCab a, SigCdOpe b, SigCdTom c, SigCdCli e ]+;
				[Where d.cpros=']+lcCodPro+[' And a.dopes=b.dopes ]+;
				[And b.tipoops = c.codigos and c.gergdmis=6 ]+;
				[And D.empdopnums = A.empdopnums and a.chksubn = 0 ]+;
				[And e.Iclis = a.ContaDs ] + ;
				[group by a.Emps,a.Dopes,a.Numes,a.grupods,a.contads,d.cpros,d.CodCors,d.CodTams,e.Rclis]
	Else
		lcSql = [Select a.Emps,a.Dopes,a.Numes,a.grupods,a.contads,d.cpros, sum(d.qtds-d.qtbaixas) as qtds, '' as CodCors, '' as CodTams, e.Rclis as Dcontas ]+;
				[from SigMvItn d, SigMvCab a, SigCdOpe b, SigCdTom c, SigCdCli e ]+;
				[Where d.cpros=']+lcCodPro+[' And a.dopes=b.dopes ]+;
				[And b.tipoops = c.codigos and c.gergdmis=6 ]+;
				[And D.empdopnums = A.empdopnums and a.chksubn = 0 ]+;
				[And e.Iclis = a.ContaDs ] + ;
				[group by a.Emps,a.Dopes,a.Numes,a.grupods,a.contads,d.cpros,e.Rclis]
	EndIf

	lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTprMvCabix')
	If lnQueryOk < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		ThisForm.Release
	EndIf 

	Select Emps, Dopes, Numes, GrupoDs, ContaDs, CPros, Sum(Qtds) as Qtds, CodCors, CodTams, DContas ;
	  From crTprMvCabIx ;
	 Where Qtds > 0 ;
	 Group By Emps, Dopes, Numes, GrupoDs, ContaDs, CPros, CodCors, CodTams, Dcontas ;
	  Into Cursor crTprMvCabI ReadWrite

	Select crTprMvCabI
	Scan
		Select crSigMvEst
		Go Top In crSigMvEst
		Locate For Emps = crTprMvCabI.Emps And Grupos = crTprMvCabI.Grupods And Estos = crTprMvCabI.ContaDs And ;
				   Cpros = crTprMvCabI.Cpros And CodCors = crTprMvCabI.CodCors And CodTams = crTprMvCabI.CodTams
		If Eof()
			Append Blank
			Replace Emps     With crTprMvCabI.Emps,;
					Grupos   With crTprMvCabI.GrupoDs,;
					Estos    With crTprMvCabI.ContaDs,;
					CPros    With crTprMvCabI.Cpros,;
					CodCors  With crTprMvCabI.CodCors,;
					CodTams  With crTprMvCabI.CodTams,;
					Fk_Chave With fUniqueIds(),;
					DContas  With crTprMvCabI.Dcontas
		EndIf

		lcEdn = crTprMvCabI.Emps + crTprMvCabI.Dopes + Str(crTprMvCabI.Numes, 6)
		Select crSigMvEstPd
		Go Top In crSigMvEstPd
		Locate For EmpDopNums = lcEdn
		If Eof([crSigMvEstPd])
			Insert Into crSigMvEstPd (Fk_Chave, EmpDopNums) Values (crSigMvEst.Fk_Chave, lcEdn)
		EndIf
			
		Replace Pedidos With Pedidos + crTprMvCabI.Qtds In crSigMvEst
		Replace Qtds 	With Qtds 	 + crTprMvCabI.Qtds In crSigMvEstPd
	EndScan
EndIf

lnEst = 0
lnPes = 0
Select CrSigMvEst
Sum sqtds,sPesos to lnEst,lnPes
Set Order To EmpGruCon
lnOrd = CrSigCdPac.OrdCongps 
If lnOrd > 0
	lcColumn = 'Column'+Iif(LnOrd>4,Str(lnOrd-1,1),Str(lnOrd))
	
	If lnOrd = 4
		lcPict = [\VBMP\DOWN.JPG]
		ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.estoque.grd_estoque.CoLUMN4.Header1.Picture = lcPict
		ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.pag_etiqueta.grd_estoque.CoLUMN4.Header1.Picture = lcPict
	Else
		If LnOrd = 5
			lcPict = [\VBMP\UP.JPG]
			ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.estoque.grd_estoque.CoLUMN4.Header1.Picture = lcPict
			ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.pag_etiqueta.grd_estoque.CoLUMN4.Header1.Picture = lcPict
		EndIf
	Endif

	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.estoque.grd_estoque.&lcColumn..Header1.Click()
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.pag_etiqueta.grd_estoque.&lcColumn..Header1.Click()

Endif

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta
	.getTotEst.Value = lnEst
	.GettotPes.Value = lnPes
	.getTotEst.Refresh
	.GettotPes.Refresh
EndWith
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE montaequivals
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE montaequivals
Lparameters pPro

Create Cursor crProEtqEqs (CPros c(14))
Index On CPros Tag CPros

If (ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.chkEquivalentes.Value = 1)
	* Apanhando o Produto e Seus Equivalentes (Pais e Filhos)
	lcQuery = [Select CPros, CProEqs From SigCdPro Where CPros = '] + pPro + [' ] + ;
			   [Union All ] + ;
			  [Select CPros, CProEqs From SigCdPro Where CProEqs = '] + pPro + [']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalProEt]) < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalProEt)')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	
	* Apanhando os Produtos Equivalentes dos Pais e Filhos
	Select LocalProEt
	Scan
		lcPai = LocalProEt.CPros
		lcFil = Iif(Empty(LocalProEt.CProEqs), LocalProEt.CPros, LocalProEt.CProEqs)
		
		lcQuery = [Select CPros, CProEqs From SigCdPro Where CPros In ('] + lcPai + [', '] + lcFil + [') ] + ;
				   [Union All ] + ;
				  [Select CPros, CProEqs From SigCdPro Where CProEqs In ('] + lcPai + [', '] + lcFil + [') ]

		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalProEt1]) < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalProEt1)')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	
		* Inserindo os Produtos "Parentes" no Cursor Para Filtragem
		Select LocalProEt1
		Scan
			If Not Seek(LocalProEt1.CPros, [crProEtqEqs], [CPros])
				Insert Into crProEtqEqs (CPros) Values (LocalProEt1.CPros)
			EndIf

			If Not Empty(LocalProEt1.CProEqs) And Not Seek(LocalProEt1.CProEqs, [crProEtqEqs], [CPros])
				Insert Into crProEtqEqs (CPros) Values (LocalProEt1.CProEqs)
			EndIf
		EndScan
	EndScan
	Use In LocalProEt
Else
	Insert Into crProEtqEqs (CPros) Values (pPro)
EndIf

Return .t.
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE montastrtpope
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE montastrtpope
Local lcStr

lcStr = []
Select crSigCdTom
Go Top in crSigCdTom
Scan
	If crSigCdTom.Selec = 1
		lcStr = lcStr + Iif(Empty(lcStr),[(],[,]) + Chr(39) + Str(crSigCdTom.Codigos,2) + Chr(39)		
	Endif
EndScan
If not Empty(lcStr)
	lcStr = lcStr + [)]
Endif

Return(lcStr)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE totalgrupo
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE totalgrupo
Private pPro, pDat, pHor

Zap In TotGrupo2

pPro = Iif(Empty(crTSigPro.CPros), Replicate(Chr(254), 14), crTSigPro.CPros)

Select Cgrus, Moeds, pCompos, Qtds From crSigPrCpo Into Cursor crtmp

Select crtmp
Scan
	Thisform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdGrp','TmpGru','Cgrus',crtmp.Cgrus,[cvestims])
	Select TotGrupo2
	Set Order To GruMoe
	Seek crtmp.Cgrus + crtmp.Moeds
	If Eof()
		Valgr = (crtmp.Qtds * crtmp.pCompos)
		Insert Into TotGrupo2 (Grupo, ValGrupo, Moeda ) Values ;
			(crtmp.Cgrus, Valgr, crtmp.Moeds)
	Else
		Replace ValGrupo With ValGrupo + (crtmp.Qtds * crtmp.pCompos) In TotGrupo2
	Endif
Endscan

Select TotGrupo2
Locate For Estimado = 1
If Not Eof()
	If crTSigPro.Moedas = ' '
		Replace Moedas With MoeCs In crTSigPro
	Endif

	Thisform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdMoe', 'crMoe', 'CMoes', crTSigPro.Moedas)
	Go Top In crMoe
	If Not Empty(crMoe.Moeqs)
		m.Moedas = crMoe.Moeqs
		_Qtdeqs = Iif(crMoe.Qtdeqs=0, 1, crMoe.Qtdeqs)
	Else
		m.Moedas = crTSigPro.Moedas
		_Qtdeqs = 1
	Endif
	Moeda  = fBuscarCotacao(m.Moedas, Datetime(), Thisform.Podatamgr) * _Qtdeqs

	_VlrT = 0
	Scan
		If Estimado = 1
			If TotGrupo2.Moeda <> crTSigPro.Moedas
				Thisform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdMoe', 'crMoe', 'CMoes', TotGrupo2.Moeda)
				Go Top In crMoe
				If Not Empty(crMoe.Moeqs)
					m.Moedas = crMoe.Moeqs
					_Qtdeqs = Iif(crMoe.Qtdeqs=0, 1, crMoe.Qtdeqs)
				Else
					m.Moedas = TotGrupo2.Moeda
					_Qtdeqs = 1
				Endif
				Moeg  = fBuscarCotacao(m.Moedas, Datetime(), Thisform.Podatamgr) * _Qtdeqs

				_VlrT = _VlrT + (TotGrupo2.ValGrupo * Moeg / Moeda)
			Else
				_VlrT = _VlrT + TotGrupo2.ValGrupo
			Endif
		Endif
	Endscan
	Replace Valors With _VlrT In crTSigPro
Endif

Select crSigPrCpo
Thisform.Fld_principal.pag_Resultado.result.composicao.gradeGRUPO2.Refresh

* Fim *********************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE verbarra
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE verbarra
LParameters pCod
Local llRet

For lnCnt = 1 To Len(Alltrim(pCod))
	If !IsDigit(Substr(pCod,lnCnt,1))
		llRet = .F.
		Exit
	EndIf
Next
Return(llRet)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mgrdcodpai
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mgrdcodpai
Local lcCodPro,lcFilQtd,lnEscolha,lnErro1,lcMcrsql1,lcMcrsql2,llContinua1,lnNreg1,;
	lnCbar,lcEmpBar1,lcIdChave1

lnSegIni = Datetime()
lcCodPai  = Iif(Type([CrTSigPro.Conjunts])=[N],Str(CrTSigPro.Conjunts),CrTSigPro.Conjunts)
lnEscolha = Thisform.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_CodPai.optiongroup1.Value
lnErro1 = Thisform.poDataMgr1.pnIdConn


Thisform.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta.grd_historico.RecordSource = ''
Thisform.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.Grd_Estoque.RecordSource = ''

Create Cursor crCpEtq1 (CPros c(14), EmpOs c(3), Grupos c(10), Contas c(10), CBars N(14), Qtds N(10,3), Pesos N(10,3), ;
	Localizas c(10), CodTams c(4), CodCors c(4), DataS d(8), CPs1s c(1), Locals c(10), CodAmss c(10), CBars_Old N(14))

lcFilQtd = Iif(lnEscolha = 2,[a.sqtds > 0],[0 = 0])

lcQuery = [Select e.cpros,e.empos,e.grupos,e.contas,e.cbars,e.qtds,e.pesos, e.localizas,e.codtams,] + ;
	[e.codcors,e.datas,e.CodAmss,'1' as cps1s, IsNull(c.CBars_old,0) as CBars_old, b.conjunts ] + ;
	[From SigOpEtq e ] + ;
	[Join SigCdPro b on e.cpros = b.cpros ] + ;
	[Left join SigOpEta c on e.cbars = c.cbars_new ] + ;
	[Where b.conjunts = ]+lcCodPai+[ ] + ;
	[And e.Empos In (Select b.cemps From SigCdEmp b Where Not b.dircolins=Space(32)) ] + ;
	[And e.cpros + e.empos + e.grupos + e.contas In (Select a.cpros + a.emps + a.grupos + a.estos ] + ;
	[From SigMvEst a join sigcdpro b on a.cpros = b.cpros ] + ;
	[where b.conjunts = ]+lcCodPai+[ and &lcFilQtd.) ] + ;
	[Union All ] + ;
	[Select e.cpros,e.empos,e.grupos,e.contas,e.cbars,e.qtds,e.pesos, e.localizas,e.codtams,] + ;
	[e.codcors,e.datas,e.CodAmss,'0' as cps1s, IsNull(c.CBars_old,0) as CBars_old, b.conjunts ] + ;
	[From SigOpEtq e ] + ;
	[Left join SigOpEta c on e.cbars = c.cbars_new ] + ;
	[Join SigCdPro b on e.cpros = b.cpros ] + ;
	[Where b.conjunts = ]+lcCodPai+[ ] + ;
	[And e.Empos In (Select b.cemps From SigCdEmp b Where b.dircolins=Space(32)) ] + ;
	[And e.cpros + e.empos + e.grupos + e.contas In (Select a.cpros + a.emps + a.grupos + a.estos ] + ;
	[From SigMvEst a join sigcdpro b on a.cpros = b.cpros ] + ;
	[where b.conjunts = ]+lcCodPai+[ and &lcFilQtd.) ] + ;
	[Order by 1,2,3,4,5]

lnQueryOk = Thisform.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrOpEtq2')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
Endif

If Used('crTmpChkEti1')
	Use In ('crTmpChkEti1')
Endif

Select CrOpEtq2
Index On CBars Tag CBars
Index On Str(CBars,14)+EmpOs Tag skgroup1
Set Order To ('') In CrOpEtq2

m.lcMcrsql1 = [Select a.empos, a.cbars, IsNull(c.CBars_old,0) as CBars_old ] + ;
	[From SigOpEtq a ] + ;
	[Left join SigOpEta c on a.cbars = c.cbars_new ] + ;
	[join SigCdPro b on a.cpros = b.cpros ] + ;
	[Where b.conjunts = ]+lcCodPai+[ ] + ;
	[Order By 1]

If m.lnErro1>0
	m.lnErro1 = Thisform.poDataMgr2.SqlExecute(m.lcMcrsql1, 'crTmpChkEti1')
Endif
If Used('crTmpChkEti1')
	Select crTmpChkEti1
	Set Order To ('') In crTmpChkEti1
	Locate

	Scan While m.lnErro1>0 And Not Eof('crTmpChkEti1')
		m.lnCbar = crTmpChkEti1.CBars
		m.lcEmpBar1 = crTmpChkEti1.EmpOs
		m.lcIdChave1 = (Str(m.lnCbar,14)+m.lcEmpBar1)

		Update CrOpEtq2 Set CPs1s='0' Where Str(CBars,14)+EmpOs=m.lcIdChave1
	Endscan
Endif

Select CrOpEtq2
Scan
	Scatter Memvar Memo
	m.Datas  = Nvl(m.Datas, Ctot([]))
	Insert Into crCpEtq1 From Memvar
Endscan

Zap In crMvEstPd
Zap In crMvEst

If Used([CsEtiqCodP])
	Zap In CsEtiqCodP
EndIf 


lcQuery = [Select a.CPros, a.Emps, a.Grupos, a.Estos, a.Sqtds, a.CodCors, a.CodTams, ] + ;
	[9999999999.999 as QtdEmpHs, a.SPesos, ] + ;
	[9999999999.999 as Pedidos, Space(20) as Fk_Chave, b.Rclis as DContas ] + ;
	[From SigMvEst a ] + ;
	[inner join SigCdCli b on a.Estos = b.Iclis ] + ;
	[inner join sigcdpro c on a.cpros = c.cpros ] + ;
	[Where c.conjunts = ]+lcCodPai+[ And &lcFilQtd ] + ;
	[Order By a.Emps, a.Grupos, a.Estos, a.CodTams, a.CodCors]

If (Thisform.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalEst]) < 1)
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (mGrdEstoque - LocalEst)')
	Return(.F.)
Endif

Select LocalEst
Scan
	Scatter Memvar Memo
	If Seek(LocalEst.Emps + LocalEst.Grupos + LocalEst.Estos + LocalEst.CodCors + LocalEst.CodTams, [CrMvEst], [EmpGruCon])
		Replace SQtds  With SQtds + LocalEst.SQtds, ;
			SPesos With SPesos + LocalEst.SPesos In CrMvEst
	Else
		Insert Into CrMvEst From Memvar
	Endif

	Replace Fk_Chave With fUniqueIds(), ;
		QtdEmphs With 0, ;
		Pedidos  With 0 In CrMvEst
Endscan


* Alimentando a Coluna de Entradas Pendentes
If Inlist(CrTSigPro.TipoEstos, 2, 3, 4)
	lcQuery = [Select a.CPros, a.CodCors, a.CodTams, a.Emps, a.Dopes, a.Numes, a.Qtds, a.QtBaixas ] + ;
		[From SigMvIts a, SigCdOpe o, SigCdTom t, SigCdPro b ]
Else
	lcQuery = [Select a.CPros, '    ' as CodCors, '    ' as CodTams, a.Emps, a.Dopes, a.Numes, a.Qtds, a.QtBaixas ] + ;
		[From SigMvItn a, SigCdOpe o, SigCdTom t, SigCdPro b ]
Endif
lcQuery = lcQuery + [Where b.conjunts = ]+lcCodPai+[ And ] + ;
		[a.Dopes = o.Dopes And ] + ;
		[o.TipoOps = t.Codigos And ] + ;
		[a.cpros = b.cpros And ] + ;
		[t.EntPends = 1]

lnQueryOk = Thisform.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalEstI])
If (lnQueryOk < 1)
	=Messagebox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, [Falha na Conexão])
	Thisform.Release
Endif

Select LocalEstI
Scan
	lcEdn = LocalEstI.Emps + LocalEstI.Dopes + Str(LocalEstI.Numes, 6)

	lcQuery = [Select a.GrupoOs, a.ContaOs, b.Rclis From SigMvCab a inner join SigCdCli b on a.ContaOs = b.Iclis Where a.EmpDopNums = ']+ lcEdn +[']
	lnQueryOk = Thisform.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalEest])
	If (lnQueryOk < 1)
		=Messagebox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, [Falha na Conexão])
		Thisform.Release
	Endif
	Select LocalEest
	Go Top In LocalEest

	Select CrMvEst
	Locate For Grupos = LocalEest.GrupoOs And Estos = LocalEest.ContaOs And ;
		CPros = LocalEstI.CPros And CodCors = LocalEstI.CodCors And ;
		CodTams = LocalEstI.CodTams

	If Eof([CrMvEst])
		Insert Into CrMvEst (Emps, Grupos, Estos, CPros, CodCors, CodTams, Fk_Chave, DContas) ;
			Values (LocalEstI.Emps, LocalEest.GrupoOs, LocalEest.ContaOs, ;
			LocalEstI.CPros, LocalEstI.CodCors, LocalEstI.CodTams, ;
			fUniqueIds(), LocalEest.Rclis)
	Endif

	Replace Qtds 	 With Qtds 	   + LocalEstI.Qtds, ;
		QtBaixas With QtBaixas + LocalEstI.QtBaixas, ;
		EntPends With EntPends + (LocalEstI.Qtds - LocalEstI.QtBaixas) In CrMvEst

	If ((LocalEstI.Qtds - LocalEstI.QtBaixas) > 0)
		Select crMvEstPd
		Go Top In crMvEstPd
		Locate For EmpDopNums = lcEdn
		If Eof([crMvEstPd])
			Insert Into crMvEstPd (Fk_Chave, EmpDopNums) Values (crSigMvEst.Fk_Chave, lcEdn)
		Endif
		Replace Qtds With Qtds + (LocalEstI.Qtds - LocalEstI.QtBaixas) In crMvEstPd
		If Not Eof()
			Replace QtdEmphs With QtdEmphs + (LocalEstI.Qtds - LocalEstI.QtBaixas) In CrMvEst
*!*				*** Tiago - 04/10/11 - Vianna - Atualiza cursor com as operações que possuem empenho
*!*				If (LocalEstI.Qtds - LocalEstI.QtBaixas) > 0
*!*					Insert into crEmpenhado(Cpros, CodCors, CodTams, Qtds, Emps, Dopes, Numes, ContaDs, GrupoDs, Datas) ;
*!*						values (LocalEstI.Cpros, LocalEstI.CodCors, LocalEstI.CodTams, (LocalEstI.Qtds - LocalEstI.QtBaixas), ;
*!*						 LocalEstI.Emps, LocalEstI.Dopes, LocalEstI.Numes, crTprMvCab.ContaDs, crTprMvCab.GrupoDs, crTprMvCab.Datas)
*!*				EndIf
		Endif
		
	Endif
Endscan

Select CrMvEst
Set Order To EmpGruCon
Go Top

With ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_CodPai.Grd_Estoque
	.RecordSource = 'CrMvEst'
	.Column1.ControlSource = 'CrMvEst.emps'
	.Column2.ControlSource = 'CrMvEst.grupos'
	.Column3.ControlSource = 'CrMvEst.estos'
	.Column4.ControlSource = 'CrMvEst.sqtds'
	.Column4.Header1.Caption = 'Qtd ('+CrTSigPro.CUnis+')'
	.Column5.ControlSource = 'CrMvEst.codcors'
	.Column6.ControlSource = 'CrMvEst.codtams'
	.Column7.Visible = .T.
	If IsEmpty(CrSigCdPam.transfres) And IsEmpty(crSigCdPac.MovEmpEco)
		.Column7.ControlSource = ""
		.Column7.Header1.Caption = ""
		.Column7.InputMask       = ""
		.Column7.text1.InputMask = ""
		.Column7.Visible = .F.
	Else
		.Column7.ControlSource = 'CrMvEst.QtdEmphs'
	Endif
	.Column9.ControlSource  = 'CrMvEst.Pedidos'
	.Column10.ControlSource = 'CrMvEst.EntPends'
	.Column8.Visible = .T.
	If Not IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.cEstos) And CrTSigPro.cEstos = '3'
		.Column8.ControlSource = 'CrMvEst.sPesos'
		.Column8.Header1.Caption = 'Qtd ( '+CrTSigPro.CUniPs+' )'
		.Column8.InputMask       = "99999999.999"
		.Column8.text1.InputMask = "99999999.999"
	Else
		.Column8.ControlSource = ""
		.Column8.Header1.Caption = ""
		.Column8.InputMask       = ""
		.Column8.text1.InputMask = ""
		.Column8.Visible = .F.
	Endif
	.Refresh
EndWith


If !IsEmpty(CrSigCdPam.transfres) Or !IsEmpty(CrSigCdPac.MovEmpEco)

	Select CrMvEst
	Select Distinct Emps From CrMvEst Into Cursor csTmpContas ReadWrite 
	Select csTmpContas
	Go Top
	Scan 
		
		For i=1 to 2
			
			lcMovEmp = []
			If i=1
				lcMovEmp = CrSigCdPam.transfres	
			Else
				lcMovEmp = CrSigCdPac.MovEmpEco
			EndIf 
			
			Select CrSigCdOpe
			_QtRet = 0
			Locate For (CrSigCdOpe.dopes = lcMovEmp)
			If Found() And CrSigCdOpe.estoqs # 1
				lcDopes  = CrSigCdOpe.dopes
				_QtRet = 0
				lcEdnIs = csTmpContas.Emps + lcDopes + Str(0,6)
				lcEdnFs = csTmpContas.Emps  + lcDopes + Str(999999,6)
				
				If Not Used('CrEmpenhado')
					*** Tiago - 04/10/2011 - Criado cursor para armazenar as movimentações que possuem empenho - Vianna
					lcQuery = [Select cpros, codCors, codTams, emps, dopes, numes, qtds, Space(10) as ContaDs, Space(10) as GrupoDs, DtAlts as Datas From SigMvIts Where 0=1]
					If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrEmpenhado') < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão - CrEmpenhado')
						ThisForm.Release()
						Return .f.
					EndIf
				EndIf 
				***
				If InList(CrTSigPro.TipoEstos,2,3,4)
					lcQuery = [Select cpros, codCors, codTams, emps, dopes, numes, qtds, qtbaixas ]+;
							[From SigMvIts Where EmpDopNums Between ?lcEdnIs And ?lcEdnFs And Cpros in(Select cpros from sigcdpro where conjunts = ]+lcCodPai+[) ]
				Else
					lcQuery = [Select cpros, '    ' as codCors, '    ' as codTams, emps, dopes, numes, qtds, qtbaixas ]+;
							[From SigMvItn Where EmpDopNums Between ?lcEdnIs And ?lcEdnFs And Cpros in(Select cpros from sigcdpro where conjunts = ]+lcCodPai+[)]
				Endif
				lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrSigMvItn')
				If lnQueryOk < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					ThisForm.Release
				EndIf 
				Select CrSigMvItn
				Go Top
				If Not Eof()
					Scan
						If Qtds - QtBaixas # 0
							lcEdn = crSigMvItn.Emps + crSigMvItn.Dopes + Str(crSigMvItn.Numes, 6)
							
							lcQuery = [Select Grupoos, ContaOs, GrupoDs, ContaDs, Datas From SigMvCab Where EmpDopNums = ']+ lcEdn +[']
							If ThisForm.PodataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcquery,'CrTprMvCab') > 0
								Select CrMvEst
								Do Case 
								Case CrTSigPro.TipoEstos = 2
									Locate For Grupos = crTprMvCab.GrupoOs And Estos = crTprMvCab.ContaOs And ;
										   Cpros = crSigMvItn.Cpros And CodCors = crSigMvItn.CodCors And Emps = crSigMvItn.Emps
								Case CrTSigPro.TipoEstos = 3
									Locate For Grupos = crTprMvCab.GrupoOs And Estos = crTprMvCab.ContaOs And ;
										   Cpros = crSigMvItn.Cpros And CodTams = crSigMvItn.CodTams And Emps = crSigMvItn.Emps
								Case CrTSigPro.TipoEstos = 4
									Locate For Grupos = crTprMvCab.GrupoOs And Estos = crTprMvCab.ContaOs And ;
										   Cpros = crSigMvItn.Cpros And CodCors = crSigMvItn.CodCors And ;
										   CodTams = crSigMvItn.CodTams And Emps = crSigMvItn.Emps
								OtherWise 
									Locate For Grupos = crTprMvCab.GrupoOs And Estos = crTprMvCab.ContaOs And ;
										   Cpros = crSigMvItn.Cpros And Emps = crSigMvItn.Emps
								EndCase 								
								If Not Eof()
									Replace QtdEmphs With QtdEmphs + (crSigMvItn.Qtds - crSigMvItn.QtBaixas) In CrMvEst
									*** Tiago - 04/10/11 - Vianna - Atualiza cursor com as operações que possuem empenho
									If (crSigMvItn.Qtds - crSigMvItn.QtBaixas) > 0
										Insert into crEmpenhado(Cpros, CodCors, CodTams, Qtds, Emps, Dopes, Numes, ContaDs, GrupoDs, Datas) ;
											values (crSigMvItn.Cpros, crSigMvItn.CodCors, crSigMvItn.CodTams, (crSigMvItn.Qtds - crSigMvItn.QtBaixas), ;
											 crSigMvItn.Emps, crSigMvItn.Dopes, crSigMvItn.Numes, crTprMvCab.ContaDs, crTprMvCab.GrupoDs, crTprMvCab.Datas)
									EndIf
								Endif
							Endif
						EndIf
					EndScan
				EndIf
			EndIf
		Next 
		Select csTmpContas
	EndScan 
EndIf


Thisform.Draw
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE acertagradeeticodpai
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE acertagradeeticodpai
Local lcCor, lcTam, lcLoc, lcFil, lnTot
lnTot = 0
lnTot2= 0

If !Used('CrTSigPro') Or !Used('CrMvEst') Or !Used('crCpEtq1')
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

If Used('CsEtiqCodP')
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_CodPai.grd_historico.RecordSource = ''
EndIf

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_CodPai
	*********************************************************************
	* Dados Para Filtro da Grade de Etiquetas
	lcCor = .GetCor.Value
	lcTam = .getTam.Value
	lcLoc = .getLoc.Value
	lcFil = IIf(!IsEmpty(lcCor),[a.codcors = ']+lcCor+[' And ],'')+;
			IIf(!IsEmpty(lcTam),[a.codtams = ']+lcTam+[' And ],'')+;
			IIf(!IsEmpty(lcLoc),[a.localizas = ']+lcLoc+[' And ],'')+ [.T.]

	lcEstEmp = CrMvEst.emps
	lcEstGru = CrMvEst.grupos
	lcEstCta = CrMvEst.estos
	lcCodPro = CrTSigPro.cpros

	Select a.cpros,a.empos,a.grupos,a.contas,a.cbars,a.qtds,a.pesos,a.localizas,a.codtams,a.codcors,a.datas,a.cps1s,a.locals,a.codamss, a.CBars_old ;
  	  From crCpEtq1 a ;
	 Where a.empos = lcEstEmp And ;
		   a.grupos = lcEstGru And ;
		   a.contas = lcEstCta And &lcFil. ;
	  Into Cursor CsEtiqCodP ReadWrite

	Select CsEtiqCodP
	Index on cBars Tag cBars
	*** Mena 12/07/2013 - estes indices foram criados para ordenar a grade de etiquetas no click das colunas correspondentes ***
	Index on qtds  Tag qtds
	Index on pesos Tag pesos
	Index on localizas Tag localizas
	Index on codtams Tag codtams
	Index on codcors Tag codcors
	Index on DTOS(datas) Tag datas

	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_CodPai.grd_historico
		.RecordSource = 'CsEtiqCodP'
		.Column1.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqCodP.cbars'
		.Column2.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqCodP.qtds'
		.Column3.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqCodP.pesos'
		.Column4.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqCodP.localizas'
		.Column5.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqCodP.codtams'
		.Column6.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqCodP.codcors'
		.Column7.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqCodP.datas'
		.Column8.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqCodP.CPros'
		.Column9.ControlSource = 'CsEtiqCodP.CBars_old'
		*.Column8.Visible = (ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.chkEquivalentes.Value = 1)
		.Column2.Header1.Caption = 'Qtd ('+CrTSigPro.CUnis+')'
		.Column3.Header1.Caption = 'Peso'
		If Not IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.cestos) And CrTSigPro.cEstos = '3'
			.Column3.Header1.Caption = 'Qtd ('+CrTSigPro.CUniPs+')'
		EndIf
	EndWith

	.getRefs.ControlSource = [CsEtiqCodP.CodAmss]

	Select CsEtiqCodP
	Go Top
	Scan
		lnTot = lnTot + CsEtiqCodP.qtds
		lnTot2= lnTot2 + CsEtiqCodP.Pesos
	EndScan
	.getTotEti.Value = lnTot
	.getTotEti2.Value = lnTot2
	.getTotEti.Refresh
	.getTotEti2.Refresh
	
	Select CsEtiqCodP
	Go Top

	.Grd_Historico.Refresh
EndWith
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Release
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Release
With ThisForm
	.poDataMgr1.Release
	.poDataMgr2.Release
EndWith 
=DoDefault()
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Load
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Load
=fConfigGeral()

* Cria o Cursor das matérias primas para filtro
Create Cursor CsMatPrima (mats c(14),dcompos c(40),qtd_inicial n(10,2),qtd_final n(10,2),apenas l, Cgrus c(3), Tipos c(20))
Select CsMatPrima
Index On mats Tag mats
Append Blank
Set Order To

Create Cursor CsEtiqueta (CPros c(14), CBars n(14), Qtds n(10,3), Pesos n(9,2), Localizas c(10), CodTams c(4), ;
						  CodCors c(4), Datas d(8), Cps1s c(1), Locals c(3))
Index on cBars Tag cBars

Create Cursor CsEtiqCodP (CPros c(14), CBars n(14), Qtds n(10,3), Pesos n(9,2), Localizas c(10), CodTams c(4), ;
						  CodCors c(4), Datas d(8), Cps1s c(1), Locals c(3))
Index on cBars Tag cBars

Create Cursor CsFase (ordems n(2,0),grupos c(10),etiquetas l,descrs c(20),obs m)

Create Cursor CrSigPrCpo (mats c(14),dcompos c(30),unicompos c(3),pcompos n(12,2),qtds n(9,3),;
				moeds c(3),obscompos c(10),qtscons n(9,3),grupos c(10),dscgrp c(20),cats c(6), cUniPs c(3), Pesos n(9,3) ,TpCalcCus n(1),Tipos C(20))

Create Cursor CrSigMvEst (CPros c(14), Emps c(3), Grupos c(10), Estos c(10), SQtds n(12,3), CodCors c(4), CodTams c(4), ;
						QtdEmphs n(12,3), SPesos n(16,3), Pedidos n(12,3), Qtds n(12,3), QtBaixas n(12,3), ;
						EntPends n(12,3), EmpDs c(3), Fk_Chave c(20), DContas char(40))
Index On Emps + Grupos + Estos + CodCors + CodTams Tag EmpGruCon
Index On Emps + Grupos + Estos + CodCors + CodTams Tag Emps
Index on Grupos + Estos + Emps Tag Grupos
Index on Estos + Grupos + Emps Tag Estos
Index on Sqtds Tag Sqtds
Index on Sqtds Tag Sqtdsd Descending 
Index on CodCors Tag CodCors
Index on CodTams Tag CodTams


Create Cursor CrMvEst (CPros c(14), Emps c(3), Grupos c(10), Estos c(10), SQtds n(12,3), CodCors c(4), CodTams c(4), ;
						QtdEmphs n(12,3), SPesos n(16,3), Pedidos n(12,3), Qtds n(12,3), QtBaixas n(12,3), ;
						EntPends n(12,3), EmpDs c(3), Fk_Chave c(20), DContas char(40))
Index On Emps + Grupos + Estos + CodCors + CodTams Tag EmpGruCon
Index On Emps + Grupos + Estos + CodCors + CodTams Tag Emps
Index on Grupos + Estos + Emps Tag Grupos
Index on Estos + Grupos + Emps Tag Estos
index on Cpros + Emps + Grupos + Estos + CodCors + CodTams tag Cpros
Index on Sqtds Tag Sqtds
Index on Sqtds Tag Sqtdsd Descending 
Index on CodCors Tag CodCors
Index on CodTams Tag CodTams

Create Cursor crSigMvEstPd (Fk_Chave c(20), EmpDopNums c(29), Qtds n(12,3))

Create Cursor crMvEstPd (Fk_Chave c(20), EmpDopNums c(29), Qtds n(12,3))

Create Cursor crFilOper( EmpDopNums c(29) )
Index on EmpDopNums Tag EDN

Create Cursor crSigMvEtr (CPros c(14), Emps c(3), Grupos c(10), Estos c(10), SQtds n(12,3), CodCors c(4), CodTams c(4), ;
						 QtdEmphs n(12,3), SPesos n(16,3), Pedidos n(12,3), DContas c(40))
Index On Emps + Grupos + Estos + CodCors + CodTams Tag EmpGruCon
Index On Emps + Grupos + Estos + CodCors + CodTams Tag Emps
Index on Grupos + Estos + Emps Tag Grupos
Index on Estos + Grupos + Emps Tag Estos
Index on Sqtds Tag Sqtds
Index on Sqtds Tag Sqtdsd Descending 
Index on CodCors Tag CodCors
Index on CodTams Tag CodTams

Create Cursor CrSigMvHst (datas d,numes n(6,0),dopes c(20),qtds n(10,3),sqtds n(12,3),opers c(1),;
				codbarras n(8,0), pesos n(12,3), spesos n(12,3), emps c(3), empos c(3), Fators n(9,3))				

Create Cursor CrProdutos (cpros c(14),dpros c(40),pecas c(10), offs c(1))

Create Cursor Tmp_EstPro (cpros c(14), saldo n(16,4), nTemSaldos n(10))
Index On CPros Tag CPros

Create Cursor Tmp_CompoF (cpros c(14),compos c(10),mats c(14),qtds n(12,4), Cgrus c(3), Tipos c(10))
Index On Compos + CPros + Mats + Tipos + Transform(Qtds * 10000,'@L 999999999999') Tag skGroup1
Index On CPros Tag CPros

Create Cursor crGrpOpe (Emps c(3), Dopes c(20), Numes n(6), Qtds n(10, 3))

Create Cursor crMGOpe (DgOpes c(20))
Index On DgOpes Tag DgOpes

Create Cursor TotGrupo2 (Grupo c(3), ValGrupo n(12,3), Moeda c(3), Estimado n(1))
Index On Grupo + Moeda Tag GruMoe
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Init
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Init
Lparameters pFrm, prEca

ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.cntSombra.lblSombra.Caption = ThisForm.Caption
ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.cntSombra.lblTitulo.Caption = ThisForm.Caption

Private pcdope, pcdope2, pPro
Local lnCor, lnTam, lnColuna, lnShpW

ThisForm.Tpcalcp = 0
ThisForm.Composiciona = []
ThisForm.prEca = prEca

m.pcdope = Null
m.pcdope2 = Null
m.pPro = Chr(254)
lcQueryCot     	= [Select a.cmoes,a.datas,a.valos,a.cidchaves From SigCdCot a]
lcQueryEesti   	= [Select a.cpros,a.emps,a.dopes,a.numes,a.units,a.moedas,a.cunis,a.moefats,]+;
						[a.pesos,a.univals,a.moevals,a.tpesos,a.totas,a.qtds,a.fators,]+;
						[a.fatvals,a.descvals,a.cidchaves From SigMvItn a]
lcQueryEmp     	= [Select a.cemps,a.razas From SigCdEmp a Where a.cemps = ']+_empr+[' ]
lcQueryMoe     	= [Select a.cmoes,a.dmoes,a.cotas From SigCdMoe a]
lcQueryOpe     	= [Select a.dopes,a.ndopes,a.estoqs From SigCdOpe a where (a.dopes=?m.pcdope or a.dopes=?m.pcdope2)]
lcQueryParam   	= [Select a.gesind,a.moedetqs,a.grupoests,a.contaests,a.transfres,a.empmasters,a.moepadvens,tpcbars,tpinstalas ]+;
				   [From SigCdPam a]
lcQueryParac   	= [Select * From SigCdPac a]
lcQueryLpc		= [Select lPrecos From SigCdLpc a Where emps = ']+_empr+[' ]

ThisForm.poObjExt2 = Null

With ThisForm
	.LockScreen = .T.
	.cPrompt = ThisForm.Caption
	.Fld_Principal.pag_resultado.Result.Selecao.Bnt_Preco.Visible = .T.
	.lPrimeira  = .T.
	.poDataMgr1 = CreateObject('fSqlConector','SigOpCgp')  && conexao dos dados produtos...
	.poDataMgr2 = CreateObject('fSqlConector','SigOpCgp2') && conexao dos dados do palm...
	.poDataMgr  = .poDataMgr1
	.EmpOperas  = []
	.ChamadaExt = Iif(Type([pFrm])=[C], pFrm, [])

	If (.poDatamgr1.pnIdconn>0 And .poDatamgr2.pnIdconn>0)
		With .poDataMgr1
			.AddCursor('SigCdEmp'  	,'cemps'     	,'CrSigCdEmp'  	,'','',lcQueryEmp)
			.AddCursor('SigCdOpe'  	,'dopes'     	,'CrSigCdOpe'  	,'','',lcQueryOpe)
			.AddCursor('SigCdPam'	,'empmasters'	,'CrSigCdPam'	,'','',lcQueryParam)
			.AddCursor('SigCdPaC'	,'cidchaves'	,'CrSigCdPac'	,'','',lcQueryParac)
			.AddCursor('SigCdLpc'	,'lPrecos'		,'CrSigCdLpc'	,'','',lcQueryLpc)
			.Requery('CrSigCdPam')
			.Requery('CrSigCdPaC')
			.Requery('CrSigCdEmp')
			.Requery('CrSigCdLpc')
			m.pcdope = crSigCdPam.transfres
			m.pcdope2 = CrSigCdPaC.MovEmpEco
			.Requery('CrSigCdOpe')
			.AddCursor('SigCdPrf','Cidchaves','CrSigCdPrf','','',[Select * from SigCdPrf where 0=1])
		EndWith

		*** Mena - 06/10/2017 - seleciona contas visiveis - visiveis apenas com acesso ***
		lcSql = [Select iclis From SigCdCli a, SigCdGcr b where b.invisivel=1 and a.grupos = b.codigos and a.invisivel = 1 ]
		ThisForm.Podatamgr1.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'crEstInv')

		ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.chkEquivalentes.Value = Iif(crSigCdPac.CProEqs = 2, 1, 0)
		
		ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.btnProduto.Visible = Not ThisForm.prEca And fChecaAcesso([SigCdPro])
		
		lcQuery = [Select TpInstalas From SigCdPam]
		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [csTmpParam]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (csTmpParam)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Go Top In csTmpParam

		lStrQuery = [Select a.*,b.Padraos From SigOpTdz a, SigOpTde b ]+;
					[Where a.Codigos = b.CodTabDs And b.Emps = ?_Empr ]
		If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(lStrQuery,'CsSigOpTdz') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (CsSigOpTdz)')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		Select CsSigOpTdz
		Go Top
		If Eof()
			lStrQuery = [Select a.*,0 As Padraos From SigOpTdz a ]
			If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(lStrQuery,'CsSigOpTdz') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (CsSigOpTdz)')
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
		EndIf
		Select CsSigOpTdz
		Index On Codigos Tag Codigos
		Go Top

		If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute([Select * From SigCdCrg ],'CrSigCdCrg') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrSigCdCrg)')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		Select CrSigCdCrg
		Index On CCargs Tag CCargs
		Go Top

		If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute([Select Colecoes From SigCdCol Where Not ConProds = 2], 'crSigCdCol') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (crSigCdCol)')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		Select crSigCdCol
		Index On Colecoes Tag Colecoes
		If Not Seek(Space(10), 'crSigCdCol', 'Colecoes')
			Append Blank In crSigCdCol
		EndIf
		Go Top


		*-- Rafael - 12/04/2017 - Coloca a Lista de preço que está cadastrada para a empresa
		ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Lista.Value = CrSigCdLpc.lPrecos
		*****************
		lStrQuery = [Select Tipos From SigCdCom]
					  
		If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery,'LocalCdCom') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (LocalCdCom)')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		Go Top In LocalCdCom

		lcQuery = [Select a.Tipos, a.Custos, b.CGrus ] + ;
				    [From SigCdTpc a, SigCdCom b ] + ;
				   [Where a.Tipos = b.Tipos]

		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crSigCdCom]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crSigCdCom)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Select crSigCdCom
		Index On Tipos + CGrus Tag Tipos


		lcQuery = [Select Tipos ] + ;
				    [From SigCdTpc ] + ;
				   [Where Custos = 2]

		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crSigCdTpc]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crSigCdTpc)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Select crSigCdTpc
		Index On Tipos Tag Tipos

		With thisform.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.result.Composicao
			.Enabled = fChecaAcesso('SigCdPro', 'COMPOSICAO')
			If Not .Enabled 
				thisform.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.result.Custo.Caption = 'Composição'
				thisform.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.result.Composicao.Caption = ''
			EndIf
		EndWith
		
		If fChecaAcesso([SIGOPCGP], [CNTDADOSPG])
			ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.cntDados1.Visible = .T.
			ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_peso_medio.Visible = .T.
			ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.Say1.Visible = .T.
		Else
			ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.cntDados1.Visible = .F.
			ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_peso_medio.Visible = .F.
			ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.Say1.Visible = .F.
		EndIf 
		
		* Acesso Para Observação de Compras - Richard em 26/02/2016
		llAcO = fChecaAcesso([SIGOPCGP], [OBSCOMPRAS])
		
		*-- Rafael - 31/10/2016 - QUANDO TEM O ACESSO PARA A OBS COMPRAS O SISTEMA DIMINUI O TAMANHO DO CAMPO DPRO3S
		ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.getObsCompras.Visible = llAcO
		ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.getDPro3s.Height = Iif(llAcO,50,102)
		
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.getObsCompras.Visible = llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.lbl_qtd_minima.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_qtd_minima.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.say25.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_Datainclusao.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_UsuInc.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.say23.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_Dtucp.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.say24.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_Vucp.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_Mucp.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.lbl_fator_venda.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_fator_venda.Visible = Not llAcO
*!*			ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_fator_venda_moeda.Visible = Not llAcO

		* Acesso Para Fornecedor - Richard em 26/02/2016
		llAcO = fChecaAcesso([SIGOPCGP], [FORNECEDOR])
		ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.lbl_fornecedor.Visible    = Not llAcO
		ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_cd_fornecedor.Visible = Not llAcO
		ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.get_ds_fornecedor.Visible = Not llAcO
		
		*****************

		lcQuery = [Select a.Cods, a.Descs ] + ;
					[From SigCdCls a ] 

		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crSigCdCls]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crSigCdCls)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Select crSigCdCls
		Index On Cods Tag Cods

		lcSql = [Select Count(1) as Qt From SigCdUni WherE cEstos = '3' ]
		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, 'CstmpUni') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (Init - CsTmpUni)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Go Top in CsTmpUni
		ThisForm.Habilitapeso = CsTmpUni.Qt > 0

		If fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','GRPOPE')
			ThisForm.poDataMgr1.CursorQuery('SigCdGpe', 'LocalGope', .f., .f., 'DgOpes, NGopes')
			Select LocalGope
			Scan
				If fChecaAcesso('GE' + Padl(LocalGope.NGopes, 8, '0'))
					Insert Into crMGOpe (DgOpes) Values (LocalGope.DgOpes)
				EndIf
			EndScan
		Else
			With ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta
				.chkGrpOpe.Visible = .f.
				.chkGrpOpe.Enabled = .f.
			EndWith
		EndIf
		
		With ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.Result.Estoque
			If CrSigCdPac.ddCongps > 0
				.Periodo_Historico.visible = .t.
				.periodo_Historico.Get_HistI.Value = Date() - CrSigCdPac.ddCongps
				.Periodo_historico.Get_Histf.Value = Date()
			Endif
		EndWith

		* Inicializa campos
		With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro
			With .Principal.cmbSituas
				.Clear
				.Additem([Ativos  ])
				.Additem([Inativos])
				.Additem([Ambos   ])
				.Value = [Ambos   ]
			EndWith
			With .Principal.cmbImagem
				.Clear
				.Additem([c/Imagem])
				.Additem([s/Imagem])
				.Additem([Ambos   ])
				.Value = [Ambos   ]
			EndWith
		
			With .Principal
				.Get_produto_inicial.Value = []
				.Get_produto_final.Value   = []
				.Get_descricao.Value       = []
				.Get_cd_grupo.Value        = []
				.Get_ds_grupo.Value        = []
				.Get_cd_sgrupo.Value       = []
				.Get_Barra.Value           = 0
				.Get_peso_inicial.Value    = 0
				.Get_peso_final.Value      = 0
				.Get_peso_inicial.Value    = 0
				.Get_peso_final.Value      = 0
				.getEquivalente.Value 	   = []
				.Get_Ref_Fornecedor.Value  = []
				.Get_ReffsF.Value          = []
				.Opt_TpReffs.Value         = 3
				.Get_Promo.Value           = []
				.Get_Venda_Inicial.Value   = 0
				.Get_Venda_Final.Value     = 0
				.Get_Moe_Venda.Value       = []
				.Get_Cd_Fornecedor.Value    = []
				.Get_Ds_Fornecedor.Value    = []
				.Get_Custo_Inicial.Value    = 0
				.Get_Custo_Final.Value      = 0
				.Get_Moe_Custo.Value        = []
				.Get_Estimado_Inicial.Value = 0
				.Get_Estimado_Final.Value   = 0
				.Get_Moe_Estimado.Value     = []
				.getConjunto.Value 			= []
				.getCodMacro.Value 			= []			
				.chkOff.value 				= 0		
				.Refresh
				.Get_Operação.Value  = Space(20)
				.Get_OperaçãoI.Value = 0
				.OptOperacao.Value   = 1
				.OptPagamento.Value  = 3
				.OptTpData.Value     = 1
				.Get_ContaO.Value     = Space(10)
				.Get_NContaO.Value    = Space(30)
				.Say_Conta.Visible   = .F.
				.Get_ContaO.Visible   = .F.
				.Get_NContaO.Visible  = .F.
				.Get_CPF.Visible     = .F.
				.cmdConjuge.Visible  = .F.
				.OptTpData.Option2.Visible = .T.
			EndWith
		EndWith 
		
		llAltPreco   = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','ALTPRECO') And fChecaAcesso('SigCdPro','ALTERAR')
		llDados      = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','COMPLETO') Or  fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','DADOS')
		llcusto 	 = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','COMPLETO') Or  fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','CUSTO') Or  fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','FCUSTO') 
		llEstoque    = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','COMPLETO') Or  fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','ESTOQUE')
		llRelPecas   = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','RELPECAS')
		llListar     = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','LISTAR')
		llrcusto 	 = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','COMPLETO') Or  fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','CUSTO') Or  fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','RCUSTO') 
		llValor      = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','VALOR')
		llVCusto     = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','VCUSTO')
		llVValor     = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','VVALOR')
		llProducao   = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','COMPLETO') Or fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','PRODUCAO')
		llEtiquetas  = IIF(llEstoque,.T.,fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','ETIQUETAS'))
		llAltObs     = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','ALTOBS')
		.llUser 	 = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','IMAGEM')
		llOperacao   = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','OPERACAO')
		lldoc 		 = fChecaAcesso([SigCdPro], [CDDOCS])		
		llCompo 	 = fChecaAcesso([SigCdPro], [COMPOSICAO])
		llMin 		 = .t.
		
		* Acesso Para Referencia do Fornecedor - Rafael - 06/2016
		llAcRefForec = fChecaAcesso([SIGOPCGP], [REFFORNEC])
		
		
		
		ThisForm.Habvalor = llVCusto

		With ThisForm.fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro
			.Principal.Enabled = llOperacao
			If llOperacao
				.Principal.Caption = 'Operação'
			Else
				.Principal.Caption = ''
			EndIf
		EndWith

		With .Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result
			.custo.cntValor.altpreco.Visible = llAltPreco
			.custo.cntValor.altpreco.enabled = llAltPreco
			If !llrcusto
				.custo.Enabled           = .F.
				.custo.Caption           = ''
			Else
				.custo.Enabled           = .T.
			EndIf
			If !llEstoque
				.Estoque.Enabled              = .F.
				.Estoque.Caption              = ''
				If !llEtiquetas
					.Pag_Etiqueta.Enabled     = .F.
					.Pag_Etiqueta.Caption     = ''
				Else
					.Pag_Etiqueta.Enabled     = .T.
				EndIf
			Else
				.Estoque.Enabled              = .T.
				.Pag_Etiqueta.Enabled         = .T.
			EndIf

			.selecao.Imagem.enabled              = llRelPecas
			.selecao.Imagem.Visible              = llRelPecas
			.selecao.Listar.enabled              = llListar
			.selecao.Listar.Visible              = llListar
			.selecao.lbl_fornecedor.Visible      = llCusto
			.selecao.Get_cd_fornecedor.Visible   = llCusto
			.Selecao.Bot_Fornecedor.Visible      = llCusto
			.selecao.Get_ds_fornecedor.Visible   = llCusto
			.selecao.lbl_ref_fornecedor.Visible  = llAcRefForec &&Acesso Para Referencia do Fornecedor - Rafael - 06/2016
			.Selecao.get_ref_fornecedor.Visible  = llAcRefForec &&Acesso Para Referencia do Fornecedor - Rafael - 06/2016
			
			.Selecao.Grd_Produtos.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','IIf(CrProdutos.situas = 2,RGB(255,0,0),;
			                                                Iif(CrProdutos.Cproeqs<>[ ],RGB(0,0,192),Rgb(0,0,0)))','Column')
			.Selecao.Grd_produtos.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','IIf(CrProdutos.situas = 2,IIf(CrProdutos.foralinha = 1,RGB(255,255,128),RGB(255,255,255)),IIf(CrProdutos.foralinha = 1,RGB(255,255,128),;
															RGB(255,255,255)))','Column')	&& Mena - 16/02/2018 - se fora de linha destaca em amarelo
			
			.Selecao.grd_produtos.Column3.DynamicBackColor = IIf(CrProdutos.Offs = 'S','RGB(0,0,0)','RGB(255,255,255)')
			.Selecao.grd_produtos.Column3.DynamicForeColor = IIf(CrProdutos.Offs = 'S','RGB(255,255,255)','RGB(0,0,0)')
										
			.Custo.cntCusto2.Visible = llvCusto
			.Custo.cntCusto2.Enabled = llvCusto
			.custo.cntCusto.Visible = llvCusto
			.custo.cntCusto.Enabled = llvCusto
			.custo.cntValor.Visible = llvValor
			.custo.cntValor.Enabled = llvValor
			.Fase.AltObs.EnaBled    = llAltObs
			.Fase.Cmdgfigura.Enabled = llAltObs
			If Not llvValor
				.custo.grd_custo.precoMT.Width = 0
				.custo.grd_custo.moedaMT.Width = 0
			EndIf

			If llProducao
				If CrSigCdPam.gesind = 1
					.Fase.Enabled = .T.
				Else
					.Fase.Enabled = .F.
				EndIf
			Else
				.Fase.Enabled = .F.
			EndIf
		EndWith
		
		Create Cursor creesti (CPROS C(14) Not Null, ;
			EMPS C(3) Not Null, ;
			DOPES C(20) Not Null, ;
			NUMES N(6, 0) Not Null, ;
			UNITS N(12, 6) Not Null, ;
			MOEDAS C(3) Not Null, ;
			CUNIS C(3) Not Null, ;
			MOEFATS C(3) Not Null, ;
			PESOS N(9, 2) Not Null, ;
			UNIVALS N(12, 6) Not Null, ;
			MOEVALS N(12, 6) Not Null, ;
			TPESOS N(12, 2) Not Null, ;
			TOTAS N(12, 2) Not Null, ;
			QTDS N(10, 3) Not Null, ;
			FATORS N(8, 3) Not Null, ;
			FATVALS N(12, 6) Not Null, ;
			DESCVALS N(8, 2) Not Null, ;
			CIDCHAVES C(20) Not Null, ;
			parcelas n(12,2), ;
			Tabds c(10),;
			lPrecos c(30),;
			Compris n(6,2))

		= aFields(v01,'creesti')
		Create Cursor LpItem From Array v01
		Index On LPrecos + Tabds Tag LPreco

		With .Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.Cnt_Preco.Grd_Preco
			.ColumnCount = 5
			.RecordSource = 'LpItem'

			.Column1.ControlSource = 'LpItem.lprecos'
			.Column1.Movable = .F.
			.Column1.Resizable = .F.
			.Column1.ReadOnly = .T.

			.Column2.ControlSource = 'LpItem.tabds'
			.Column2.Movable = .F.
			.Column2.Resizable = .F.
			.Column2.ReadOnly = .T.

			.Column3.ControlSource = 'LpItem.units'
			.Column3.Movable = .F.
			.Column3.Resizable = .F.
			.Column3.ReadOnly = .T.

			.Column4.ControlSource = 'LpItem.moedas'
			.Column4.Movable = .F.
			.Column4.Resizable = .F.
			.Column4.ReadOnly = .T.

			.Column5.ControlSource = 'LpItem.Parcelas'
			.Column5.Movable = .F.
			.Column5.Resizable = .F.
			.Column5.ReadOnly = .T.
		EndWith
		
		.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.pag_etiqueta.grd_historico.SetAll('DynamicForeColor',[Iif(csetiqueta.cps1s=='1',Rgb(255,0,0),Iif(csEtiqueta.cps1s=='2',Rgb(185,185,0),Rgb(0,0,0)))],'Column')
		.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.pag_etiqueta.grd_historico.SetAll('DynamicBackColor',[Iif(InList(csetiqueta.Locals,'OSCLIE','PBAIXA','BAL','SUP','SP1','SP2','SP3','SP4','FAZ','ASS','PDF','CC1'),Rgb(255,0,255),RGB(255,255,255))],'Column')

		Select CrSigCdPam

		ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getFxPreco.Enabled = .f.

		* Inicializa o Cursor no campo correto
		If Empty(.ChamadaExt)
			.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Produto_Inicial.SetFocus
		EndIf 
		
		** Grade com as Características
		lcQuery = [Select 0 as Selec, Codigos From SigCrRap]
		If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,[crSigCrRap]) < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (crSigCrRap)')
			Return(.F.)	
		Endif

		With ThisForm.Fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.grdCaracts
			.ColumnCount       = 2
			.RecordSource      = 'crSigCrRap'
			.DeleteMark        = .f.
			.RecordMark        = .f.
			.ReadOnly          = .f.
			.AllowHeaderSizing = .f.
			.AllowRowSizing    = .f.

			.Column1.ControlSource   = 'crSigCrRap.Selec'
			.Column1.FontName        = 'Courier New'
			.Column1.Movable         = .f.
			.Column1.Resizable       = .f.
			.Column1.ReadOnly        = .f.
			.Column1.Sparse          = .f.
			.Column1.Check1.AutoSize = .t.
		
			.Column2.ControlSource   = 'crSigCrRap.Codigos'
			.Column2.FontName  = 'Courier New'
			.Column2.Movable   = .f.
			.Column2.Resizable = .f.
			.Column2.ReadOnly  = .t.
		EndWith     						

		** Grade com os tipos de operação
		lcQuery = [Select 0 as Selec, Codigos, Descrs from SigCdTom]
		If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcQuery,[crSigCdTom]) < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (crSigCdTom)')
			Return(.F.)	
		Endif
		
		With ThisForm.Fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.grdTOpe
			.ColumnCount       = 3
			.RecordSource      = 'crSigCdTom'
			.DeleteMark        = .f.
			.RecordMark        = .f.
			.ReadOnly          = .f.
			.AllowHeaderSizing = .f.
			.AllowRowSizing    = .f.

			.Column1.ControlSource   = 'crSigCdTom.Selec'
			.Column1.FontName        = 'Courier New'
			.Column1.Movable         = .f.
			.Column1.Resizable       = .f.
			.Column1.ReadOnly        = .f.
			.Column1.Sparse          = .f.
			.Column1.Check1.AutoSize = .t.
		
			.Column2.ControlSource   = 'crSigCdTom.Codigos'
			.Column3.ControlSource   = 'crSigCdTom.Descrs'
		
			For I=2 To .ColumnCount
				lcCol = Alltrim(Str(I))
				.Column&lcCol..FontName  = 'Courier New'
				.Column&lcCol..Movable   = .f.
				.Column&lcCol..Resizable = .f.
				.Column&lcCol..ReadOnly  = .t.
			Next
		EndWith     						
	Else 
		= Messagebox('Impossivel Efetuar Conexão com Servidor de Banco de Dados.',48,'Create Object')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf 

	.LockScreen = .F.

	goSistema.ConsultaAberta = .t.
EndWith
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Pag_Consulta.Activate
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Pag_Consulta.Activate
ThisForm.Caption = ThisForm.cPrompt
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Pag_Resultado.Activate
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Pag_Resultado.Activate
If !ThisForm.AlteraPreco
	If !ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada
		ThisForm.Fld_Principal.ActivePage = 1
		ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Grp_Operacao.Cmd_Consulta.Click
	EndIf
Else
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.custo.cntValor.altpreco.SetFocus
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.ActivePage = 3
	ThisForm.AlteraPreco = .F.
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.custo.Click()
EndIf

If Used('CrProdutos') And !Eof('CrProdutos')
	ThisForm.Caption = 'Consulta do Produto: ' + AllTrim(CrProdutos.CPros) + ' / '  + AllTrim(CrProdutos.DPros)
Else
	ThisForm.Caption = ThisForm.cPrompt
EndIf

ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Enabled = .f.
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_ds_grupo
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 8
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
ThisForm.Antvalue = This.Value
Return(Empty(This.Parent.Get_Cd_Grupo.Value))
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal
	If Empty(This.Value)
		.Get_Cd_Grupo.Value = ''
		.Get_Cd_Grupo.Refresh
	Else
		m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',ThisForm.poDataMgr1.pnIdConn,'SigCdGrp','CrGrupos','DGrus',This.Value,'Grupos',.T.)
		If !(m.loLista.plAchouRegistro)
			m.loLista.mAddColuna('DGrus','','Descrição')
			m.loLista.mAddColuna('CGrus','XXXXXX','Código')
			m.loLista.Show()
		EndIf
		m.loLista = NULL
		If LastKey() <> 27 And Used('CrGrupos')
			.Get_Cd_Grupo.Value = Evaluate('CrGrupos.cgrus')
			.Get_Ds_Grupo.Value = Evaluate('CrGrupos.dgrus')
		Else
			.Get_Cd_Grupo.Value = ''
			.Get_Ds_Grupo.Value = ''
		EndIf
	EndIf
	.Get_Cd_Grupo.Refresh
	.Get_Ds_Grupo.Refresh
EndWith

If Empty(ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_Cd_grupo.Value)
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getFxPreco.Value = []
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getFxPreco.Enabled = .f.
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getFxPreco.Refresh
Else
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getFxPreco.Enabled = .t.
EndIf

Return(.T.)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
This.Parent.Get_cd_sgrupo.Value = Space(6)
This.Parent.Get_ds_sgrupo.Value = Space(20)
This.Parent.Get_cd_sgrupo.Refresh
This.Parent.Get_ds_sgrupo.Refresh
ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada = .F.
ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !IsEmpty(This.value)
	fAcessoContab(Usuar,'D',This.value,This.Parent.get_Grupo,This)
Else
	This.Parent.Get_grupo.value = ''
	This.Parent.Get_grupo.refresh	
	This.parent.Get_Conta.Value = ''
	This.parent.Get_NConta.Value = ''
endif
return(.t.)
ENDPROC

* Registro: 11
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local Grupo, loLista
Grupo = ''
If !Empty(This.Value)
	If !fAcessoContas(Usuar,Grupo,'C',This.Value,This,This.Parent.Get_NConta)
		Messagebox('Acesso Negado !!!',48,'')
		This.Value = ''
		This.Refresh
		This.Parent.Get_NConta.Value = ''
		This.Parent.Get_NConta.Refresh
		Return 0
	Endif
Else
	This.Parent.Get_NConta.Value = ''
	This.Parent.Get_NConta.Refresh
	Return(.T.)
EndIf
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE InteractiveChange

* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.grd_composicao.col_mats.txt_mats

================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return (Empty(CsMatPrima.Cgrus))
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid

Local loLista

If IsEmpty(This.Value)
	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.filtro.Principal.grd_composicao
		.col_dcompos.txt_dcompos.Value = ''
		.Refresh
	EndWith
	Return(.T.)
EndIf

m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',ThisForm.poDataMgr1.pnIdConn,'SigCdPro','CrProds','CPros',This.Value,'Produtos',1000)
If !(m.loLista.plAchouRegistro)
	m.loLista.mAddColuna('cpros','','Código')
	m.loLista.mAddColuna('dpros','','Descrição')
	m.loLista.Show()
EndIf
m.loLista = NULL
If LastKey() <> 27 And Used('CrProds')
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.filtro.Principal.grd_composicao.col_mats.txt_mats.Value       = Evaluate('CrProds.cpros')
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.filtro.Principal.grd_composicao.col_dcompos.txt_dcompos.Value = Evaluate('CrProds.dpros')
Else
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.filtro.Principal.grd_composicao.col_mats.txt_mats.Value       = ''
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.filtro.Principal.grd_composicao.col_dcompos.txt_dcompos.Value = ''
EndIf

Return(.T.)

		ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.filtro.Principal.grd_composicao.col_dcompos.txt_dcompos.Value = ''

lnQtdFinal     = CsMatPrima.qtd_final

	Return(.T.)
Else
	If IsEmpty(lcMateriaPrima) and IsEmpty(lnQtdInicial) and IsEmpty(lnQtdFinal)

	EndIf

	MessageBox('Quantidade final inválida !',16,'')
	Return(.T.)
EndIf

* consiste a diferença entre as quantidades
If lnQtdFinal < lnQtdInicial
	MessageBox('Quantidade final não pode ser menor do que a quantidade inicial !',16,'')
	Return(.T.)
EndIf

	Append Blank
	keyboard "{DNARROW}"

* PROCEDURE KeyPress
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE KeyPress
LParameters nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

If nKeyCode = 13 
	If IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.mats) and IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.tipos) and IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.qtd_inicial) and ;
		IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.qtd_final) And IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.Cgrus)
		ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Grp_Operacao.Cmd_Consulta.SetFocus
		Return(.T.)
	EndIf
EndIf

* em caso de tecla TAB, passar para o próximo objeto
If nKeyCode = 9
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Grp_Operacao.Cmd_Consulta.SetFocus
	Return(.T.)
EndIf

* em caso de tecla SHIFT+TAB, passar para o objeto anterior
If nKeyCode = 15
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Peso_Final.SetFocus
	Return(.T.)
EndIf
	
ENDPROC

ENDPROC

PROCEDURE Valid

If !Empty(This.Value)
	If !fAcessoContas(Usuar,'','D',This.Value,This.Parent.Get_ContaO,This)
		MessageBox('Acesso Negado !!!',48,'')
		This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Value  = ''
		This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Value = ''
	Else
		Wait Window 'Aguarde! Selecionando Movimentações...' NoWait
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdCli],[crTmpCli],[Iclis],This.Parent.get_ContaO.Value,[Cpfs])
		This.Parent.get_CPF.Value = crTmpCli.Cpfs
		Use in crTmpCli
	EndIf
Else
	This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Value  = ''
	This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Value = ''
	This.Parent.Get_CPF.Value = ''
	This.Parent.Get_CPF.Refresh()
EndIf
ENDPROC

	This.Parent.get_Operaçãoi.Enabled = .F. 
	This.Parent.Say_Conta.Visible  = .T.

	This.Parent.OptTpData.Value = 1
Else

	This.Parent.get_Operação.Enabled = .T.
	This.Parent.get_Operaçãoi.Enabled = .T.
	This.Parent.Say_Conta.Visible  = .F.
	This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Visible  = .F.
