================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_ds_sgrupo
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 74
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return(Empty(This.Parent.Get_cd_sgrupo.Value))
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid

Local loLista

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal
	If Empty(This.Value)
		.Get_Cd_SGrupo.Value = ''
		.Get_Cd_SGrupo.Refresh
	Else
		*** Tiago - 03/02/2016 - Incluído para filtrar os subgrupos do grupo informado
		lcCgru = This.Parent.Get_Cd_Grupo.Value
		lcCgru = Iif(!Empty(lcCgru),[Cgrus = '] + lcCgru + ['],[])
	
		m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',ThisForm.poDataMgr1.pnIdConn,'SigCdPsg','CrSubGrupo','Descricaos',This.Value,'Sub-Grupos',.T.,.F.,lcCgru)
		If !(m.loLista.plAchouRegistro)
			m.loLista.mAddColuna('Descricaos','XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX','Descrição')
			m.loLista.mAddColuna('Codigos','XXXXXXXXXX','Sub-Grupo')
			m.loLista.mAddColuna('CGrus','XXXXXXXXXX','Grupo')
			m.loLista.Show()
		EndIf
		m.loLista = NULL
		If LastKey() <> 27 And Used('CrSubGrupo')
			.Get_Cd_SGrupo.Value = Evaluate('CrSubGrupo.codigos')
			.Get_Ds_SGrupo.Value = Evaluate('CrSubGrupo.descricaos')
		Else
			.Get_Cd_SGrupo.Value = ''
			.Get_Ds_SGrupo.Value = ''
		EndIf
	EndIf
	.Get_Cd_SGrupo.Refresh
	.Get_Ds_SGrupo.Refresh
EndWith
Return(.T.)
ENDPROC

	loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigCdCor', ;
						   'crListaRemota', 'Descs', This.Value, 'Seleção', .t.)

	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna('Descs', '', 'Descrição')
		loLista.mAddColuna('Cods',  '', 'Código')
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	loLista = NULL

	loObj.getCor.Value   = crListaRemota.Cods
	loObj.get_DCor.Value = crListaRemota.Descs
	Use In crListaRemota
Else
	loObj.getCor.Value   = ''
	loObj.get_DCor.Value = ''

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getFxPreco
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 91
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return Not Empty(ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_cd_grupo.Value)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcGrp, loBrow, lcQuery

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	lcGrp = Padr(ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_cd_grupo.Value, 10)

	lcQuery = [Select b.Descrs, b.FaixaIs, b.FaixaFs ] + ;
			    [From SigCdPpc a, SigCdPpi b ] + ;
			   [Where a.Codigos = '] + lcGrp + [' And ] + ;
			   		 [a.cIdChaves = b.FkChaves]

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalProp') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalProp)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Select LocalProp
	Index On Descrs Tag Descrs
	Go Top In LocalProp
	Locate For Descrs = This.Value
	If Eof([LocalProp])
		loBrow = CreateObject([fwbuscaint], [LocalProp], [Descrs], This.Value, [Faixas de Preço], .t.)
		loBrow.mAddColuna([Descrs],  [], [Faixas], .t.)
		loBrow.mAddColuna([FaixaIs], [999999999.99], [Inicial])
		loBrow.mAddColuna([FaixaFs], [999999999.99], [Final])
		loBrow.Show		
		loBrow = NULL
		This.Value = Iif(Lastkey()=27, [], LocalProp.Descrs)
	EndIf
	Use In LocalProp
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada = .F.
ENDPROC


================================================================================

	loLista = CreateObject([fwbuscaext], ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, [SigCdFip], ;
						   [crListaRemota], [Cods], This.Value, [Seleção], .t.)

	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna([Cods],  [], [Código])
		loLista.mAddColuna([Descs], [], [Descrição])
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	loLista = NULL
	
	loObj.getCodFinPF.Value = crListaRemota.Cods
	Use In crListaRemota
EndIf
loObj.getCodFinPF.Refresh 
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada = .F.
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_CPF
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 97
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return(Empty(This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Value) And Empty(This.Parent.Get_operaçãoi.Value))
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(This.Value)
	lnVerCPFCGC = 0
	lcCGC = This.Value
	lcCGC = StrTran(StrTran(StrTran(lcCGC,'.',''),'-',''),'/','')
	If Len(Alltrim(lcCGC)) <> 14
		lcCGC1 = TransForm(lcCGC,'@R 999.999.999-99')
		If Len(Alltrim(lcCGC)) = 11
			lnVerCPFCGC = Iif(fValidarCPF(lcCGC1),1,2)
		EndIf
	Else
		lcCGC1 = TransForm(lcCGC,'@R 99.999.999/9999-99')
		lnVerCPFCGC = Iif(fValidarCNPJ(lcCGC1),1,2)
	EndIf
	
	If lnVerCPFCGC = 2
		MessageBox('CPF / CGC Incorreto !!!',48,'')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	If !(ThisForm.poDataMgr1.CursorQuery('SigCdCli',,'CPFs',Padr(Alltrim(lcCGC1),20)))
		If lnVerCPFCGC = 1
			MessageBox('CPF / CGC não encontrado !!!',48,'')
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf
	
	lcIclis = CqSigCdCli.IClis
	If !fAcessoContas(Usuar,'','C',lcIClis,This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Value,This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Value)
		Messagebox('Acesso Negado !!!',48,'')
		This.Value                   = ''
		This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Value  = ''
		This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Value = ''
		This.Refresh
		This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Refresh
		This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Refresh
		Return 0
	Else
		Select CqSigCdCli
		This.Value                   = lcCGC1
		This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Value  = CqSigCdCli.IClis
		This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Value = CqSigCdCli.RClis
		This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Valid()
	EndIf
Else
	This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Value  = ''
	This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Value = ''
EndIf
This.Refresh
This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Refresh
This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Refresh
Return(.T.)
ENDPROC


================================================================================

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Barra
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 100
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If IsEmpty(This.Value)
	Return(.T.)
EndIf

lnCBars = This.Value
lcCBars = Alltrim(Str(lnCBars,14))
llBarra = .T.

For lnCnt = 1 To Len(lcCBars)
	If !IsDigit(Substr(lcCBars,lnCnt,1))
		llBarra = .F.
		Exit
	EndIf
Next

lnDig = crSigCdPam.TpCbars 

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal
	If llBarra
		llDig = .T.
		Do Case
			Case (lnDig = 1)
				llDig = (Len(lcCBars) = 8)
			Case (lnDig = 2)
				llDig = InList(Len(lcCBars), 6, 8)
			Case (lnDig = 3)
				llDig = InList(Len(lcCBars), 6, 7, 8)
			Case (lnDig = 4) And InList(Len(lcCBars),8,9,10)
				llDig = .T.
			Case (lnDig = 4)
				llDig = (Len(lcCBars) = 14)
			Case (lnDig = 5) 
				llDig = InList(Len(lcCBars), 4, 5, 6, 7, 8)
		EndCase
		
		If llDig
			lStrQuery = [Select a.CPros,a.CBars ] + ;
					[From SigOpEtq a ] + ;
					[left join sigopeta b on a.cpros = b.cpros and a.cbars = b.cbars_new ] + ;
					[Where (a.CBars = ]+lcCBars+[ or b.cbars_old = ]+lcCBars+[) ]
			If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lStrQuery,'CrEtiqs') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrEtiqs)')
				Return(.T.)
			EndIf
			Select CrEtiqs
			Go Top
			If !Eof()
				If LastKey() <> 27
					.Get_Barra.Value           = Evaluate('CrEtiqs.CBars')
					.Get_Produto_Inicial.Value = Evaluate('CrEtiqs.CPros')
					.Get_Produto_Final.Value   = Evaluate('CrEtiqs.CPros')
				Else
					.Get_Barra.Value = 0
				EndIf
			Else
				MessageBox('A Etiqueta de Produto Não Foi Encontrada!!!',48,'Etiqueta de ' + Alltrim(Str(lnDig, 10)) + ' dígitos!')
				This.Value = 0
				This.Refresh
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
		Else
			If Len(lcCBars) = 13
				lStrQuery = [Select a.CPros,a.EAN13 From SigCdPro a Where a.EAN13 = ]+lcCBars+[ ]
				If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lStrQuery,'CrProEAN') < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrProEAN)')
					Return(.T.)
				EndIf
				Select CrProEAN
				Go Top
				If !Eof()
					If LastKey() <> 27
						.Get_Barra.Value           = Evaluate('CrProEAN.EAN13')
						.Get_Produto_Inicial.Value = Evaluate('CrProEAN.CPros')
						.Get_Produto_Final.Value   = Evaluate('CrProEAN.CPros')
					Else
						.Get_Barra.Value = 0
					EndIf
				Else
					MessageBox('O Código EAN13 de Produto Não Foi Encontrado!!!',48,'Código EAN13')
					This.Value = 0
					This.Refresh
					Return(.F.)
				EndIf
			Else
				If (Len(lcCBars) <= 14)
					lStrQuery = [Select a.CPros,a.CBars From SigCdPro a Where a.CBars = ]+lcCBars+[ ]
					If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lStrQuery,'CrEtiPro') < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrEtiPro)')
						Return(.T.)
					EndIf
					Select CrEtiPro
					Go Top
					If !Eof()
						If LastKey() <> 27
							.Get_Barra.Value           = Evaluate('CrEtiPro.CBars')
							.Get_Produto_Inicial.Value = Evaluate('CrEtiPro.CPros')
							.Get_Produto_Final.Value   = Evaluate('CrEtiPro.CPros')
						Else
							.Get_Barra.Value = 0
						EndIf
					Else
						MessageBox('O Código de Barras do Produto Não Foi Encontrado!!!',48,'Código de Produto')
						This.Value = 0
						This.Refresh
						Return(.F.)
					EndIf
				Else
					MessageBox('O Código de Barras Está Fora do Padrão!!!',48,'Configuração com 13 ou ' + Alltrim(Str(lnDig, 10)) + ' dígitos!')
					This.Value = 0
					This.Refresh
					Return(.F.)
				Endif
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Else
		MessageBox('Código de Barra Inválido e/ou Não Numérico!!!',48,'Barra Contendo Caracteres Estranhos')
		This.Value = 0
		This.Refresh
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf
	.Get_Barra.Refresh
	.Get_Produto_Inicial.Refresh
	.Get_Produto_Final.Refresh
EndWith

Return(.T.)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada = .F.
ENDPROC

================================================================================

* Registro: 113

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	lcQuery = [Select LPrecos, Formulas, Emps ] + ;
				[From SigCdLpc a ] + ;
			   [Where Emps In ('   ', '] + _Empr + [') And ] + ;
			   		 [Tipos = ' ']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crSigCdLpc') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crSigCdLpc)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Select crSigCdLpc
	Index On LPrecos Tag LPrecos

	If Not Seek(This.Value, 'crSigCdLpc', 'LPrecos')
		xBrow = CreateObject('fwbuscasel', 'crSigCdLpc', 'LPrecos', This.Value, 'Listas de Preços')
		xBrow.AddColuna('LPrecos',  '', 'Lista', .t.)
		xBrow.AddColuna('Formulas', '', 'Fórmula')
		xBrow.AddColuna('Emps',     '', 'Emps')
		xBrow.Show
	EndIf
	This.Value = crSigCdLpc.LPrecos
EndIf
This.Refresh
* Fim *********************************************************************************

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Commandgroup1
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 139
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Command1.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Command1.Click
Wait Window 'Aguarde! Selecionando Movimentações...' NoWait
lcDopes = This.Parent.Parent.Get_Operação.Value
lcTpOpe = ThisForm.MontaStrTpOpe()
llDtIni = fDtoSQL(This.Parent.Parent.Get_Dt_inicial.Value)
llDtFim = fDtoSQL(This.Parent.Parent.Get_Dt_final.Value, [23:59:59])
lnTpData = This.Parent.Parent.OptTpData.Value
lcCliente= This.Parent.Parent.get_contaO.Value

lcSql = [Select Caixas, copers, opers From SigCdOpe Where Dopes=']+lcdopes+[']
If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(lcSql,'TmpOpe') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (TmpOpe)')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf
lnTipo = 2
If (TmpOpe.Caixas = 1)
	If (TmpOpe.COpers = 1)
		lnTipo = 2
	Else
		lnTipo = 1
	EndIf
Else
	If (TmpOpe.Opers = 1)
		lnTipo = 1
	Else
		lnTipo = 2
	EndIf
EndIf

lStrQuery = [Select a.Datas,a.Emps,a.Dopes,a.Numes,a.PrazoEnts,c.Rclis,c.conjuges, ]+;
			Iif(lnTipo=1,[a.ContaOs as Contas ],[a.ContaDs as Contas ])+;
			[From SigMvCab a, SigCdCli c, SigCdOpe b ]+;
			[Where 0=0 ] + ;
			Iif(not Empty(lcDopes),[ And A.Dopes = ']+lcDopes+[' ],[])+;
			Iif( not Empty(This.Parent.Parent.get_Dt_Inicial.Value),;
			Iif(lnTpData = 1,[And A.Datas Between ?llDtINi And ?llDtFim And ],[And A.PrazoEnts Between ?llDtINi And ?llDtFim And ]),;
			[ And ])+;
			Iif(Empty(ThisForm.EmpOperas), [], [ a.Emps = '] + ThisForm.EmpOperas + [' And ]) + ;
			[ b.Dopes = a.Dopes ] + Iif(Empty(lcTpOpe),[],[And b.TipoOps in ]+lcTpOpe+[ ]) + ;
			[ And Case When b.Opers = 1 then a.ContaOs Else a.ContaDs end = c.Iclis ]+;
			Iif(not Empty(lcCliente),[ And Case When b.Opers = 1 then a.ContaOs Else a.ContaDs end = ']+lcCliente+[' ],[]) + ;
			[Order By a.Emps,a.Dopes,a.Numes ]
If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(lStrQuery,'TprMvCab') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (TprMvCab)')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf
Wait Clear

lcCap = [Prev.Entrega]
If Not Empty(lcDopes)
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigOpCdc', 'LocalDcOpe', 'Dopes', Padr(lcDopes, 20), 'DckPrzE, DocPrzE')
	lcCap = Iif(LocalDcOpe.DckPrzE = 1 And Not Empty(LocalDcOpe.DocPrzE), Alltrim(LocalDcOpe.DocPrzE), lcCap)
EndIf

lcQuery = [Select Distinct Emps,Datas,Dopes,Numes,Nvl(PrazoEnts,Ctod('')) As PrazoEnts,Contas,RClis,Conjuges ] + ;
		  	[From TprMvCab Order By 1,]+Iif(lnTpData = 1,[2],[5])+[,3,4 Into Cursor CrTprMvCab]

Select TprMvCab
Go Top

&lcQuery

Select CrTprMvCab
Go Top
Do Form SigPrEop with ThisForm, lcCap

If Reccount([crFilOper]) = 1
	This.Parent.Parent.Get_Operação.Value  = Substr(crFilOper.EmpDopNums,4,20)
	This.Parent.Parent.Get_Operaçãoi.Value = Val(Substr(crFilOper.EmpDopNums,24,6))
	This.Parent.Parent.Get_Operação.Refresh
	This.Parent.Parent.Get_Operaçãoi.Refresh
EndIf

ENDPROC

	This.Parent.Get_Dt_inicial.Value = {}

	This.Parent.Get_nContaO.Value = Space(40)

================================================================================

* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 143
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local llSelTpOpe
Select crSigCdTom
Locate for Selec = 1
llSelTpOpe = not Eof([crSigCdTom])

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	=fAcessoMovmto(Usuar, This.Value, This)
EndIf
This.Refresh
If (Empty(This.Value) Or !Empty(This.Parent.Get_Operaçãoi.Value)) and not llSelTpOpe
	This.Parent.Say_Conta.Visible  = .F.
	This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Visible  = .F.
	This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Visible = .F.
	This.Parent.Get_CPF.Visible    = .F.
	This.Parent.cmdConjuge.Visible = .F.
	This.Parent.Ck_MarcaTOpe.Enabled = .T.
	This.Parent.grdTOpe.Enabled = .T.  
	This.Parent.lbl_Periodo.Caption = [Dt Operação :]
	This.Parent.OptTpData.Option2.Visible = .F. 
	This.Parent.OptTpData.Value = 1
Else
	This.Parent.Ck_MarcaTOpe.Enabled = .F.
	This.Parent.grdTOpe.Enabled = .F.  
	This.Parent.Say_Conta.Visible  = .T.
	This.Parent.Get_ContaO.Visible  = .T.
	This.Parent.Get_NContaO.Visible = .T.
	This.Parent.Get_CPF.Visible    = .T.
	This.Parent.cmdConjuge.Visible = fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP', 'CONJUGE')
	This.Parent.OptTpData.Option2.Visible = .T. 
EndIf

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigOpCdc', 'LocalDcOpe', 'Dopes', Padr(This.Value, 20), 'DckPrzE, DocPrzE')
	lcCap = Iif(LocalDcOpe.DckPrzE = 1 And Not Empty(LocalDcOpe.DocPrzE), Alltrim(LocalDcOpe.DocPrzE) + [ :], 'Dt Operação :')
	ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.lbl_periodo.Caption = lcCap
	This.Parent.OptTpData.Option2.Visible = .T. 
	This.Parent.OptTpData.Option2.Caption = Left(lcCap,Len(lcCap)-2)
	This.Parent.OptTpData.Value = 2
EndIf

Return(.T.)
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getDCategoria
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 159
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada = .F.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigCdCtg', ;
						   'CrListaRemota', 'Descs', Alltrim(This.Value), 'Seleção', .t.)

	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna('Descs',    '', 'Descrição')
		loLista.mAddColuna('Cods', '', 'Código')
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	
	If (Lastkey() <> 27)
		This.Parent.getCategoria.Value  = CrListaRemota.Cods
		This.Parent.getDCategoria.Value = CrListaRemota.Descs
	Else
		This.Parent.getCategoria.Value  = ''
		This.Parent.getDCategoria.Value = ''
	EndIf
	Use In CrListaRemota
Else
	This.Parent.getCategoria.Value  = ''
	This.Parent.getDCategoria.Value = ''
EndIf
This.Parent.getCategoria.Refresh
This.Parent.getDCategoria.Refresh
* Fim ****************************************************************************

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return Empty(ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getCategoria.Value)
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.grp_operacao
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 173
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmd_consulta.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmd_consulta.Click
Private ldData

Local Array laFieldMoe1(1,6)
Local lnCofirma, llcusto, lnImagem
Local lcProI, lcProF, lcDesc, lcCGrp, lcDsGrupo, lcCdSGrupo, lcDsSGrupo 
Local lcCdFornec, lcDsFornec, lcRefI, lcGdeGru, lnTpReffs
Local lnCustoInicial, lnCustoFinal, lcMoeCusto
Local lnVendaInicial, lnVendaFinal, lcMoeVenda
Local lnEstimadoInicial, lnEstimaF, lcMoeEstima, lnPesoInicial, lnPesoFinal,lcMcrsql1,lnErro1,lcStrChave1
Local lcStrFltMoeda1, lcSqlPro1, lcSqlPro2, llGenData1, lcIdQuery1, lcLPrecos, lcIdTab1, lcWhere1, ;
		lcWhereFpro1, lcWhereFpro2, lcWhereFproTB1, lcWhereFproTB2

ThisForm.lPrimeira = .F.
m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.pnIdConn

If !ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada

	** Operações Pré-Selecionadas
	Local llSelTpOpe
	Select crSigCdTom
	Locate for Selec = 1
	llSelTpOpe = not Eof([crSigCdTom])
	If Empty(ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Operaçãoi.Value) and Reccount([crFilOper]) <= 0 and (llSelTpOpe or not Empty(ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Operação.Value)) and ;
		( not Empty(ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Dt_inicial.Value) or not Empty(ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_ContaO.Value) )
		lcDopes = ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Operação.Value
		lcTpOpe = ThisForm.MontaStrTpOpe()
		llDtIni = fDtoSQL(ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Dt_inicial.Value)
		llDtFim = fDtoSQL(ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Dt_final.Value, [23:59:59])
		lnTpData = ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.OptTpData.Value
		lcCliente= ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_contaO.Value

		lcSql = [Select Caixas, copers, opers From SigCdOpe Where Dopes=']+lcdopes+[']
		If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(lcSql,'TmpOpe') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (TmpOpe)')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		lnTipo = 2
		If (TmpOpe.Caixas = 1)
			If (TmpOpe.COpers = 1)
				lnTipo = 2
			Else
				lnTipo = 1
			EndIf
		Else
			If (TmpOpe.Opers = 1)
				lnTipo = 1
			Else
				lnTipo = 2
			EndIf
		EndIf

		lStrQuery = [Select distinct(a.empdopnums) as empdopnums ]+;
					[From SigMvCab a, SigCdCli c, SigCdOpe b ]+;
					[Where 0=0 ] + ;
					Iif(not Empty(lcDopes),[ And A.Dopes = ']+lcDopes+[' ],[])+;
					Iif( not Empty(ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Dt_inicial.Value),;
					Iif(lnTpData = 1,[And A.Datas Between ?llDtINi And ?llDtFim And ],[And A.PrazoEnts Between ?llDtINi And ?llDtFim And ]),;
					[ And ])+;
					Iif(Empty(ThisForm.EmpOperas), [], [ a.Emps = '] + ThisForm.EmpOperas + [' And ]) + ;
					[ b.Dopes = a.Dopes ] + Iif(Empty(lcTpOpe),[],[And b.TipoOps in ]+lcTpOpe+[ ]) + ;
					[ And Case When b.Opers = 1 then a.ContaOs Else a.ContaDs end = c.Iclis ]+;
					Iif(not Empty(lcCliente),[ And Case When b.Opers = 1 then a.ContaOs Else a.ContaDs end = ']+lcCliente+[' ],[]) + ;
					[Group By a.EmpDopNums]
		If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(lStrQuery,'TprMvCab') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (TprMvCab)')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		Select TprMvCab
		Go Top in TprMvCab
		Scan
			Insert into crFilOper Values(TprMvCab.EmpDopNums)
		EndScan		
		
		If Reccount([crFilOper]) <= 0
			MessageBox('Nenhum produto foi encontrado com as características digitadas !',64,'')
			ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Produto_Inicial.SetFocus
			Return
		Endif
	Endif
	**

	Select ('CrSigCdPam')
	Locate 

	Select CsMatPrima
	Scan
		If IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.mats) And IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.Cgrus) And IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.Tipos)
			Delete
		EndIf
	EndScan
	If Used('CrSigMvHst')
		This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.grd_historico.RecordSource = ''
		Use In CrSigMvHst
	EndIf
	If Used('CsFase')
		This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.GradFase.RecordSource = ''
		This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Get_Obs.ControlSource = ''
		Use in CsFase
	EndIf
	If Used('CrProdutos')
		This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.grd_produtos.RecordSource = ''
		Use In CrProdutos
	EndIf

	* Inicializa variáveis de campos
	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro
		With .Principal
			lcProI            = .Get_produto_inicial.Value
			lcProF            = .Get_produto_final.Value
			lcDesc            = Alltrim(.Get_descricao.Value)
			lcDesc2           = Alltrim(.Get_descritivo.Value)
			lcCGrp            = .Get_cd_grupo.Value
			lcDsGrupo         = .Get_ds_grupo.Value
			lcCdSGrupo        = .Get_cd_sgrupo.Value
			lcDsSGrupo        = .Get_ds_sgrupo.Value
			lnPesoInicial     = .Get_peso_inicial.Value
			lnPesoFinal       = .Get_peso_final.Value
			lnOptSituas       = Iif(.cmbSituas.Value = [Ativos  ], 1, Iif(.cmbSituas.Value = [Inativos], 2, 3))
			lcLinhaInicial    = .GetLin.Value
			lcLinhaFinal      = .GetLin2.Value
			lcGvenInicial     = .GetCol.Value
			lcGvenFinal       = .GetCol1.Value
			lcGdeGru          = .Get_gde.Value
			lnImagem		  = Iif(.cmbImagem.Value = [c/Imagem], 1, Iif(.cmbImagem.Value = [s/Imagem], 2, 3))
			lcCor			  = .getCor.Value
			lcTam			  = .getTam.Value
			lcCategoria		  = .getCategoria.Value
			lcLocals		  = .GetLocals.Value
			lcEqv			  = .getEquivalente.Value
			lcFinI			  = Padr(.getCodFinP.Value,  3)
			lcFinF			  = Padr(.getCodFinPF.Value, 3)
			lccClass		  = .Get_cClass.Value
			lnWebs			  = .optWebs.Value
			lnTpReffs		  = .Opt_TpReffs.Value
			lcRefI            = .Get_ref_fornecedor.Value
			lcRefI            = IIf(lnTpReffs = 1, Upper(lcRefI), lcRefI)
			lcRefF            = IIf(lnTpReffs = 3, lcRefI, .Get_ReffsF.Value)
			lcLPrecos         = .Get_Lista.Value
			lcPromo			  = .Get_promo.Value
			lnVendaInicial    = .Get_venda_inicial.Value
			lnVendaFinal      = .Get_venda_final.Value
			lcMoeVenda        = .Get_moe_venda.Value
			lcCFor            = .Get_cd_fornecedor.Value
			lcDsFornecedor    = .Get_ds_fornecedor.Value
			lnCustoInicial    = .Get_custo_inicial.Value
			lnCustoFinal      = .Get_custo_final.Value
			lcMoeCusto        = .Get_moe_custo.Value
			lnEstimadoInicial = .Get_estimado_inicial.Value
			lnEstimaF         = .Get_estimado_final.Value
			lcMoeEstima       = .Get_moe_estimado.Value
			lcEmpEst          = .getEmps.Value
			lcGruEst		  = .get_Grupo.Value
			lcContaEst		  = .get_Conta.Value
			lcCompl			  = ALLTRIM(.get_Dcompl.Value)
			lcOperacao        = .Get_Operação.Value
			lnOperacao        = .Get_OperaçãoI.Value
			lcOperai          = Str(lnOperacao,6)
			lnOptOper         = .OptOperacao.Value
			lnOptPag		  = .OptPagamento.Value
			lcTamA 			  = Val(.get_tamas.Value)
			lcTamP 			  = Val(.get_tamps.Value)
			lcTamC 			  = Val(.get_tamcs.Value)			
			ldDtAltI 		  = .getDtAltI.Value 
			ldDtAltF 		  = .getDtAltF.Value 
			lnTpDtCad		  = ThisForm.Fld_principal.PAg_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.OptTpDtCad.Value	&& mena 18/12/2015 incluida tipo de data a pesquisar
			lcConjunto		  = .getConjunto.value && Tiago - 26/01/2016 - Rosana Jóias - Filtro por conjunto
			lcCodMacro		  = .getCodMacro.value 
			lnProdOff		  = .chkOff.Value
		EndWith
		
		* Checagem da Faixa de Venda da Primeira Página Para Avaliar se É mais Ampla do Que as Demais
		If Not Empty(ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getFxPreco.Value)
		
			lcGrp = Padr(ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_cd_grupo.Value, 10)
			lcFxa = Padr(ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.getFxPreco.Value, 15)

			lcQuery = [Select b.Descrs, b.FaixaIs, b.FaixaFs ] + ;
					    [From SigCdPpc a, SigCdPpi b ] + ;
					   [Where a.Codigos = '] + lcGrp + [' And ] + ;
					   		 [a.cIdChaves = b.FkChaves And ] + ;
					   		 [b.Descrs = '] + lcFxa + [']

			If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalProp') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalProp)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Select LocalProp
			Go Top In LocalProp
			If Not Eof([LocalProp])
				lnVendaInicial = Iif(LocalProp.FaixaIs < lnVendaInicial Or Empty(lnVendaInicial), LocalProp.FaixaIs, lnVendaInicial)
				lnVendaFinal   = Iif(LocalProp.FaixaFs > lnVendaFinal Or Empty(lnVendaFinal), LocalProp.FaixaFs, lnVendaFinal)
			EndIf 
		EndIf

		* Consiste o campo produto
		If Not Empty(lcProI) And Empty(lcProF)
			=MessageBox([Referência Final do Produto Inválida!!!], 16, [Atenção!!!])
			.Principal.Get_Produto_Final.SetFocus
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		If Not Empty(lcProF) And Empty(lcProI)
			=MessageBox([Referência Inicial do Produto Inválida!!!], 16, [Atenção!!!])
			.Principal.Get_Produto_Inicial.SetFocus
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		If Not Empty(lcProI) And Not Empty(lcProF) And (lcProI > lcProF)
			=MessageBox([Referência Inicial do Produto Maior Que a Final!!!], 16, [Atenção!!!])
			.Principal.Get_Produto_Inicial.SetFocus
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		* Consiste o campo preço de venda
		If Not IsEmpty(lnVendaInicial) Or Not IsEmpty(lnVendaFinal)
			If lnVendaFinal < lnVendaInicial
				MessageBox('Preço de venda inválido !',16,'')
				.Principal.Get_Venda_Inicial.SetFocus
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
		EndIf

		* Consiste o campo preço de custo
		If Not IsEmpty(lnCustoInicial) Or Not IsEmpty(lnCustoFinal)
			If lnCustoFinal < lnCustoInicial
				MessageBox('Preço de custo inválido !',16,'')
				.Principal.Get_Custo_Inicial.SetFocus
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
		EndIf

		* Consiste o campo valor estimado
		If Not IsEmpty(lnEstimadoInicial) Or Not IsEmpty(lnEstimaF)
			If lnEstimaF < lnEstimadoInicial
				MessageBox('Valor estimado inválido !',16,'')
				.Principal.Get_Estimado_Inicial.SetFocus
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
		EndIf

		* Consiste o campo peso
		If Not IsEmpty(lnPesoInicial) Or Not IsEmpty(lnPesoFinal)
			If lnPesoFinal < lnPesoInicial
				MessageBox('Peso inválido !',16,'')
				.Principal.Get_Peso_Inicial.SetFocus
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
		EndIf

		* Consiste o campo linha
		If Not IsEmpty(lcLinhaInicial) Or Not IsEmpty(lcLinhaFinal)
			If lcLinhaFinal < lcLinhaInicial
				MessageBox('Linha de produto inválida !',16,'')
				.Principal.GetLin.SetFocus
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
		EndIf

		* Consiste o Campo de Finalidade
		If (Not Empty(lcFinI) Or Not Empty(lcFinF)) And (lcFinF < lcFinI)
			=MessageBox([Modelo de Produto Inválido!!!], 16, [Atenção!!!])
			.Principal.getCodFinP.SetFocus
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		
		* Consiste as Datas de Alteração de Produtos
		If (Not Empty(ldDtAltI) Or Not Empty(ldDtAltF))
			If (ldDtAltI > ldDtAltF)
				=MessageBox([A Data de Alteração Inicial Não Pode Ser Maior Que a Final!!!], 16, [Atenção!!!])
				.Principal.getDtaltI.SetFocus
				Return .f.
			EndIf

			If Empty(ldDtAltF) And Not Empty(ldDtAltI)
				ldDtAltF = ldDtAltI
				.Principal.getDtaltF.Value = ldDtAltF
			EndIf

			If Not Empty(ldDtAltF) And Empty(ldDtAltI)
				ldDtAltI = ldDtAltF
				.Principal.getDtaltI.Value = ldDtAltI
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndWith

	* Verifica a existência de composição de produto
	llcusto = .F.
	Select CsMatPrima
	Scan
		If Not IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.mats) Or Not IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.Cgrus) Or Not IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.Tipos)
			llcusto = .T.
			Exit
		Endif
	EndScan

	If Empty(lcDesc) And Empty(lcProI) And Empty(lcProF) And Empty(lcCGrp) And Empty(lcDsGrupo) And Empty(lcCdSGrupo) And Empty(lcCFor) ;
		And Empty(lcRefI) And Empty(lcRefF) And (lnCustoInicial = 0) And (lnCustoFinal = 0) And Empty(lcMoeCusto) ;
		And (lnVendaInicial = 0) And (lnVendaFinal = 0) And Empty(lcMoeVenda) ;
		And (lnEstimadoInicial = 0) And (lnEstimaF = 0) And Empty(lcMoeEstima) ;
		And (lnPesoInicial = 0) And (lnPesoFinal = 0) And Empty(lcLinhaInicial) And Empty(lcLinhaFinal) ;
		And Empty(lcGVenInicial) And Empty(lcGVenFinal) And Empty(ldDtAltI) And Empty(ldDtAltF) ;
		And Empty(lcPromo) And Empty(lcOperacao) And (lnOperacao = 0) And Empty(lcGdeGru) And (!llcusto) ;
		And Empty(lcCor) And Empty(lcTam) And Empty(lcLPrecos) And Empty(lcEqv) And Empty(lcDesc2) And Empty(lcCategoria) ;
		And Empty(lcFinI) And Empty(lcFinF) And Empty(lccClass) and Empty(lcEmpEst) and Empty(lcGruEst) and Empty(lcContaEst) ;
		And Empty(lcCompl) And Empty(lcTamP) And Empty(lcTamA) And Empty(lcTamC) And Empty(lcLocals) And (lnWebs = 5) And ;
		Empty(lcConjunto) And Empty(lcCodMacro) And lnProdOff = 0 And Reccount([crFilOper]) = 0
		
		=MessageBox('Nenhum critério de pesquisa informado !!!',48,'Parâmetros Inválidos')
		
		This.Parent.Parent.Filtro.Principal.Get_Produto_Inicial.SetFocus
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf

	ThisForm.Draw

	************************************************************************************************
	* Iniciando a Montagem do Filtro da Select do cadastro de Produtos (SigCdPro)

	lcProI = IIf(IsEmpty(lcProI),Space(10),lcProI)
	lcProF = IIf(IsEmpty(lcProF),Replicate(Chr(254),10),lcProF)

	lcWhereFpro1 = []
	lcWhereFpro2 = []
	lcWhereFproTB1 = []
	lcWhereFproTB2 = []

	lcWhere1 = [a.cpros Between ']+lcProI+[' And ']+lcProF+[' ]

	lcDriver = Thisform.poDataMgr1.GetDriver()

	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(lnImagem = 1, [ And Not a.FigJpgs Is Null ],;
			IIf(lnImagem = 2, [ And a.FigJpgs Is Null ],''))
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(!IsEmpty(lcDesc), [And a.dpros Like '%]+AllTrim(lcDesc)+[%' ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(!IsEmpty(lcDesc2), [And a.dpro2s Like '%]+AllTrim(lcDesc2)+[%' ],'')

	If (!IsEmpty(lcCFor)) And !IsEmpty(lcCGrp) And ;
	   ((lnTpReffs = 2) And (!IsEmpty(lcRefI) And ;
	    !IsEmpty(lcRefF)  And (lcRefI = lcRefF))) 

		lcWhereFpro1 = [And a.ifors = '] + lcCFor + [' And a.reffs = '] + lcRefI + [' And a.cgrus = '] + lcCGrp + [' ] + ;
							[And a.cpros Not in (Select xa.Cpros From SigPrFnc xa Where xa.Ifors = '] + lcCfor + [' And xa.Reffs = '] + lcRefI + [') ]
		lcWhereFproTB1 = [And a.cpros in (Select xa.Cpros From SigPrFnc xa Where xa.Ifors = '] + lcCfor + [' And xa.Reffs = '] + lcRefI + [') ]

	Else
		If !IsEmpty(lcCFor)
			lcWhereFpro1 = [And a.ifors = ']+lcCFor+[' ] + ;
							[And a.cpros Not in (Select xa.Cpros From SigPrFnc xa Where xa.Ifors = '] + lcCfor + [') ]
			lcWhereFproTB1 = [And a.cpros in (Select xa.Cpros From SigPrFnc xa Where xa.Ifors = '] + lcCfor + [') ]

		EndIf 

		If (lnTpReffs = 1)
			If !IsEmpty(lcRefI)
				lcWhereFpro2 = [And Upper(a.reffs) Like '%] + AllTrim(lcRefI) + [%' ] + ;
								[And a.Cpros Not in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Upper(reffs) Like '%]+AllTrim(lcRefI)+[%') ]
				lcWhereFproTB2 = [And a.Cpros in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Upper(reffs) Like '%]+AllTrim(lcRefI)+[%') ]
				
			EndIf 

		Else
			Do Case
				Case !IsEmpty(lcRefI) And !IsEmpty(lcRefF) And (lcRefI = lcRefF)
					lcWhereFpro2 = [And a.Reffs = '] + lcRefI + [' ] + ;
									[And a.cpros Not in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Reffs = '] + lcRefI + [') ]						
					lcWhereFproTB2 = [And a.cpros in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Reffs = '] + lcRefI + [') ]

				Case !IsEmpty(lcRefI) And !IsEmpty(lcRefF)
					lcWhereFpro2 = [And a.Reffs Between '] + lcRefI + [' And '] + lcRefF + [' ] + ;
									[And a.cpros Not in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Reffs Between '] + lcRefI + [' And '] + lcRefF + [') ]
					lcWhereFproTB2 = [And a.cpros in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Reffs Between '] + lcRefI + [' And '] + lcRefF + [') ]

				Case  IsEmpty(lcRefI) And !IsEmpty(lcRefF)
					lcWhereFpro2 = [And a.Reffs <= '] + lcRefF + [' ] + ;
									[And a.cpros Not in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Reffs <= '] + lcRefF + [') ]
					lcWhereFproTB2 = [And a.cpros in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Reffs <= '] + lcRefF + [') ]

				Case !IsEmpty(lcRefI) And  IsEmpty(lcRefF)
					lcWhereFpro2 = [And a.Reffs >= '] + lcRefI + [' ] + ;
									[And a.cpros Not in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Reffs >= '] + lcRefI + [') ]
					lcWhereFproTB2 = [And a.cpros in (Select Cpros From SigPrFnc Where Reffs >= '] + lcRefI + [') ]
			EndCase
		EndIf
		lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(!IsEmpty(lcCGrp), [ And a.cgrus = ']+lcCGrp+[' ],'')
	EndIf

	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcCdSGrupo)  ,[And a.sgrus = ']+lcCdSGrupo+[' ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcGdeGru)    ,[And a.mercs = ']+lcGdeGru+[' ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcMoeCusto)  ,[And a.moecusfs = ']+lcMoeCusto+[' ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcMoeVenda)  ,[And a.moevs = ']+lcMoeVenda+[' ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcMoeEstima) ,[And a.moedas = ']+lcMoeEstima+[' ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcLinhaFinal),[And a.linhas between ']+lclinhainicial+[' And ']+lclinhafinal+[' ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcGvenFinal) ,[And a.colecoes between ']+lcGvenInicial+[' And ']+lcGvenFinal+[' ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lnCustoFinal),[And a.custofs between ]+AllTrim(Str(lnCustoInicial,12,2))+[ And ]+AllTrim(Str(lnCustoFinal,12,2))+[ ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lnVendaFinal),[And a.pvens between ]+AllTrim(Str(lnVendaInicial,12,2))+[ And ]+AllTrim(Str(lnVendaFinal,12,2))+[ ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lnEstimaF)   ,[And a.valors between ]+AllTrim(Str(lnEstimadoInicial,12,2))+[ And ]+AllTrim(Str(lnEstimaF,12,2))+[ ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lnPesoFinal) ,[And a.pesoms between ]+AllTrim(Str(lnPesoInicial,9,2))+[ And ]+AllTrim(Str(lnPesoFinal,9,2))+[ ],'')
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(lnOptSituas = 3, '',IIf(lnOptSituas = 2, [And a.situas = 2 ],   [And (a.situas = 1 Or a.situas = 0) ]))
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcCor),       [And a.CodCors = ']   + lcCor + [' ], [])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcCategoria), [And a.Categoria = ']   + lcCategoria + [' ], [])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcLocals), 	[And a.Locals = ']   + lcLocals + [' ], [])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Not IsEmpty(lcEqv),       [And (a.cproeqs = '] + lcEqv     + [' Or a.Cpros = ']+lcEqv+[') ], [])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + Iif(Not IsEmpty(lccClass),    [And a.cClass = ']+lccClass+[' ],[])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + Iif(lnWebs = 5, [],           [And a.ProdWebs = ] + Str(lnWebs, 1) +[ ])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Empty(lcFinI), [], 		[And a.CodFinP BetWeen '] + lcFinI + [' And '] + lcFinF + [' ])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Empty(lcCompl), [], 		[And a.dpro3s like '%] + lcCompl + [%' ])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Empty(lcTamP), [], 		[And a.TamPs = ?lcTamP ])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Empty(lcTamA), [], 		[And a.TamHs = ?lcTamA ])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Empty(lcTamC), [], 		[And a.TamLs = ?lcTamC ])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Empty(lcConjunto), [], 	[And a.Conjunts = ?lcConjunto ])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Empty(lcCodMacro), [], 	[And a.CodMacro = ?lcCodMacro ])
	lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIf(Empty(lnProdOff), [], 	[And a.ProdOff = 1 ])
	If !fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP','SEMCONSUL')
		lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + [And Not a.SemConsulta = 1 ]
	EndIf 	
	
	

	If Not Empty(ldDtAltI) And Not Empty(ldDtAltF)
		Private pDaI, pDaF
		pDaI = fDtoSQL(ldDtAltI)
		pDaF = fDtoSQL(ldDtAltF, [23:59:59])
		*** mena 18/12/2015 - incluido checagem para escolher campo de data ***
		lcWhere1 = lcWhere1 + IIF(lnTpDtCad=1, [And a.Datas ], [And a.Dtincs ]) + [BetWeen ?pDaI and ?pDaF ]
	EndIf
			
	m.llGenData1 = (ThisForm.poDataMgr1.GetDriver()=='sql')
	m.SelectPro   = ''
	m.FromPro     = ''
	m.WherePro    = ''
	m.WhereEst    = ''	

	m.SelectPro  = ([Select a.cpros,a.dpros,a.situas,a.linhas,a.cunis,a.pesoms,a.reffs,a.obspeds,]+;
					[a.cftios,a.moevs,a.moecs,a.moepcs,a.moecusfs,a.moedas,a.cproeqs,]+;
					[a.colecoes,a.Sgrus, a.foralinha,case when a.prodoff = 1 then 'S' else 'N' end as offs ])
	m.FromPro    = ([From SigCdPro a ])
	m.WherePro   = [where ] + lcWhere1
	
	*-- Rafael - 17/04/2017 - Não filtra os produtos da lista de preço
	*If !IsEmpty(lcPromo) or !IsEmpty(lcLPrecos)
	If !IsEmpty(lcPromo) 
		m.FromPro  = ""
		m.WherePro = ""
		If !IsEmpty(lcPromo)
			m.SelectPro = m.SelectPro + [,p.pecas ]
			m.FromPro  = ([From SigCdPro a Inner Join SigPrPmi p on p.cpros=a.cpros ])
			If !IsEmpty(lcGdeGru)
				m.FromPro = m.FromPro + [ Right Join SigCdGrp g On a.cgrus = g.cgrus ]
			EndIf
			m.WherePro = ([where p.promos = ']+lcPromo+[' And ]+Iif(IsEmpty(lcLPrecos),[&lcWhere1. ],[]))
		EndIf
		*-- Rafael - 17/04/2017 - Não filtra os produtos da lista de preço
		*If !IsEmpty(lcLPrecos)
		*	ldData     = Datetime()
		*	m.FromPro  = (Iif(IsEmpty(lcPromo),[From SigCdPro a ],[])+[ Inner Join SigCdLpi l on l.cpros=a.cpros ])
		*	m.WherePro = (Iif(Empty(m.WherePro),[ where ],[])+[l.LPrecos = ']+lcLPrecos+[' And &lcWhere1. ])  &&(?ldData Between l.Vencis And l.Vencfs  or (vencis is Null And ?ldData <= vencfs)) And 
		*Endif
	Else
		If (!Empty(lcOperacao) And !Empty(lnOperacao)) or Reccount([crFilOper]) > 0
			pTru = .T.
			pNot = .F.
			m.FromPro    = ([From SigCdPro a Inner Join SigMvItn e On a.cpros = e.cpros Inner Join SigMvCab x on x.EmpDopNums = e.EmpDopNums ])
			If Reccount([crFilOper]) <= 0
				If Empty(ThisForm.EmpOperas)
					m.WherePro = ([Where e.Dopes = '] + lcOperacao + [' And e.Numes = ] + lcOperai + [ ] + ;
								  Iif(lnOptOper = 1,[And ((e.Qtds - e.QtBaixas) <> 0) ],;
								  Iif(lnOptOper = 2,[And ((e.Qtds - e.QtBaixas) = 0) ],'')) + ;
								  Iif(lnOptPag = 1,[And x.ChkPagos = ?pTru ],Iif(lnOptPag = 2,[And x.ChkPagos = ?pNot ],[]))) + [And ] + lcWhere1 
				Else
					m.WherePro = ([Where e.EmpDopNums = '] + ThisForm.EmpOperas + lcOperacao + lcOperai + [' ] + ;
								  Iif(lnOptOper = 1,[And ((e.Qtds - e.QtBaixas) <> 0) ],;
								  Iif(lnOptOper = 2,[And ((e.Qtds - e.QtBaixas) = 0) ],'')) + ;
								  Iif(lnOptPag = 1,[And x.ChkPagos = ?pTru ],Iif(lnOptPag = 2,[And x.ChkPagos = ?pNot ],[]))) + [And ] + lcWhere1 
				EndIf
			Else
				lcEdns = [(]
				Go Top in crFilOper
				Do While not Eof([crFilOper])
					lcEdns = lcEdns + Iif(Len(lcEdns) > 1,[,],[]) + Chr(39) + crFilOper.EmpDopNums + Chr(39)
					Skip +1 in crFilOper
				EndDo
				lcEdns = lcEdns + [)]
				m.WherePro = ([Where e.EmpDopNums in ]+ lcEdns + ;
							  Iif(lnOptOper = 1,[ And ((e.Qtds - e.QtBaixas) <> 0) ],;
							  Iif(lnOptOper = 2,[ And ((e.Qtds - e.QtBaixas) = 0) ],'')) + ;
							  Iif(lnOptPag = 1,[And x.ChkPagos = ?pTru ],Iif(lnOptPag = 2,[And x.ChkPagos = ?pNot ],[]))) + [And ] + lcWhere1 
			Endif	
		Else
			If !IsEmpty(lcGdeGru)
				m.FromPro = ([From SigCdPro a Right Join SigCdGrp g On a.cgrus = g.cgrus ])
				m.WherePro = ([where &lcWhere1. ])
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf

	** Filtro da Aba Estoque (Nova)
	If not IsEmpty(lcEmpEst) or not IsEmpty(lcGruEst) or not IsEmpty(lcContaEst) or not IsEmpty(lcTam)
		m.FromPro = m.FromPro + [Inner Join SigMvEst t on t.cPros = a.cPros ]
		m.WhereEst = m.WhereEst + [And t.Sqtds > 0 ]
		m.WhereEst = m.WhereEst + Iif(Empty(lcEmpEst)  ,[],[And t.Emps = ']+lcEmpEst+[' ])
		m.WhereEst = m.WhereEst + Iif(Empty(lcGruEst)  ,[],[And t.Grupos = ']+lcGruEst+[' ])
		m.WhereEst = m.WhereEst + Iif(Empty(lcContaEst),[],[And t.Estos = ']+lcContaEst+[' ])
		m.WhereEst = m.WhereEst + Iif(Empty(lcTam),[],[And t.CodTams = ']+lcTam+[' ])
		If At([WHERE],Upper(m.WherePro)) <= 0
			m.WhereEst = [Where ] + Substr(m.WhereEst,5)
		Endif
	EndIf
	
	llApenas  = .F.
	lcCodMats = []
	lcCodTips = []

	Select CsMatPrima
	Go Top

	Scan
		llApenas  = CsMatPrima.apenas
		lcCodMats = CsMatPrima.mats
		lcCodTips = CsMatPrima.Tipos
		lnQtdIni  = IIf(IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.qtd_inicial),0,CsMatPrima.qtd_inicial)
		lnQtdFim  = IIf(IsEmpty(CsMatPrima.qtd_final),9999999.99,CsMatPrima.qtd_final)

	EndScan

	m.lcSqlPro1 = IIf(!IsEmpty(m.SelectPro),m.SelectPro,'') + IIf(!IsEmpty(m.FromPro),m.FromPro,'') +;
				IIf(!IsEmpty(m.WherePro),m.WherePro + m.lcWhereFpro1 + m.lcWhereFpro2,'') + m.WhereEst + ' '

	If Not Empty(m.lcWhereFproTB1)
		Text To m.lcSqlPro1 Additive NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
			<<' '>>
			Union All
			<<m.SelectPro>>
			<<m.FromPro>>
			<<Iif(!IsEmpty(m.WherePro), m.WherePro, '')>>
			<<m.lcWhereFproTB1>>

		EndText

	EndIf 

	If Not Empty(m.lcWhereFproTB2)
		Text To m.lcSqlPro1 Additive NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
			<<' '>>
			Union All
			<<m.SelectPro>>
			<<m.FromPro>>
			<<Iif(!IsEmpty(m.WherePro), m.WherePro, '')>>
			<<m.lcWhereFproTB2>>

		EndText

	EndIf 

	m.lcSqlPro2 = ('select a.cpros,sum(b.sqtds) As sqtds '+IIf(!IsEmpty(m.FromPro),m.FromPro,'') +;
					[Inner Join SigMvEst b On b.cpros=a.cpros ]+;
					Iif(!IsEmpty(m.WherePro),m.WherePro + m.lcWhereFpro1 + m.lcWhereFpro2,'')+' group by a.cpros ')

	If Not Empty(m.lcWhereFproTB1)
		Text To m.lcSqlPro2 Additive NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
			<<' '>>
			Union All
			Select a.cpros, Sum(b.sqtds) As sqtds
			<<m.FromPro>>
			Inner Join SigMvEst b On b.cpros=a.cpros
			<<Iif(!IsEmpty(m.WherePro), m.WherePro, '')>>
			<<m.lcWhereFproTB1>>
			Group By a.cpros

		EndText

	EndIf

		Text To m.lcSqlPro2 Additive NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
			<<' '>>
			Union All
			Select a.cpros, Sum(b.sqtds) As sqtds
			<<m.FromPro>>
			Inner Join SigMvEst b On b.cpros=a.cpros
			<<Iif(!IsEmpty(m.WherePro), m.WherePro, '')>>
			<<m.lcWhereFproTB2>>
			Group By a.cpros

		EndText

	EndIf 

	m.lcSqlPro2 = (m.lcSqlPro2 + ' Order by 1')

	m.laFieldMoe1 = ''
	m.laFieldMoe1(1) = [Nvl(Tmp_Pros.moevs,'   ')]
	m.laFieldMoe1(2) = [Nvl(Tmp_Pros.moecs,'   ')]
	m.laFieldMoe1(3) = [Nvl(Tmp_Pros.moepcs,'   ')]
	m.laFieldMoe1(4) = [Nvl(Tmp_Pros.moecusfs,'   ')]
	m.laFieldMoe1(5) = [Nvl(Tmp_Pros.moedas,'   ')]
	m.laFieldMoe1(6) = [']+Nvl(crSigCdPam.moedetqs,'   ')+[']
	m.lcStrFltMoeda1 = [(]

	If !llcusto
		If m.lnErro1>0 
			m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(m.lcSqlPro1,'Tmp_Pros1')
	
		EndIf 
		
		If Empty(lcPromo)
			Select a.* From Tmp_Pros1 a, crSigCdCol b Where a.Colecoes = b.Colecoes Into Cursor Tmp_Pros ReadWrite
		Else
			m.FromPro  = ([From SigCdPro a Inner Join SigPrPmv p on p.Colecoes = a.Colecoes ])
			If !IsEmpty(lcGdeGru)
				m.FromPro = m.FromPro + [ Right Join SigCdGrp g On a.cgrus = g.cgrus ]
			EndIf
			m.WherePro = ([Where p.Promos = '] + lcPromo + [' And ] + Iif(IsEmpty(lcLPrecos), [&lcWhere1. ], []))

			m.SelectPro = Strtran(m.SelectPro, [,p.pecas], [,Space(10) as pecas])
			m.lcSqlPro1 = IIf(!IsEmpty(m.SelectPro),m.SelectPro,'') + IIf(!IsEmpty(m.FromPro),m.FromPro,'') +;
						IIf(!IsEmpty(m.WherePro),m.WherePro + m.lcWhereFpro1 + m.lcWhereFpro2,'')

			If Not Empty(m.lcWhereFproTB1)
				Text To m.lcSqlPro1 Additive NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
					<<' '>>
					Union All
					<<m.SelectPro>>
					<<m.FromPro>>
					<<Iif(!IsEmpty(m.WherePro), m.WherePro, '')>>
					<<m.lcWhereFproTB1>>

				EndText

			EndIf 

			If Not Empty(m.lcWhereFproTB2)
				Text To m.lcSqlPro1 Additive NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
					<<' '>>
					Union All
					<<m.SelectPro>>
					<<m.FromPro>>
					<<Iif(!IsEmpty(m.WherePro), m.WherePro, '')>>
					<<m.lcWhereFproTB2>>

				EndText

			EndIf 

			If m.lnErro1>0 
				m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(m.lcSqlPro1,'Tmp_Pros2')

			EndIf 

			Select a.* From Tmp_Pros1 a, crSigCdCol b Where a.Colecoes = b.Colecoes ;
			 Union All ;
			Select c.* From Tmp_Pros2 c, crSigCdCol d Where c.Colecoes = d.Colecoes ;
			  Into Cursor Tmp_Pros3 ReadWrite
			  
			Select Distinct * ;
			  From Tmp_Pros3 ;
			  Into Cursor Tmp_Pros ReadWrite
		EndIf
		
		Select Tmp_Pros
		Go Top
		If Not m.lnErro1>0 
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return(.F.)
		EndIf
		If m.llGenData1

			m.lcIdQuery1 = fUniqueIds()

			If m.lnErro1>0
				m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr2.fSqlTemporario(m.lcSqlPro2, @lcIdTab1, ThisForm.poDataMgr2)

			EndIf

			Text To m.lcSql1 NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
				Select a.cpros, Sum(a.Sqtds) As Saldo, f.nTemSaldos 
					From SigMvEst a
						Inner Join <<m.lcIdTab1>> b On b.Cpros = a.Cpros
						Left Join (Select e.cPros, Sum(1) As nTemSaldos From SigMvEst e Inner Join <<m.lcIdTab1>> t On t.Cpros = e.Cpros Where Not e.Sqtds = 0 Group by e.cPros) f On f.Cpros = a.Cpros
					Group By a.cPros, f.nTemSaldos 
				Order by 1 Asc

			EndText
			If m.lnErro1 > 0
				m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr2.SQLExecute(m.lcSql1, 'Tmp_EstPro')
				
			EndIf

			Select Tmp_EstPro
			Go Top
			If Used('Tmp_EstPro')
				Select ('Tmp_EstPro')
				Index On cpros Tag cpros
				Go Top
			EndIf

				m.lcMcrsql1 = []
				m.lnNreg1 = 0
				
				Zap In ('Tmp_EstPro')

				Select ('Tmp_Pros')
				Go Top 
				
				m.llContinua1 = (Not Eof('Tmp_Pros'))
				
				DO While (m.lnErro1>0 And m.llContinua1)
					m.lnNreg1 = (m.lnNreg1+1)
					If Not IsEmpty(Tmp_Pros.cpros)
						m.lcMcrSql1 = (m.lcMcrSql1+[']+Tmp_Pros.cpros+[',])

					EndIf 

					If ((m.lnNreg1>=24 Or Eof('Tmp_Pros')) And Not Empty(m.lcMcrSql1))

						Text To m.lcSql1 NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
							Select e.cPros, Sum(1) As nTemSaldos 
								From SigMvEst e 
								Where e.cPros In (<<Left(m.lcMcrSql1, (Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))>>) And Not e.Sqtds = 0 
								Group by e.cPros
							
						EndText

						If m.lnErro1 > 0
							m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(m.lcSql1, 'crTmpProEst1')
										
						EndIf

						Text To m.lcSql1 NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
							Select a.cpros, Sum(a.Sqtds) As Saldo
								From SigMvEst a
								Where a.cPros In (<<Left(m.lcMcrSql1, (Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))>>)
								Group By a.cPros

						If m.lnErro1 > 0

							From crTmpEst0 a ;

						Into Cursor crTmpEst1

								If Not Seek(crtmpest1.cpros,'Tmp_EstPro','cpros')

						m.lnNreg1 = 0

					EndIf

					If m.llContinua1
						Skip 1 In ('Tmp_Pros')
					EndIf 
				EndDo
			EndIf 
		EndIf 

		Select Distinct a.*, Val(Str(Nvl(b.nTemSaldos, 0), 10, 0)) As nTemSaldos, Val(Str(Nvl(b.saldo,0),16,4)) as saldo, Val(Str(0,16,4)) as SaldoEstR ;
		  From Tmp_Pros a ;
		  Left Join Tmp_EstPro b On a.cpros = b.cpros ;
		 Order by a.cpros ;
		  Into Cursor Tmp_Produtos

		Use dbf('Tmp_Produtos') Again Alias CrProdutos In 0
		Use In Tmp_Pros

	Else
	
		If llApenas And (!IsEmpty(lcCodMats) Or !IsEmpty(lcCodTips))
			If m.lnErro1>0 
				Do Case
					Case Not Empty(lcCodMats) And Not Empty(lcCodTips)
						lcWxpt01 = [m.mats = ']+lcCodMats+[' And m.Tipos = ']+lcCodTips+[' ]
						lcWxpt02 = [n.mats <> ']+lcCodMats+[' And n.Tipos <> ']+lcCodTips+[']
					Case Not Empty(lcCodMats) And Empty(lcCodTips)
						lcWxpt01 = [m.mats = ']+lcCodMats+[' ]
						lcWxpt02 = [n.mats <> ']+lcCodMats+[']
					Case Empty(lcCodMats) And Not Empty(lcCodTips)
						lcWxpt01 = [m.Tipos = ']+lcCodTips+[' ]
						lcWxpt02 = [n.Tipos <> ']+lcCodTips+[']
				EndCase
				
				m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select m.cpros,m.mats,m.tipos,sum(m.qtds) as qtds ]+;
					   [From SigPrCpo m ]+;
					  [Where ] +lcWxpt01+;
							[And m.qtds between ]+AllTrim(Str(lnQtdIni,10))+[ And ]+AllTrim(Str(lnQtdFim,10))+[ ]+;
							[And Not Exists(Select n.cpros From SigPrCpo n ]+;
							[Where m.cpros = n.cpros And ]+lcWxpt02+[) ]+;
				    [Group by m.cpros,m.mats,m.tipos ]+;
				    [Order by m.cpros ],'Tmp_Compos')
			EndIf 
			If Not m.lnErro1>0 
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				ThisForm.Release
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf
			If Used('tmp_compos')
				Select Tmp_Compos
				Go Top
				m.lcMcrsql1 = (IIf(!IsEmpty(m.SelectPro),m.SelectPro,'') + IIf(!IsEmpty(m.FromPro),m.FromPro,'') +;
							   ' where 0=1')
				If m.lnErro1>0 
					m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(m.lcMcrSql1,'Tmp_Pros1')
				EndIf 
				If Used('Tmp_Pros1')
					Select a.* From Tmp_Pros1 a, crSigCdCol b Where a.Colecoes = b.Colecoes Into Cursor Tmp_Pros ReadWrite

					Go Top

				m.lcMcrsql1 = []

					m.lnNreg1 = (m.lnNreg1+1)
					If Not IsEmpty(tmp_compos.cpros)
						m.lcMcrSql1 = (m.lcMcrSql1+[']+tmp_compos.cpros+[',])
					EndIf 
					If ((m.lnNreg1>=24 Or Eof('tmp_compos')) And Not Empty(m.lcMcrSql1))

							Select e.cPros, Sum(1) As nTemSaldos 
								From SigMvEst e 
								Where e.cPros In (<<Left(m.lcMcrSql1, (Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))>>) And Not e.Sqtds = 0 
								Group by e.cPros
							
						EndText

						If m.lnErro1 > 0
							m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(m.lcSql1, 'crTmpProEst1')
										
						EndIf

						Text To m.lcSql1 NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
							Select a.cpros, Sum(a.Sqtds) As Saldo
								From SigMvEst a
								Where a.cPros In (<<Left(m.lcMcrSql1, (Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))>>)
								Group By a.cPros
							Order by 1 Asc

						EndText
						If m.lnErro1 > 0
							m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(m.lcSql1, 'crTmpEst0')

						EndIf

						Select a.cPros, a.Saldo, b.nTemSaldos ;
							From crTmpEst0 a ;
								Left Join crTmpProEst1 b On b.cPros = a.cPros ;
						Order by 1 Asc ;
						Into Cursor crTmpEst1

						If Used('crtmpest1')
							Select ('crtmpest1')
							Go Top

								If Not Seek(crtmpest1.cpros,'Tmp_EstPro','cpros')
									Insert Into Tmp_EstPro (cpros,saldo,ntemsaldos) Values (crtmpest1.cpros,crtmpest1.saldo,Nvl(crtmpest1.ntemsaldos,0))

								EndIf 

							EndScan 

						EndIf 

						m.lcMcrSql2 = Strtran(m.lcSqlPro1,[where ],[where a.cpros in (]+Left(m.lcMcrSql1,(Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))+[) and ])
						If m.lnErro1>0 
							m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(m.lcMcrSql2,'crtmppro1')
						EndIf 
						If Used('crtmppro1')
							Select ('crtmppro1')
							Go Top
							Scan While m.lnErro1>0
								If Not Seek(crtmppro1.cpros,'Tmp_Pros','cpros')
									Select ('crtmppro1')
									Scatter MemVar Memo
									Insert Into Tmp_Pros From Memvar
								EndIf

						m.lnNreg1 = 0

			  From Tmp_Produtos a ;

				Left Join Tmp_EstPro e On e.cpros = b.cpros ;
			  Into Cursor Tmp_Prods

			Use dbf('Tmp_Prods') Again Alias CrProdutos In 0

		Else
		
			If Used('csMatprima')
				m.lcMcrsql1 = []

				Go Top

					If Not IsEmpty(csMatprima.mats)
						m.lcMcrSql1 = (m.lcMcrSql1+[']+csMatprima.mats+[',])
					EndIf

					EndIf

						Do Case
							Case Not Empty(m.lcMcrSql1) And Empty(m.lcMcrSql3)
								m.lcMcrSql1 = ([select a.cpros,a.compos,a.mats,a.qtds,a.Tipos from SigPrCpo a where a.mats in (]+Left(m.lcMcrSql1,(Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))+[) order by a.cpros])

												[From SigPrCpo a ] + ;
											   [Where a.mats in (]+Left(m.lcMcrSql1,(Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))+[) And ] + ;

									Insert Into Tmp_CompoF (cpros,compos,mats,tipos,qtds) Values (crtmpcompf1.cpros,crtmpcompf1.compos,crtmpcompf1.mats,crtmpcompf1.tipos,crtmpcompf1.qtds)
								EndIf 
							EndScan 
						EndIf 
						m.lcMcrsql1 = []
						m.lnNreg1 = 0
					EndIf

					If m.llContinua1

					EndIf

				Go Top

				m.llContinua1 = (Not Eof('csMatprima'))

					If Not IsEmpty(csMatprima.Cgrus)
						m.lcMcrSql1 = (m.lcMcrSql1+[']+csMatprima.cgrus+[',])
					EndIf

									Insert Into Tmp_CompoF (cpros,compos,mats,tipos,qtds,Cgrus) Values ;
									(crtmpcompf1.cpros,crtmpcompf1.compos,crtmpcompf1.mats,crtmpcompf1.tipos,;
									crtmpcompf1.qtds,CrTmpCompf1.Cgrus)
								EndIf

				Go Top
				lFirst = .T.
				Scan
					lcMatPrima = CsMatPrima.mats
					lcTipos    = CsMatPrima.tipos
					lnQtdIni   = CsMatPrima.qtd_inicial
					lnQtdFim   = CsMatPrima.qtd_final
					lcGrupo    = CsMatPrima.Cgrus
					
					Do Case
						Case Not Empty(lcMatPrima) And Not Empty(lcTipos)
							lcSlxp01 = [a.Mats = lcMatPrima And a.tipos = lcTipos]

						Case Not Empty(lcGrupo) And Not Empty(lcTipos)
							lcSlxp01 = [a.Cgrus = lcGrupo And a.tipos = lcTipos]

						Case Not Empty(lcMatPrima) And Empty(lcTipos)
							lcSlxp01 = [a.Mats = lcMatPrima]

						Otherwise
							lcSlxp01 = [a.Cgrus = lcGrupo]
					EndCase

					If lFirst
						Select a.cpros,a.Tipos,IIf(IsEmpty(a.compos),a.mats,a.compos) as mats, sum(a.qtds) as qtds ;
						  From Tmp_CompoF a ;
						 Where &lcSlxp01. And Iif(Not Empty(lnQtdFim), a.qtds Between lnQtdIni And lnQtdFim, .T.) ;
						 Order By a.cpros ;
						 Group By 1,2,3 ;
						  Into Cursor 'Tmp_Compos'
						lFirst = .F.
					Else
						Select a.cpros,a.tipos,IIf(IsEmpty(a.compos),a.mats,a.compos) as mats, sum(a.qtds) as qtds ;
						  From Tmp_CompoF a;
						 Where &lcSlxp01. And ;
						 	   ((IIf(!IsEmpty(lnQtdFim),a.qtds Between lnQtdIni And lnQtdFim,.T.)) And ;
									 a.cpros In (Select b.cpros From Tmp_Compos b)) ;
						 Order By a.cpros ;
						 Group By 1,2,3 ;
						  Into Cursor Tmp_custo
						  
						Use In Tmp_Compos
						Use Dbf('Tmp_custo') Again Alias Tmp_Compos In 0
						Use In Tmp_custo
					EndIf
					Select CsMatPrima
				EndScan
			EndIf 
			If Used('tmp_compos')
				m.lcMcrsql1 = (IIf(!IsEmpty(m.SelectPro),m.SelectPro,'') + IIf(!IsEmpty(m.FromPro),m.FromPro,'') +;
							   ' where 0=1')
				If m.lnErro1>0 
					m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(m.lcMcrSql1,'Tmp_Pros1')
				EndIf 
				If Used('Tmp_Pros1')
					Select a.* From Tmp_Pros1 a, crSigCdCol b Where a.Colecoes = b.Colecoes Into Cursor Tmp_Pros ReadWrite

					Select ('Tmp_Pros')
					Index On CPros Tag CPros
					Go Top
				EndIf 
				m.lcMcrsql1 = []
				m.lnNreg1 = 0
				
				Zap In ('Tmp_EstPro')

				Select ('tmp_compos')
				Go Top 
				
				m.llContinua1 = (Not Eof('tmp_compos'))
				
				DO While (m.lnErro1>0 And m.llContinua1)
					m.lnNreg1 = (m.lnNreg1+1)
					If Not IsEmpty(tmp_compos.cpros)
						m.lcMcrSql1 = (m.lcMcrSql1+[']+tmp_compos.cpros+[',])
					EndIf 
					If ((m.lnNreg1>=24 Or Eof('tmp_compos')) And Not Empty(m.lcMcrSql1))

						Text To m.lcSql1 NoShow TextMerge PreText 1 + 2 + 4 + 8
							Select a.cpros, Sum(a.Sqtds) As Saldo, f.nTemSaldos 
								From SigMvEst a
									Left Join (Select e.cPros, Sum(1) As nTemSaldos 
												From SigMvEst e 
												Where e.cPros In (<<Left(m.lcMcrSql1, (Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))>>) And Not e.Sqtds = 0 
													Group by e.cPros) f On f.Cpros = a.Cpros
								Where a.cPros In (<<Left(m.lcMcrSql1, (Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))>>)
								Group By a.cPros, f.nTemSaldos 
							Order by 1 Asc

						EndText
						If m.lnErro1 > 0
							m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(m.lcSql1, 'crtmpest1')
							
						EndIf

						If Used('crtmpest1')
							Select ('crtmpest1')
							Go Top
							Scan While m.lnErro1>0
								If Not Seek(crtmpest1.cpros,'Tmp_EstPro','cpros')
									Insert Into Tmp_EstPro (cpros,saldo,ntemsaldos) Values (crtmpest1.cpros,crtmpest1.saldo,Nvl(crtmpest1.ntemsaldos,0))
								EndIf
							EndScan
						EndIf 
						m.lcMcrSql2 = Strtran(m.lcSqlPro1,[where ],[where a.cpros in (]+Left(m.lcMcrSql1,(Len(m.lcMcrSql1)-1))+[) and ])
						If m.lnErro1>0 
							m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(m.lcMcrSql2,'crtmppro1')
						EndIf 
						If Used('crtmppro1')
							Select ('crtmppro1')
							Go Top
							Scan While m.lnErro1>0
								If Not Seek(crtmppro1.cpros,'Tmp_Pros','cpros')
									Select ('crtmppro1')
									Scatter MemVar Memo
									Insert Into Tmp_Pros From Memvar
								EndIf 
							EndScan 
						EndIf 
						m.lcMcrsql1 = []
						m.lnNreg1 = 0
					EndIf 
					m.llContinua1 = (Not Eof('tmp_compos'))
					If m.llContinua1
						Skip 1 In ('tmp_compos')
					EndIf 
				EndDo
			EndIf 

			If Not m.lnErro1>0 
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				ThisForm.Release
				Return(.F.)
			EndIf

			Select Tmp_Pros
			Go Top

			Select Distinct * ;
			  From Tmp_Pros ;
			 Order by CPros ;
			  Into Cursor Tmp_Produtos

			Use In Tmp_Pros
			
			** Atualização dos cursores		
			SELECT Tmp_Compos
			LOCATE
			SELECT Tmp_EstPro
			LOCATE
			SELECT Tmp_Produtos
			LOCATE

			Select Distinct a.*, Val(Str(Nvl(e.nTemSaldos, 0), 10, 0)) As nTemSaldos, Val(Str(Nvl(e.saldo,0),16,4)) as Saldo, Val(Str(0,16,4)) as SaldoEstR ;
			  From Tmp_Produtos a ;
				Inner Join Tmp_Compos b On b.cpros = a.cpros ;
				Left Join Tmp_EstPro e On e.cpros = b.cpros ;
			  Into Cursor Tmp_Prods

			Use dbf('Tmp_Prods') Again Alias CrProdutos In 0
		
			Use In Tmp_Compos
			Use In Tmp_Produtos
			Use In Tmp_Prods

		EndIf
	EndIf	

	If Not Used('crSigCdMoe')
		If m.lnErro1>0 
			= ThisForm.poDataMgr1.CursorQuery('SigCdMoe','crSigCdMoe')
			m.lnErro1 = Iif(Used('crSigCdMoe'),1,-1)
		EndIf 
	EndIf 
	If Used('crSigCdMoe')
		Select ('crSigCdMoe')
		Index On cmoes Tag cmoes
		Set Order To ('') In ('crSigCdMoe')
	EndIf 
	If Not Used('crSigCdCot')
		m.lcMcrsql1 = [select a.cmoes,a.datas,a.horas,a.valos from SigCdCot a inner join SigCdMoe b on b.cmoes=a.cmoes order by 1,2 Desc,3 Desc]
		If m.lnErro1>0 
			m.lnErro1 = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SQLExecute(m.lcMcrSql1,'crSigCdCot')
		EndIf 
	EndIf 
	If Used('crSigCdCot')
		Select ('crSigCdCot')
		Index On cmoes+Dtos(datas)+horas DESCENDING Tag cmoedata
		Set Order To ('') In ('crSigCdCot')
	Else
		=MessageBox('Erro ao Criar o Cursor de Cotações!!!', 0+16, 'Atenção!!! Contate o Suporte Técnico')
		Return 0
	EndIf 

	
	Select csMatPrima
	Append Blank
	With ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.filtro.Principal.grd_composicao
		.Col_Mats.Txt_Mats.Refresh
		.Refresh
	EndWith	

	* Checando Filtros de Características de Produtos
	Select Codigos From crSigCrRap Where Selec = 1 Into Cursor LocalCarcs ReadWrite
	Select LocalCarcs
	Index On Codigos Tag Codigos
	Go Top In LocalCarcs
	If Not Eof([LocalCarcs]) && Se selecionou alguma Característica para filtro
		Select crProdutos
		Scan
			llTem = .f.
			
			lcQuery = [Select Codigos ] + ;
					    [From SigPrCar ] + ;
					   [Where CPros = '] + crProdutos.CPros + [']

			If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [BusCars]) < 1)
				=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (BusCars)])
				Return .f.
			EndIf
			Select BusCars
			Go Top In BusCars
			If Not Eof([BusCars])
				Scan While Not llTem
					If Seek(BusCars.Codigos, [LocalCarcs], [Codigos])
						llTem = .t.
					EndIf
				EndScan
			EndIf

			If Not llTem
				Delete In crProdutos
			EndIf
		EndScan
	EndIf

	* Mensagem de advertência no caso de não encontrar nada
	Select CrProdutos
	Go Top
	If Eof()
		MessageBox('Nenhum produto foi encontrado com as características digitadas !',64,'')
		ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Produto_Inicial.SetFocus
		Return
	EndIf

	If !IsEmpty(lcPromo)
		Index On pecas+cpros Tag cpros
	Else
		Index On cpros Tag cpros
	EndIf
	Set Order To cpros
	Go Top

	ThisForm.ConsultaExecutada = .T.

	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta
		.GetCor.Value = Space(04)
		.getTam.Value = Space(04)
		.getLoc.Value = Space(10)
	EndWith

	With This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.grd_produtos
		.RecordSource            = 'CrProdutos'
		.col_cpros.ControlSource = 'CrProdutos.cpros'
		.col_dpros.ControlSource = 'CrProdutos.dpros'
		.Column3.ControlSource = 'CrProdutos.Offs'
	EndWith

	Select CrProdutos
	Set Filter To
	This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.Optiongroup1.Value = 1
	
	Go Top In ('crProdutos')

	With This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao
		.Refresh
		.grd_produtos.AfterRowColChange
	EndWith
	
	Select ('crProdutos')
	Go Top In ('crProdutos')
	If Eof()
		This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.Command1.Visible = .F.
		This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.Command1.Refresh
		Return 0
	EndIf

	Zap in crFilOper

	Select ('crProdutos')
	Go Bottom In ('crProdutos')
	ThisForm.poDataMgr1.RollBack()
EndIf

ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.btnMinimo.Visible = .t.

ThisForm.Caption = 'Consulta Genérica do Produto : ' + AllTrim(CrProdutos.cpros) + ' / '  +;
				   AllTrim(CrProdutos.dpros)

ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Enabled = .f.

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado
	.ZOrder
	With .Result.selecao
		.ZOrder
		.grd_produtos.Refresh
		.grd_produtos.SetFocus
	EndWith
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmd_cancela.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmd_cancela.Click
goSistema.ConsultaAberta = .f.
ThisForm.Release
ENDPROC
