------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Custo.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Custo.Click
Local lcCodPro

ThisForm.MousePointer = 11

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.custo
	.cntCusto.Get_precoc.Value   = Nvl(CrTSigPro.pcuss,0)
	.cntCusto.Get_moedac.Value   = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moecs,'')
	.cntCusto.Get_pesom.Value    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.pesoms,0)
	.cntCusto.Get_fcusto.Value   = Nvl(CrTSigPro.fcustos,0)
	.cntCusto.Get_moepc.Value    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moepcs,'')
	.cntCusto.Get_precop.Value   = Nvl(CrTSigPro.custofs,0)
	.cntCusto.Get_moepesos.Value = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moecusfs,'')
	.cntValor.Get_margem.Value   = Nvl(CrTSigPro.markupa,0)
	.cntValor.Get_moeideal.Value = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moedas,'')
	.cntCusto.GetMargem.Value    = Nvl(crTSigpro.margems,0)
	.cntValor.Get_precov.Value   = Nvl(CrTSigPro.pvens,0)
	.cntValor.Get_moedav.Value   = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moevs,'')
	.cntValor.Get_fvenda.Value   = Nvl(CrTSigPro.fvendas,0)
	.cntValor.Get_moepv.Value    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moepvs,'')
	.cntValor.Get_CFtio.Value    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.cftios,'')
	.cntValor.Get_PFtio.Value    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.pftios,0)
	.cntValor.Get_MFtio.Value    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.mftios,'')
EndWith

* Rotina utilizada no formulário SigPrCpO
custo = CrTSigPro.pcuss
fpeso = CrTSigPro.pesoms * CrTSigPro.fcustos
fator = CrTSigPro.margems
moec  = 1
moep  = 1
moev  = 1
moecf = 1
moeda = 1
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moecs)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moecs)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moecs)
		If Found()
			moec = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moepcs)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moepcs)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moepcs)
		If Found()
			moep = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moecusfs)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moecusfs)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moecusfs)
		If Found()
			moecf = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moedas)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moedas)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moedas)
		If Found()
			moeda = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
custof = ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.custo.cntCusto.Get_precop.Value
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moevs)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moevs)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moevs)
		If Found()
			moev = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
If CrTSigPro.moecusfs <> CrTSigPro.moedas
	ideal = custof * moecf / moeda * fator
Else
	ideal = custof * fator
EndIf

ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.custo.cntValor.Get_precoi.Value = CrTSigPro.PvIdeals &&ideal

lcCodPro = CrTSigPro.cpros

If Used('CrSigPrCpo')
	This.grd_custo.RecordSource = '' && grade da pagina atual
	This.Parent.Fase.grd_consumo.RecordSource = ''	&& grade da pagina consumo
	This.Parent.composicao.grdCompo.RecordSource = ''	&& grade da pagina Composicao
	Use In CrSigPrCpo
EndIf

lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select a.cpros,a.mats,a.dcompos,a.unicompos,a.pcompos,a.qtds,]+;
				[a.moeds,a.obscompos,a.qtscons,a.grupos,a.dscgrp,a.cats,a.cunips,a.pesos,0 AS TpCalcCus, Space(20) aS Tipos, a.cgrus ]+;
		   [From SigPrCpo a ]+;
		  [Where a.cpros = ']+lcCodPro+[' ],'CrSigPrCpo')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	ThisForm.Release
EndIf
Select CrSigPrCpo
Go Top

With This.grd_custo
	.RecordSource = 'CrSigPrCpo'
	.material.ControlSource = 'CrSigPrCpo.mats'
	.descrimt.ControlSource = 'CrSigPrCpo.dcompos'
	.unidmt.ControlSource   = 'CrSigPrCpo.unicompos'
	.precomt.ControlSource  = 'CrSigPrCpo.pcompos'
	.qtdmt.ControlSource    = 'CrSigPrCpo.qtds'
	.moedamt.ControlSource  = 'CrSigPrCpo.moeds'
	.obscomp.ControlSource  = 'CrSigPrCpo.obscompos'
	.Column8.ControlSource  = 'CrSigPrCpo.Pesos'
	.Column9.ControlSource  = 'CrSigPrCpo.cUniPs'
	.PrecoMt.Width = 105
	.Moedamt.Width = 31
	If Not ThisForm.HabilitaPeso
		.Column8.Width = 1
		.Column9.Width = 1
		.descrimt.Width  = 188 + 104
	EndIf
	If Not Thisform.Habvalor 
		.Precomt.Width = 0
		.MoedaMt.Width = 0
	Endif
EndWith

lcSql = [Select UnitMeds, MoedMeds From SigMvEst Where Cpros = ']+CrTSigPro.Cpros+[' And Emps = ']+_Empr+[']
If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcSql,'LocalEst') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

Select LocalEst
Locate For UnitMeds <> 0

With This
	.Get_icms.Value                     = Nvl(CrTSigPro.icms,0)
	.Get_vl_estimado.Value              = Nvl(CrTSigPro.valors,0)
	.Get_vl_estimado_moeda.Value        = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moedas,'')
	.Get_cmedio.Value                   = Nvl(LocalEst.UnitMeds,'')
	.Get_moem.Value                     = Nvl(LocalEst.MoedMeds ,'')
	.Get_PFtio.Value                    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.pftios,0)
	.Get_MFtio.Value                    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.mftios,'')
	.Get_fator_custo.Value              = Nvl(CrTSigPro.fcustos,0)
	.Get_fator_custo_moeda.Value        = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moepcs,'')
	.Get_CFtio.Value                    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.cftios,'')
	.cntCusto2.Get_pr_custo.Value       = Nvl(CrTSigPro.custofs,0)
	.cntCusto2.Get_pr_custo_moeda.Value = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moecusfs,'')
	.cntCusto2.Get_margem.Value         = Nvl(CrTSigPro.markupa,0)
EndWith

ThisForm.MousePointer = 0
With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.custo.grd_custo
	.Refresh
	.SetFocus
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Estoque.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Estoque.Click
Local lcCodEmp, lcCodPro

lcCodPro = CrTSigPro.cpros

ThisForm.MousePointer = 11
lGrdOk = ThisForm.mGrdEstoque()
lGrdOk = IIf(Type('lGrdOk')='L',lGrdOk,.T.)
If !lGrdOk Or !Used('CrSigMvEst') Or !Used('CrSigOpEtq1')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

*** mena - 14/11/2017 - excluir estoques invisiveis sem checar empresa ***
*** mena - 07/10/2017 - excluir estoques invisiveis ***
IF !fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP', 'ESTOQUEINV' )
	Select CrSigMvEst
	Go Top
	SCAN
		SELECT crEstInv
		LOCATE for iclis = CrSigMvEst.estos
		IF FOUND()
			SELECT CrSigMvEst
			DELETE
		ELSE
			SELECT CrSigMvEst
		ENDIF
	ENDSCAN
ENDIF
********************************************************

Select CrSigMvEst
Go Top

If Used('Tmp_Hist')
	Use In Tmp_Hist
EndIf

With ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_resultado.Result.Estoque
	If CrSigCdPac.ddCongps > 0
		llDataPhi = fDtoSQL(.periodo_Historico.Get_HistI.Value)
		llDataphf = fDtoSQL(.Periodo_historico.Get_Histf.Value,"23:59:59")
	Else
		llDataPhi = Ctod('')
		llDataphf = Ctod('')
	Endif
EndWith

lcSql = [Select a.cpros,a.emps,a.grupos,a.estos,a.datas,a.numes,a.dopes,a.empos,]+;
		[a.qtds,a.opers,a.sqtds,a.codbarras,a.codcors,a.codtams,a.Cidchaves,a.Pesos,a.spesos,a.seqs, Convert(numeric(9,3),0) as Fators ]+;
		[From SigMvHst a ]+;
		[Where a.cpros = ']+lcCodPro+[' ] + ;
		Iif(llDataPhf = Ctod(''),[],[And a.Datas Between ?llDataPhi And ?llDataphf ])

lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcSql,'Tmp_Hist')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

*--Rafael - 02/08/2016 - Troca do Codigo de Barra Pelo Fator da Venda
If crSigCdPac.FatorBarra = 1
	Select Tmp_Hist
	Scan
		
		lcEDN = Tmp_Hist.Emps + Tmp_Hist.Dopes + Str(Tmp_Hist.Numes,6)
		
		lcQuery = [Select Fators From SigMvItn where empdopnums = '] + lcEDN + [' and cpros = '] + Tmp_Hist.Cpros +[' and codbarras = ] + Str(codbarras,14)
		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalFator') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalFator)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		
		If Reccount([LocalFator])>0
			Select Tmp_Hist
			Replace Fators With LocalFator.Fators in Tmp_Hist
		EndIf 
		
		Select Tmp_Hist
	EndScan 
EndIf 
ThisForm.MousePointer = 0

ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.estoque.grd_estoque.AfterRowColChange
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.estoque.Refresh
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.estoque.grd_estoque.Refresh
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.estoque.grd_estoque.SetFocus
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Selecao.Activate
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Selecao.Activate
If !This.Cnt_Preco.Visible
	this.grd_produtos.SetFocus() && Tiago - 14/07/2015 - Inclui para sempre dar o focu na grade para saber qual produto está analisando
EndIf

If fChecaAcesso([SIGOPCGP], [ALTPROD])
	This.BtnCadastroAlterar.Visible = .T.
Else
	This.BtnCadastroAlterar.Visible = .F.
EndIf 

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Fase.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Fase.Click
Local lcCodPro

lcCodPro = CrTSigPro.CPros

If Used('CrSigPrCpo')
	This.Grd_Consumo.RecordSource = '' && grade da pagina atual
	This.Parent.custo.grd_custo.RecordSource = ''	&& grade da pagina Custo
	This.Parent.composicao.grdCompo.RecordSource = ''	&& grade da pagina Composicao
	Use In CrSigPrCpo
EndIf

lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select a.cpros,a.mats,a.dcompos,a.unicompos,a.pcompos,a.qtds,]+;
				[a.moeds,a.obscompos,a.qtscons,a.grupos,a.dscgrp,a.cats,0 as TpCalcCus, Space(20) as Tipos ]+;
		   [From SigPrCpo a ]+;
		  [Where a.cpros = ']+lcCodPro+[' ],'CrSigPrCpo')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	ThisForm.Release
EndIf

Select CrSigPrCpo
Go Top

With This
	.grd_consumo.RecordSource           = 'CrSigPrCpo'
	.Get_dcompos.ControlSource          = 'CrSigPrCpo.dcompos'
	.grd_consumo.material.ControlSource = 'CrSigPrCpo.mats'
	.grd_consumo.unidmt.ControlSource   = 'CrSigPrCpo.unicompos'
	.grd_consumo.consmt.ControlSource   = 'CrSigPrCpo.qtscons'
	.grd_consumo.qtdmt.ControlSource    = 'CrSigPrCpo.qtds'
	.grd_consumo.grupo.ControlSource    = 'CrSigPrCpo.grupos'
	.grd_consumo.dscgrp.ControlSource   = 'CrSigPrCpo.dscgrp'
	.grd_consumo.cats.ControlSource     = 'CrSigPrCpo.cats'
EndWith

Select CrSigPrCpo
Go Top

ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.grd_consumo.Refresh
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Get_dcompos.Refresh

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Fase.Activate
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Fase.Activate
Local lcArquivo2, lcCodPro

lcArquivo2 = Sys(2023) + [\] + goSistema.Sys2015Tmp + Sys(2015) + [.jpg]

lcCodPro = CrTSigPro.cpros

If Used('CsFase')
	This.GraDFase.RecordSource = '' 
	Use In CsFase
EndIf

lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select a.produtos,a.grupos,a.ordems,a.etiquetas,a.FigProcs,]+;
			[a.obs,b.descrs ]+;
	   [From SigCdPrf a, SigCdGcr b ]+;
	  [Where a.produtos = ']+lcCodPro+[' And a.grupos = b.codigos Order By a.ordems ],'CsFase')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	ThisForm.Release
EndIf
Select CsFase
Go Top

This.Get_Obs.ControlSource = 'CsFase.obs'

With This.GradFase
	.RecordSource = 'CsFase'
	.Column1.ControlSource = 'CsFase.Ordems'
	.Column2.ControlSource = 'CsFase.Grupos'
	.Column3.ControlSource = 'CsFase.Etiquetas'
	.Column4.ControlSource = 'CsFase.Descrs'
EndWith

If !IsEmpty(CsFase.FigProcs)
	Clear Resources
	Copy Memo CsFase.FigProcs To (lcArquivo2)
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.FigJpg.Picture = (lcArquivo2)
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.FigJpg.Visible = .T.
Else
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.FigJpg.Picture = ""
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.FigJpg.Visible = .F.
EndIf
This.Refresh()
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.GradFase.Refresh
Select CrTSigPro
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Pag_Etiqueta.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Pag_Etiqueta.Click
ThisForm.Draw
ThisForm.LockScreen = .T.

ThisForm.MousePointer = 11

If (ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta.ChkGrpOpe.Value = 1)
	ThisForm.CarregaGrpOpe
EndIf

lGrdOk = ThisForm.mGrdEstoque()
lGrdOk = IIf(Type('lGrdOk')='L',lGrdOk,.T.)
If !lGrdOk Or !Used('CrSigMvEst') Or !Used('CrSigOpEtq1')
	ThisForm.LockScreen = .T.
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

If Not ThisForm.MontaEquivals(crTSigPro.CPros)
	Return .f.
EndIf

Select crSigMvEtr
Zap In crSigMvEtr

*** mena - 14/11/2017 - excluir estoques invisiveis sem checar empresa ***
*** mena - 07/10/2017 - excluir estoques invisiveis ***
IF !fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP', 'ESTOQUEINV' )
	Select CrSigMvEst
	Go Top
	SCAN
		SELECT crEstInv
		LOCATE for iclis = CrSigMvEst.estos
		IF FOUND()
			SELECT CrSigMvEst
			DELETE
		ELSE
			SELECT CrSigMvEst
		ENDIF
	ENDSCAN
ENDIF
********************************************************


Select crSigMvEst
Go Top
Scan
	*** Tiago   - 08/09/11 - Cleber pediu para não mostrar saldos que estiverm zerados
	*** Richard - 17/10/11 - Coloquei como configuração nos parâmetros pois na Carat Precisam Ver os Barras
	If (CrSigCdPac.SacGdus <> 1) And (SQtds = 0 and Qtdemphs = 0 and SPesos = 0)
		Loop
	EndIf
	***
	Scatter Memvar Memo
	If Seek(crSigMvEst.Emps + crSigMvEst.Grupos + crSigMvEst.Estos + crSigMvEst.CodCors + crSigMvEst.CodTams, [crSigMvEtr], [EmpGruCon])
		Replace SQtds    With SQtds + crSigMvEst.SQtds, ;
				QtdEmpHs With QtdEmpHs + crSigMvEst.QtdEmpHs In crSigMvEtr
	Else
		Insert Into crSigMvEtr From Memvar
	EndIf
EndScan
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta.optiongroup1.Visible = .t.
Select crSigMvEtr
Sum SQtds, Qtdemphs, SPesos To lnEst, lnEmp, lnPes

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta
	.optiongroup1.Visible = (CrSigCdPac.SacGdus = 1)
	.getTotEst.Value  = lnEst
	.GettotEmp.Value  = lnEmp
	.GetTotPes.Value  = lnPes
	.getTotEst.Refresh
	.GettotEmp.Refresh
	.Gettotpes.Refresh
EndWith
	
Select crSigMvEtr
SET ORDER TO tag emps	&& mena 02/09/2016 - ordena por empresa para ficar igual ao estoque - Rosana Joias
Go Top

ThisForm.MousePointer = 0

With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta.Grd_Estoque
	.AfterRowColChange
	.Refresh
	.SetFocus
EndWith
ThisForm.LockScreen = .F.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Composicao.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Composicao.Click
Local lcCodPro

* Rotina utilizada no formulário SigPrCpO
custo = CrTSigPro.pcuss
fpeso = CrTSigPro.pesoms * CrTSigPro.fcustos
fator = CrTSigPro.margems
moec  = 1
moep  = 1
moev  = 1
moecf = 1
moeda = 1
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moecs)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moecs)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moecs)
		If Found()
			moec = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moepcs)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moepcs)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moepcs)
		If Found()
			moep = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moecusfs)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moecusfs)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moecusfs)
		If Found()
			moecf = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moedas)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moedas)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moedas)
		If Found()
			moeda = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
custof = ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.custo.cntCusto.Get_precop.Value
If !IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.moevs)
	Select CrSigCdMoe
	Locate For (CrSigCdMoe.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moevs)
	If Found() And CrSigCdMoe.cotas
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moevs)
		If Found()
			moev = CrSigCdCot.valos
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

lcCodPro = CrTSigPro.cpros

If Used('CrSigPrCpo')
	This.grdCompo.RecordSource = '' && grade da pagina atual
	This.Parent.custo.Grd_custo.RecordSource = ''	&& grade da pagina Custo
	This.Parent.Fase.grd_consumo.RecordSource = ''	&& grade da pagina consumo
	Use In CrSigPrCpo
EndIf

lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select a.cpros,a.mats,a.dcompos,a.unicompos,a.pcompos,a.qtds,]+;
				[a.moeds,a.obscompos,a.qtscons,a.grupos,a.dscgrp,a.cats,a.cunips,a.pesos, a.Etiqs, a.Cgrus,]+;
				[0 as TpCalcCus, a.Tipos, a.cgrus ]+;
		   [From SigPrCpo a ]+;
		  [Where a.cpros = ']+lcCodPro+[' ]+;
		  [Order By a.Tipos,a.Mats],'CrSigPrCpo')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	ThisForm.Release
EndIf
Select CrSigPrCpo
Go Top

* Acertando a Grade de Custo
With This.grdCompo
	.AllowHeaderSizing      = .F.
	.AllowRowSizing         = .F.
	.ColumnCount            =  13
	.DeleteMark             = .F.
	.RecordSource           = 'crSigPrCpo'

	.Column1.ControlSource  = 'crSigPrCpo.Mats'
	.Column2.ControlSource  = 'crSigPrCpo.DCompos'
	.Column3.ControlSource  = 'crSigPrCpo.UniCompos'
	.Column4.ControlSource  = 'crSigPrCpo.PCompos'
	.Column5.ControlSource  = 'crSigPrCpo.Qtds'
	.Column6.ControlSource  = 'crSigPrCpo.Qtds * crSigPrCpo.PCompos'
	.Column7.ControlSource  = 'crSigPrCpo.Moeds'
	.Column8.ControlSource  = 'crSigPrCpo.ObsCompos'
	.Column9.ControlSource  = 'crSigPrCpo.Etiqs'
	.Column10.ControlSource = 'crSigPrCpo.QtsCons'
	.Column11.ControlSource = 'crSigPrCpo.Pesos'
	.Column12.ControlSource = 'crSigPrCpo.cUniPs'
	.Column13.ControlSource = 'crSigPrCpo.Tipos'

	.Column11.Visible = .t.
	.Column12.Visible = .t.
	.Column4.Visible = .t.
	.Column6.Visible = .t.
	If Not ThisForm.HabilitaPeso
*!*			.Column11.Width = 0
*!*			.Column12.Width = 0
*		.Column2.Width  = 135
	EndIf
*!*		.Column2.Width = 127
*!*		.Column4.Width = 73
*!*		.Column6.width = 58
	If ThisForm.TpCalcP = 2
*!*			.Column2.Width = 127 + 129
*!*			.Column4.Width = 0
*!*			.Column6.width = 0
		.Column4.Visible = .f.
		.Column6.Visible = .f.
	Endif

	For I=1 To .ColumnCount
		lcCol = Alltrim(Str(I))
		.Column&lcCol..Movable   = .f.
		.Column&lcCol..Resizable = .f.
		.Column&lcCol..ReadOnly  = .f.
	Next

	.Refresh	
	.SetFocus
	This.grdCompo.Column13.SetFocus
	This.Refresh 
EndWith

ThisForm.TotalGrupo


ENDPROC

* Classe: optiongroup (Base: optiongroup)
* Registro: 255
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Used ('CrSigMvEst')
	Select CrSigMvEst
EndIf
lGrdOk = ThisForm.mGrdEstoque()
lGrdOk = IIf(Type('lGrdOk')='L',lGrdOk,.T.)
If !lGrdOk Or !Used('CrSigMvEst') Or !Used('CrSigOpEtq1')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf
Go Top
With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.Grd_Estoque
	.AfterRowColChange
	.Refresh
	.SetFocus
EndWith
With This.Parent.Parent.Pag_Etiqueta.OptionGroup1
	.Value = This.Value
	.Refresh
ENDWITH

*** mena - 14/11/2017 - incluida a checagem de estoques invisiveis sem checar empresa ***
IF !fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP', 'ESTOQUEINV' )
	Select CrSigMvEst
	Go Top
	SCAN
		SELECT crEstInv
		LOCATE for iclis = CrSigMvEst.estos
		IF FOUND()
			SELECT CrSigMvEst
			DELETE
		ELSE
			SELECT CrSigMvEst
		ENDIF
	ENDSCAN
ENDIF
********************************************************

Return(.T.)
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.Grd_Estoque
* Classe: grid (Base: grid)
* Registro: 278
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
LParameters nColIndex
Local lcChave, lcEmps, lcGrupos, lcEstos, lcCodCors, lcCodTams

lcEmps    = CrSigMvEst.emps
lcGrupos  = CrSigMvEst.grupos
lcEstos   = CrSigMvEst.estos
lcCodCors = CrSigMvEst.codcors
lcCodTams = CrSigMvEst.codtams

lcChave = IIf(!IsEmpty(lcEmps),[a.emps = ']+lcEmps+[' ],[a.emps = ' ' ])
lcChave = lcChave + IIf(!IsEmpty(lcGrupos),[And a.grupos = ']+lcGrupos+[' ],[And a.grupos = ' ' ])
lcChave = lcChave + IIf(!IsEmpty(lcEstos),[And a.estos = ']+lcEstos+[' ],[And a.estos = ' ' ])

Do Case
	Case CrTSigPro.tipoestos = 2     && Código/Cor
		lcChave = lcChave + IIf(!IsEmpty(lcCodCors),[And a.codcors = ']+lcCodCors+[' ],[And Empty(a.codcors) ])
	Case CrTSigPro.tipoestos = 3     && Código/Tamanho
		lcChave = lcChave + IIf(!IsEmpty(lcCodTams),[And a.codtams = ']+lcCodTams+[' ],[And Empty(a.codtams) ])
	Case CrTSigPro.tipoestos = 4     && Código/Cor/Tamanho
		lcChave = lcChave + IIf(!IsEmpty(lcCodCors),[And a.codcors = ']+lcCodCors+[' ],[And Empty(a.codcors) ])
		lcChave = lcChave + IIf(!IsEmpty(lcCodTams),[And a.codtams = ']+lcCodTams+[' ],[And Empty(a.codtams) ])
EndCase

If Not Empty(lcEstos)
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdCli], [LocalCli], [IClis], lcEstos, [RClis])
	If Not Used([LocalCli])
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - LocalCli 1.a)])
		Return 0
	EndIf
	This.Parent.getDEstos.Value = LocalCli.RClis
	This.Parent.getDEstos.Refresh 
EndIf

If Used('CrSigMvHst')
	This.Parent.grd_historico.RecordSource = ''
	Use In CrSigMvHst
EndIf

If Used([Tmp_Hist])
	Select a.Emps,a.cpros,a.datas,a.numes,a.dopes,a.qtds,a.opers,a.sqtds,a.codbarras,a.cidchaves,a.pesos,a.spesos,a.Seqs,a.empos, a.Fators ;
	  From Tmp_Hist a ;
	 Where &lcChave. ; 
     Order By a.cidchaves ;
      Into Cursor CrSigMvHst readwrite 
	Index on Str(CodBarras)+Str(Seqs) Tag CodBarras
	Index On CidChaves Tag Datas
	Go Bottom

	With This.Parent.grd_historico
		.RecordSource = 'CrSigMvHst'
		.col_data.ControlSource     = 'CrSigMvHst.datas'
		.col_codigo.ControlSource   = 'CrSigMvHst.numes'
		.col_operacao.ControlSource = 'CrSigMvHst.dopes'
		.col_qtde.ControlSource     = 'CrSigMvHst.qtds'
		.Col_qtde.Header1.caption  	= 'Qtd ('+CrTSigPro.CUnis+')'
		.col_o.ControlSource        = 'CrSigMvHst.opers'
		.col_saldo.ControlSource    = 'CrSigMvHst.sqtds'
		.col_cdbarras.ControlSource = 'CrSigMvHst.codbarras'
		.col_fator.ControlSource 	= 'CrSigMvHst.Fators'
		
		*--Rafael - 02/08/2016 - Troca do Codigo de Barra Pelo Fator da Venda
		If crSigCdPac.FatorBarra = 1
			.col_cdbarras.Width = 0
			.col_cdbarras.ColumnOrder = 10
			
			.col_fator.Width = 100
			.col_fator.ColumnOrder = 7
		Else
			.col_cdbarras.Width = 80
			.col_cdbarras.ColumnOrder = 7
			
			.col_fator.Width = 0
			.col_fator.ColumnOrder = 10
		EndIf 
		
		If Not IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.cestos) And CrTSigPro.cEstos = '3'
			.Col_peso.controlsource = 'CrSigMvHst.Pesos'
			.col_speso.Controlsource= 'CrSigMvHst.sPesos'
			.Col_peso.Header1.caption='Qtd ( '+CrTSigPro.CUniPs+' )'
			.col_speso.Header1.Caption='Saldo'
		Else
			.Col_peso.controlsource = ''
			.col_speso.Controlsource= ''
			.Col_peso.Header1.caption=''
			.col_speso.Header1.Caption=''
		EndIf
	EndWith

	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.grd_Historico.Refresh
	ThisForm.AtualizaCpoEst
	Go Top In CrSigMvHst
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.grd_Historico.Refresh
	Go Bottom In CrSigMvHst
EndIf
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.grd_Historico.Refresh
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE btnOperacao.Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE btnOperacao.Valid
If Empty(crSigMvHst.Empos) Or Empty(crSigMvHst.Dopes) Or Empty(crSigMvHst.Numes)
	=MessageBox([Selecione Um Registro Na Grade de Histórico!!!], 0+48, [Atenção!!!])
	ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.grd_historico.Col_data.SetFocus 
Else
	lcSql = [Select Dopes From SigCdOpe Where Dopes = ']+CrSigMvHst.Dopes+[']
	If thisform.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcsql,'TmpOpe') > 0 And Reccount('TmpOpe') > 0
		Do Form SigMvExp With crSigMvHst.Dopes, 'C', crSigMvHst.Numes, crSigMvHst.Empos, .t.
	Else
		lcSql = [Select Dopps From SigCdOpd Where Dopps = ']+CrSigMvHst.Dopes+[']
		If Thisform.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcsql,'TmpOpp') > 0 And Reccount('TmpOpp') > 0
			Do Form SigMvPdt With crSigMvHst.Dopes, 'C', crSigMvHst.Numes, crSigMvHst.Empos, .t.
		EndIf
	Endif
EndIf

ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE btnPendentes.Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE btnPendentes.Valid
If (Not Used([crSigMvEstPd]) Or (Reccount([crSigMvEstPd]) < 0) Or Empty(crSigMvEst.Fk_Chave) Or ;
	(Empty(crSigMvEst.Pedidos) And Empty(crSigMvEst.EntPends)))
	=MessageBox([Não Existe Nenhum Produto Pendente No estoque Selecionado!!!], 16, [Operação Não Permitida])
	ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Estoque.grd_Estoque.Column1.SetFocus
Else
	Do Form SigPdPns With crSigMvEst.Fk_Chave
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.grd_produtos
* Classe: fwgrade (Base: grid)
* Registro: 342
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
LParameters nColIndex
Local sys2

Private pPro

lcArquivo2 = Sys(2023) + [\] + goSistema.Sys2015Tmp + Sys(2015) + [.jpg]

If ThisForm.llUser
	This.Parent.Command1.Visible = .F.
	This.Parent.Command1.Refresh
Else
	This.Parent.Command1.Visible = .T.
	This.Parent.Command1.Refresh
EndIf	

Select CrProdutos
lcCodPro  = CrProdutos.cpros
			
lcLstPre = ThisForm.Fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.Get_Lista.Value 

m.SelectPro   = [Select a.cpros,a.cgrus,a.dpros,a.sgrus,a.cunis,a.cunips,a.ifors,a.reffs,a.qmins,a.valors,a.moedas,a.icms,]+;
				[a.custofs,a.moecusfs,a.margems,a.markupa,a.moevs,a.dtincs,a.datas,a.pesoms,a.fcustos,a.moepcs,]+;
				[a.fvendas,a.moepvs,a.obspeds,a.obspes,a.DPro2s,a.linhas,a.situas,a.colecoes,a.cftios,a.pftios,a.mftios,]+;
				[a.pcuss,a.moecs,a.pvideals,a.CodCors,c.rclis,g.dgrus,g.arreds,g.tipoestos,]+;
				[g.Caracteris,u.dunis,u.cEstos, b.descs As desccol,l.descs As desclin,f.descs As descftio,]+;
				[a.Volumes, a.FigTecs,a.cproeqs,a.obsetqs,a.UltComps,a.vultcomps,a.multcomps,a.cClass,a.UsuIncs, a.categoria, j.descs as descCtg, ]+;
				[a.Locals,a.Mercs, a.cbars, h.descs as descgru,a.volumes,a.pesoms,] + ;
				[a.pesobs,a.tamps,a.tamhs,a.cpros,a.ltminsv,]+;
				[a.DPro3s,a.tamls,a.CodFinP,a.ProdWebs,a.Conjunts,a.CodMacro,a.Obscompras,a.foralinha,case when a.prodoff = 1 then 'S' else 'N' end as offs ] + ;
				Iif(Not Empty(lcLstPre),[,Case when isnull(i.pvens,0) = 0 then a.pvens else i.pvens end as pvens],[, a.pvens]) + ;
				Iif(Not Empty(lcLstPre),[,Case when isnull(i.PrecoDe,0) = 0 then a.PrecoDe else i.PrecoDe end as PrecoDe],[, a.PrecoDe]) + ;
				Iif(ThisForm.llUser,[,a.FigJpgs ],[ ]) + ;
				[From SigCdPro a ]+;
				[Left Join SigCdGrp g On a.cgrus = g.cgrus ]+;
				[Left Join SigCdUni u On a.cunis = u.cunis ]+;
				[Left Join SigCdCol b On a.colecoes = b.colecoes ]+;
				[Left Join SigCdLin l On a.linhas = l.linhas ]+;
				[Left Join SigCdCtg j On a.Categoria = j.Cods ]+;
				[Left Join SigPrFti f On a.cftios = f.cods ]+;
				[Left Join SigCdCli c On a.ifors = c.iclis ]+;
				[Left Join SigCdGpr h On a.Mercs= h.codigos ]+;
				Iif(Not Empty(lcLstPre),[Left Join SigCdLpi i On a.cpros = i.cpros and i.lprecos = ']+lcLstPre+[' ],[]) + ;
				[Where a.cpros = ']+lcCodPro+[' ]

If Used('CrTSigPro')
	Use In CrTSigPro
EndIf
lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(m.SelectPro,'CrTSigPro')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

Select CrTSigPro
Go Top
lcCodGru  = CrTSigPro.cgrus
lcCodSgru = CrTSigPro.sgrus
lcCodCor  = CrTSigPro.CodCors

If Used('Tmp_Sgru')
	Use In Tmp_Sgru
EndIf

lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select s.codigos,s.descricaos ]+;
				  [From SigCdPsg s ]+;
				 [Where s.cgrus = ']+lcCodGru+[' And s.codigos = ']+lcCodSgru+[' ],'Tmp_Sgru')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

If Used('Tmp_Cor')
	Use In Tmp_Cor
EndIf
lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select descs ]+;
				  [From SigCdCor ]+;
				 [Where Cods = ']+lcCodCor+[' ],'Tmp_Cor')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

With Thisform.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao
	.btnCaracters.Enabled 		  = (Nvl(crTSigPro.Caracteris, 0) = 1)
	.Get_cd_fornecedor.Value      = Nvl(CrTSigPro.ifors,'')
	.Get_ref_fornecedor.Value     = Nvl(CrTSigPro.reffs,'')
	.Get_cd_grupo.Value           = Nvl(CrTSigPro.cgrus,'')
	.Get_cd_sgrupo.Value          = Nvl(CrTSigPro.sgrus,'')
	.Get_cd_unidade.Value         = Nvl(CrTSigPro.cunis,'')
	.cntDados1.Get_fator_venda.Value        = Nvl(CrTSigPro.fvendas,0)
	.cntDados1.Get_fator_venda_moeda.Value  = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moepvs,'')
	.Get_peso_medio.Value         = Nvl(CrTSigPro.pesoms,0)
	.Get_GrVenda.Value            = Nvl(CrTSigPro.colecoes,'')
	.Get_ds_fornecedor.Value      = Nvl(CrTSigPro.rclis,'')
	.Get_ds_grupo.Value           = Nvl(CrTSigPro.dgrus,'')
	.Get_ds_sgrupo.Value          = Nvl(Tmp_Sgru.descricaos,'')
	.Get_GrDVenda.Value           = Nvl(CrTSigPro.desccol,'')
	.Get_CdCategoria.Value        = Nvl(CrTSigPro.Categoria,'')
	.Get_DsCategoria.Value        = Nvl(CrTSigPro.descCtg,'')
	.getDPro2s.Value			  = Nvl(CrTSigPro.DPro2s,'')
	.Get_Observacao1.Value        = Nvl(CrTSigPro.obspes,'')
	.Get_Observacao2.Value        = Nvl(CrTSigPro.obspeds,'')
	.GetCor.Value                 = Nvl(CrTSigPro.CodCors,'')
	.Get_dCor.Value               = Nvl(Tmp_Cor.Descs,'')
	.Get_class.Value              = Nvl(CrTSigPro.cClass,'')
	.getLocals.Value			  = Nvl(CrTSigPro.Locals,'')
	.cntDados1.Get_datainclusao.Value       = Nvl(CrTSigPro.dtincs,CtoD(Space(08)))
	.GetEquivalente.Value      	  = Nvl(CrTSigPro.cproeqs,'')
	.cntDados1.Get_qtd_minima.Value         = Nvl(CrTSigPro.qmins,0)
	.get_cd_ggrupo.value		  = Nvl(CrTSigPro.Mercs ,'')
	.get_ds_ggrupo.value		  = Nvl(CrTSigPro.descgru,'')
	.Get_vBars.Value			  = Nvl(CrTSigPro.cbars ,'')
	.getCodFinP.Value			  = Nvl(crTSigPro.CodFinP ,'')
	.getConjunto.Value			  = Nvl(crTSigPro.Conjunts,'')
	.getCodMacro.Value			  = Nvl(crTSigPro.CodMacro,'')
	.Get_cd_fornecedor.Refresh
	.Get_ref_fornecedor.Refresh
	.Get_cd_grupo.Refresh
	.Get_cd_sgrupo.Refresh
	.Get_cd_unidade.Refresh
	.cntDados1.Get_fator_venda.Refresh
	.cntDados1.Get_fator_venda_moeda.Refresh
	.Get_peso_medio.Refresh
	.Get_GrVenda.Refresh
	.Get_ds_fornecedor.Refresh
	.Get_ds_grupo.Refresh
	.Get_ds_sgrupo.Refresh
	.Get_GrDVenda.Refresh
	.Get_CdCategoria.Refresh
	.Get_DsCategoria.Refresh
	.getDPro2s.Refresh
	.Get_Observacao1.Refresh
	.Get_Observacao2.Refresh
	.GetCor.Refresh
	.Get_dcor.refresh
	.Get_Class.Refresh
	.getLocals.Refresh
	.cntDados1.Get_datainclusao.Refresh
	.GetEquivalente.Refresh
	.cntDados1.Get_qtd_minima.Refresh
	.get_cd_ggrupo.Refresh
	.get_ds_ggrupo.Refresh
	.Get_vBars.Refresh
	.getConjunto.Refresh
	.getCodMacro.Refresh	
	
	If ThisForm.llUser
		If Not IsNull(CrTSigPro.FigJpgs) And Not IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.FigJpgs)
			.FigJpg.Visible = .F.
			.FigJpg.Picture = ''
			Clear Resources
			lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(CrTSigPro.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)
			=StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArquivo2)
			.FigJpg.Picture = (lcArquivo2)
			.FigJpg.Visible = .T.
		Else
			.FigJpg.Visible = .F.
			.FigJpg.Picture = ''
		EndIf
	Else
		.FigJpg.Visible = .F.
		.FigJpg.Picture = ''
	EndIf
		
	Select CrTSigPro
	If IsEmpty(CrSigCdPam.moedetqs)
		.Get_pr_venda.Value          = Nvl(CrTSigPro.pvens,0)
		.Get_precode.Value           = Nvl(CrTSigPro.PrecoDe,0)
		.Get_pr_venda_moeda.Value    = Nvl(CrTSigPro.moevs,'')
		.GetPerDesc.Value    		 = Iif(Not IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.PrecoDe) And Not IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.pvens),((CrTSigPro.pvens / CrTSigPro.PrecoDe) * 100)-100,0)
	Else
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrSigCdPam.moedetqs) 
		If Found()
			cotacao = CrSigCdCot.valos
		Else
			cotacao = 1
		EndIf
		Select CrSigCdCot
		Go Top
		Locate For (CrSigCdCot.cmoes = CrTSigPro.moevs)
		If Found()
			cotven = CrSigCdCot.valos
		Else
			cotven = 1
		EndIf
		ValVenda = Round(CrTSigPro.pvens * cotven / cotacao,2)
		.Get_pr_venda.Value          = ValVenda
		.Get_precode.Value           = Nvl(CrTSigPro.PrecoDe,0)
		.Get_pr_venda_moeda.Value    = CrSigCdPam.moedetqs
		.GetPerDesc.Value    		 = Iif(Not IsEmpty(CrTSigPro.PrecoDe) And Not IsEmpty(ValVenda),((ValVenda / CrTSigPro.PrecoDe) * 100)-100,0)
	EndIf
	If CrTSigPro.arreds # 0
		_FatArred = CrTSigPro.arreds
		_Soma = _FatArred
		Do While _Soma < .Get_pr_venda.Value
			_Soma = _Soma + _FatArred
		EndDo
		.Get_pr_venda.Value = _Soma
	EndIf

	llDtFim = Dtot(Date())
	lcQuery = [Select a.cpros ] + ;
				[From SigCdLpi a, SigCdLpc b ] + ;
			   [Where a.Cpros = '] + CrTSigPro.cpros + [' And ] + ;
			   		[(a.VencFs Is Null Or a.Vencfs >= ?llDtFim) And ] + ;
			   		[a.LPrecos = b.LPrecos And ] + ;
			   		[b.Emps In ('   ', '] + _Empr + [')]

	If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'Tmp_lPrei') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf
	*.Consulte.Visible = ( Reccount('Tmp_lPrei') > 0 )
	*.Refresh
	
	If ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.btnMinimo.Visible 
		* Checando se o Produto Possui Estoque Mínimo
		lcQuery = [Select a.CPros ] + ;
					[From SigCdMin a ] + ;
				   [Where a.CPros = '] + crTSigPro.CPros + [']

		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalBusca]) < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (grd_Produtos - AfterRowColChange - LocalBusca)')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Go Top In LocalBusca
		ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.btnMinimo.Enabled = Not Eof([LocalBusca])
	EndIf
	**************************************************
EndWith
This.Parent.cnt_preco.Visible = .F.
This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Grp_Operacao.Cmd_Voltar.Cancel = .T.
ThisForm.Caption = 'Consulta do Produto: ' + AllTrim(CrTSigPro.CPros) + ' / '  + AllTrim(CrTSigPro.DPros)

This.parent.parent.Pag_CodPai.Enabled = .T.
This.parent.parent.Pag_CodPai.getConjunto.Value = Nvl(crTSigPro.Conjunts,'')
This.parent.parent.Pag_CodPai.getConjunto.Refresh 

If Empty(Nvl(crTSigPro.Conjunts,''))
	This.parent.parent.Pag_CodPai.Enabled = .F.
EndIf 

With ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao
	.getDesFinP.Value = []
	If Not Empty(crTSigPro.CodFinP)
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdFip], [LocalBus], [Cods], crTSigPro.CodFinP, [Descs])
		If Not Used([LocalBus])
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - LocalBus 2)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		.getDesFinP.Value = LocalBus.Descs
	EndIf
	.getCodFinP.Refresh
	.getDesFinP.Refresh
EndWith

This.Parent.getDPro3s.Refresh
This.Parent.getObsCompras.Refresh 
* Fim da Verificação Se o Grupo Possui Um Cadastro de Ficha Técnica
ENDPROC

		Do Form SigOpZom With lcArquivo, ;
							'Produto : ' + crProdutos.CPros + ' - ' + crProdutos.DPros, ' '
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

PROCEDURE Click
lcArquivo2 = Sys(2023) + [\] + goSistema.Sys2015Tmp + Sys(2015) + [.jpg]

lcCodPro = CrTSigPro.cpros

This.Visible = .F.
This.Refresh

If Used('Tmp_Img')
	Use In Tmp_Img
EndIf
lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select i.FigJpgs ]+;
				  [From SigCdPro i ]+;
				 [Where i.cpros = ']+lcCodPro+[' ],'Tmp_Img')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf

Select Tmp_Img
Go Top
If !Eof() And !IsNull(Tmp_Img.FigJpgs) And !IsEmpty(Tmp_Img.FigJpgs)
	This.Parent.figjpg.Visible                  = .F.
	This.Parent.figjpg.Picture                  = ''
	Clear Resources
	lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(Tmp_Img.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)
	=StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArquivo2)
	This.Parent.figjpg.Picture = (lcArquivo2)
	This.Parent.figjpg.Visible = .T.
Else
	This.Parent.figjpg.Visible                  = .F.
	This.Parent.figjpg.Picture                  = ''
EndIf
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.Bnt_Preco
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 391
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
If (This.Parent.Cnt_Preco.Visible)
	This.Parent.Cnt_Preco.Visible = .F.
Else
	Select ('crSigCdPam')
	Locate 
	=ConsultaPrecos(CrTSigPro.CPros,crSigCdPam.MoePadVens,.F.,.F.,.F.,.F.,.F.,ThisForm.poDataMgr1)
	
	Select LpItem
	Go Top
	Scan
		Select CsSigOpTdz
		If Seek(LpItem.TabDs,'CsSigOpTdz','Codigos')
			If CsSigOpTdz.ChkAcTb = 1 And CsSigOpTdz.Descos <> 0
				If !ThisForm.Chklimite(0,0,0,CsSigOpTdz.Descos * -1)
					Delete In LpItem
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndScan

	Select LpItem
	Go Top
	With This.Parent
		.Cnt_Preco.Visible = .T.
		.Cnt_Preco.ZOrder(0)
		.Parent.Parent.Grp_Operacao.Cmd_Voltar.Cancel = .F.
		.Cnt_Preco.Grd_Preco.Refresh
		.Cnt_Preco.Grd_Preco.Column1.Text1.SetFocus
	EndWith
EndIf


ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE Click

PROCEDURE Click
Local lcBrJpg

lcBrJpg = Addbs(JustPath(ThisForm.poDataMgr1.GetDatabase())) + 'BrJpg.JPG'
If !File(lcBrJpg)
	MessageBox('Está Faltando Jpg Branco ',0+48,'')
Else
	ThisForm.Enabled = .f. && desabilitar formulario....e na saida deste formulario no metodo release...habilita novamente o formulario anterior...
	Do Form SigOpPec With ThisForm 
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Selecao.btnExportaJpg
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 405
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local llRet, lcDir, lcPro, lcArq

llRet = (MessageBox([Confirma a Exportação de Imagens dos Produtos Selecionados?], 4+32+256, [Confimação de Processamento]) = 6)

If llRet
	lcAntPath = Sys(5) + Sys(2003)
	If Directory(goSistema.DirImagens)
		Set Default To &goSistema.DirImagens.
	EndIf

	lcDir = GetDir([C:\], [Informe o Diretório Onde As Imagens Serão Gravadas], [Selecção de Diretório])

	Select crProdutos
	Scan
		lcPro = crProdutos.CPros
		
		lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select FigJpgs From SigCdPro Where CPros = '] + lcPro + ['], [LocalImg])
		If (lnQueryOk < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		Select LocalImg
		Go Top In LocalImg
		If Not Eof([LocalImg]) And Not Empty(LocalImg.FigJpgs) And Not IsNull(LocalImg.FigJpgs)
			lcArq = Alltrim(lcDir) + Strtran(Alltrim(lcPro), [ ], [_]) + [.Jpg]
			lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(LocalImg.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)
			=StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArq)
		EndIf
		Use In LocalImg
	EndScan
EndIf

=MessageBox([Processo Finalizado!!!], 0+64, [Imagens ] + Iif(llRet, [], [Não ]) + [Exportadas])
* Fim *********************************************************************************

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
LParameters nColIndex
Local lcArquivo2

lcArquivo2 = Sys(2023) + [\] + goSistema.Sys2015Tmp + Sys(2015) + [.jpg]

If Not IsEmpty(CsFase.FigProcs)
	Clear Resources
	Copy Memo CsFase.FigProcs To (lcArquivo2)
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.FigJpg.Picture   = (lcArquivo2)
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.FigJpg.Visible   = .T.
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Bot_Zoom.Visible = .T.
Else
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Bot_Zoom.Visible = .F.
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.FigJpg.Picture   = []
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.FigJpg.Visible   = .F.
EndIf
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Get_Obs.Refresh
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.FigJpg.ZOrder
This.Refresh()
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Get_Obs
* Classe: fwmemo (Base: editbox)
* Registro: 448
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
**
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If !This.ReadOnly
	lc_Obs = This.Value
	lcUpdate = [Update SigCdPrf Set Obs = ?lc_Obs Where Produtos = ']+csFase.Produtos+[' And Ordems = ]+Str(CsFase.Ordems,2)
	If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Else
		lcDelete = [Delete from SigPrPrt Where Cpros = ']+csFase.Produtos+[' ]
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr1.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .F.
		EndIf 
		
*!*			lcDelete = [Delete from SigPrWeb Where Cpros = ']+csFase.Produtos+[' ]
*!*			If ThisForm.Podatamgr1.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
*!*				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
*!*				Return .F.
*!*			EndIf 	

		ThisForm.poDataMgr1.Commit()

	EndIf
EndIf
This.ReadOnly = .T.
ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.GradFase.SetFocus
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Zoom.Click

PROCEDURE Zoom.Click
LParameters nColIndex
Local lcArquivo9
lcArquivo9 = Sys(2023) + [\] + goSistema.Sys2015Tmp + Sys(2015) + [.jpg]
If Not IsEmpty(CsFase.FigProcs)
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Cnt_Zoom.Visible = .T.
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Cnt_Zoom.ZOrder
	Copy Memo CsFase.FigProcs to (lcArquivo9)
	Clear Resources
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Cnt_Zoom.FigJpg.Picture = (lcArquivo9)
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Cnt_Zoom.FigJpg.Visible = .T.
Else
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Cnt_Zoom.FigJpg.Picture = ""
    ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.Cnt_Zoom.FigJpg.Visible = .F.
EndIf
This.Refresh()
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.cmdgFigura
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 470
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lnConfirma,lcFigura,lcDiretorio,lcArquivo,lcFigJpg,llJpg,lcArquivo1

Select csFase
If Eof() Or Bof()
	Return 0
EndIf

ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.GradFase.SetFocus()

If this.Enabled
	lcAntPath = Sys(5) + Sys(2003)
	If Directory(goSistema.DirImagens)
		Set Default To &goSistema.DirImagens.
	EndIf
	
	lcFigura = Alltrim(GetPict('Formato Jpeg:JPG;Formato Bitmap:BMP', 'Nome do Arquivo:', 'Selecionar'))

	Set Default To &lcAntPath.

	If Not Empty(lcFigura) And File(lcFigura)
		If Not fTamanhoImagem(lcFigura, ThisForm.poDataMgr, .t.)
			Return 0
		EndIf

		If (MessageBox('Confirma a Transferência da Imagem ?', 4+32+256, '') <> 6)
			Return .t.
		EndIf

		Wait Window 'Aguarde!!! Transferindo a Imagem...' NoWait

		Replace FigProcs With FileToStr(lcFigura) IN csFase
				
		Wait Clear

		lcArquivo = Sys(2023) + [\] + goSistema.Sys2015Tmp + Sys(2015) + [.jpg]

		With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase
			Clear Resources
			.FigJpg.Picture = ''
			.FigJpg.Visible = .f.

			* Figura no Campo Memo
			If Not Empty(csFase.FigProcs) And Not IsNull(csFase.FigProcs)
				If (StrToFile(csFase.FigProcs, lcArquivo) > 0)
					.FigJpg.Visible = .t.
					.FigJpg.Picture = (lcArquivo)
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndWith

		thisform.podatamgr1.Requery('CrSigCdPrf')

		lcQuery = [Select * from SigCdPrf Where Produtos = ']+csFase.Produtos+[' And Ordems = ]+Str(CsFase.Ordems,2)
		If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'TmpCdPrf') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão - TmpCdPrf')
		Endif
		Select TmpCdPrf
		Scatter memo memvar
		m.FigProcs = csFase.FigProcs
		Insert into CrSigCdPrf from memvar
		
		If Not ThisForm.poDataMgr1.Update('crSigCdPrf')
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		Else
			lcDelete = [Delete from SigPrPrt Where Cpros = ']+csFase.Produtos+[' ]
			If ThisForm.Podatamgr1.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
			
			*-- Produto Web - Ecommerce
*!*				lcDelete = [Delete from SigPrWeb Where Cpros = ']+csFase.Produtos+[' ]
*!*				If ThisForm.Podatamgr1.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'') < 1
*!*					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
*!*					Return .f.
*!*				EndIf
			
			ThisForm.poDataMgr1.Commit()
		EndIf

		If (MessageBox('Deseja Excluir a Imagem Original ?', 4+32+256, '') = 6)
			Delete File (lcFigura)
		EndIf
	EndIf

	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Fase.GradFase.SetFocus()
EndIf
* Fim *********************************************************************************

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta.getCor
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 515
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid

Local loBrowse, lcCodCor, loLista

lcCodCor = This.Value

lnQueryOk = ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select a.cods,a.descs ]+;
	   [From SigCdCor a ]+;
	  [Where a.cods = ']+lcCodCor+[' ],'CrSigCdCor')
If lnQueryOk < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.T.)
EndIf
If !IsEmpty(This.Value)
	Select CrSigCdCor
	If !Eof()
		This.Value = Evaluate('CrSigCdCor.cods')
		This.Refresh
		Return(.T.)
	EndIf
	m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',ThisForm.poDataMgr1.pnIdConn,'SigCdCor','CrCores','cods',This.Value,'Cor',.T.)
	If !(m.loLista.plAchouRegistro)
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Cods','XXXX','Cor')
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Descs','','Descrição')
		m.loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	m.loLista = NULL
	If LastKey() <> 27 And Used('CrCores')
		This.Value = Evaluate('CrCores.cods')
	Else
		This.Value = ''
	EndIf
	This.Refresh
EndIf
lGradeOk = ThisForm.AcertaGradeEti()
If !lGradeOk
	Return(.T.)
EndIf
With This.Parent
	.ZOrder
	.GetTam.ZOrder
EndWith	
Return(.T.)
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta.Grd_Estoque
* Classe: grid (Base: grid)
* Registro: 525
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
LParameters nColIndex

lcQuery = [Select Locals ] + ;
		    [From SigCdMin ] + ;
		   [Where CPros = '] 	+ Padr(crTSigPro.CPros,     14) + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [Emps = '] 	+ Padr(crSigMvEtr.Emps,    03) + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [CodCores = '] + Padr(crSigMvEtr.CodCors, 04) + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [CodTams = '] 	+ Padr(crSigMvEtr.CodTams, 04) + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [Grupos = '] 	+ Padr(crSigMvEtr.Grupos,  10) + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [Contas = '] 	+ Padr(crSigMvEtr.Estos,   10) + [']

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalPrEmp]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalPrEmp)])
	Return .f.
EndIf
Select LocalPrEmp
Go Top In LocalPrEmp
ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.pag_Etiqueta.getLocal.Value = Iif(Eof([LocalPrEmp]), [], LocalPrEmp.Locals)
ThisForm.fld_principal.pag_Resultado.Result.pag_Etiqueta.getLocal.Refresh 

lGradeOk = ThisForm.AcertaGradeEti()
If !lGradeOk
	Return(.F.)
EndIf
ThisForm.Refresh
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_Etiqueta.Fwcheckbox1
* Classe: fwcheckbox (Base: checkbox)
* Registro: 544
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
If Empty(crProdutos.CPros) Or Empty(crSigMvEtr.Emps) Or Empty(crSigMvEtr.Grupos) Or Empty(crSigMvEtr.Estos)
	=MessageBox('Nenhum estoque foi selecionado na grade.', 64, 'Aviso')
	ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.pag_Etiqueta.grd_Estoque.Column1.SetFocus()
Else
	If Empty(crSigMvEtr.QtdEmpHs)
		=MessageBox('O estoque selecionado não possui empenho.',64, 'Aviso')
		ThisForm.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.pag_Etiqueta.grd_Estoque.Column1.SetFocus()
	Else
		
		Select crEmpenhado
		Select distinct * from crEmpenhado Into Cursor csEmpenho ReadWrite
		Select csEmpenho
		Locate For .F.
		If Reccount([csEmpenho])>0
			Zap In crEmpenhado
			Select * from csEmpenho Into Cursor crEmpenhado ReadWrite
		EndIf 
	
		Do Form SigOpEmp With thisform, Thisform.datasessionid
	EndIf
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_CodPai.Optiongroup1
* Classe: optiongroup (Base: optiongroup)
* Registro: 637
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Used ('CrMvEst')
	Select CrMvEst
EndIf
lGrdOk = ThisForm.mgrdcodpai()
lGrdOk = IIf(Type('lGrdOk')='L',lGrdOk,.T.)
If !lGrdOk Or !Used('CrMvEst') Or !Used('CrOpEtq1')
	Return(.F.)
EndIf
Go Top
With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_CodPai.Grd_Estoque
	.AfterRowColChange
	.Refresh
	.SetFocus
EndWith

*** mena - 14/11/2017 - incluida a checagem de estoques invisiveis sem checar empresa ***
IF !fChecaAcesso('SIGOPCGP', 'ESTOQUEINV' )
	Select CrMvEst
	Go Top
	Scan
		Select crEstInv
		Locate For iclis = CrMvEst.estos
		If Found()
			Select CrMvEst
			Delete
		Else
			Select CrMvEst
		Endif
	Endscan
	Endif
	********************************************************

Return(.T.)
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_CodPai.getCor
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 644
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loBrowse, lcCodCor, loLista
lcCodCor = This.Value
If ThisForm.poDataMgr1.SqlExecute([Select a.cods,a.descs From SigCdCor a Where a.cods = ']+lcCodCor+[' ],'CrSigCdCor') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return(.T.)
EndIf
If !IsEmpty(This.Value)
	Select CrSigCdCor
	If !Eof()
		This.Value = Evaluate('CrSigCdCor.cods')
		This.Refresh
		Return(.T.)
	EndIf
	m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',ThisForm.poDataMgr1.pnIdConn,'SigCdCor','CrCores','cods',This.Value,'Cor',.T.)
	If !(m.loLista.plAchouRegistro)
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Cods','XXXX','Cor')
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Descs','','Descrição')
		m.loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	m.loLista = NULL
	If LastKey() <> 27 And Used('CrCores')
		This.Value = Evaluate('CrCores.cods')
	Else
		This.Value = ''
	EndIf
	This.Refresh
EndIf
lGradeOk = ThisForm.AcertaGradeEtiCodPai()
If !lGradeOk
	Return(.T.)
EndIf
With This.Parent
	.ZOrder
	.GetTam.ZOrder
EndWith	
Return(.T.)
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.Result.Pag_CodPai.getTam
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 645

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid

PROCEDURE Valid

Local loLista

If !Empty(This.Value)
	m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',ThisForm.poDataMgr1.pnIdConn,'SigCdTam','CrTamanhos','Cods',This.Value,'Tam',.T.)
	If !(m.loLista.plAchouRegistro)
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Cods','XXXX','Tam')
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Descs','','Descrição')
		m.loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	m.loLista = NULL
	If LastKey() <> 27 And Used('CrTamanhos')
		This.Value = Evaluate('CrTamanhos.Cods')
	Else
		This.Value = ''
	EndIf
	This.Refresh
EndIf
lGradeOk = ThisForm.AcertaGradeEtiCodPai()
If !lGradeOk
	Return(.T.)
EndIf
Return(.T.)
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE Valid

		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Descricaos','','Descrição')
		m.loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	m.loLista = NULL
	If LastKey() <> 27 And Used('CrLocaliza')
		This.Value = Evaluate('CrLocaliza.codigos')
	Else
		This.Value = ''
	EndIf
	This.Refresh
EndIf
lGradeOk = ThisForm.AcertaGradeEtiCodPai()
If !lGradeOk
	Return(.T.)
EndIf
Return(.T.)
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPCGP.fld_principal.Pag_Resultado.grp_operacao
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 656
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmd_voltar.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmd_voltar.Click
If Not Empty(ThisForm.ChamadaExt)
	This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Pag_Consulta.grp_operacao.cmd_cancela.Click 
Else 
	With ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro
		With .Principal
			.Get_Barra.Value           = 0
			.Get_Produto_Inicial.Refresh
			.Get_Produto_Final.Refresh
			.Get_Barra.Refresh
			.Get_Produto_Inicial.SetFocus
		EndWith
	EndWith

	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.Pag_Consulta.Enabled = .t.

	ThisForm.Caption = ThisForm.cPrompt
	ThisForm.Fld_Principal.ActivePage = 1
	ThisForm.Fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.commandgroup1.command1.Click()
EndIf
ENDPROC
